Perfect! Now I have all the information needed. Let me compile a comprehensive report on the wingman feature.

## Wingman Feature - Complete End-to-End Analysis

I've thoroughly explored the wingman feature across the CLI codebase. Here's the complete picture of how it works:

### 1. How Wingman Review is Triggered

**Two Entry Points:**

#### A. **Auto-Commit Strategy** (via Stop Hook)
Located in `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` lines 395-408:
- When the agent finishes a turn and saves changes via the `auto-commit` strategy
- The `commitWithMetadata()` hook handler calls `triggerWingmanReview()` directly after `SaveChanges()`
- This happens in the Stop hook which is fired by Claude Code when the agent finishes working

#### B. **Manual-Commit Strategy** (via Post-Commit Hook)  
Located in `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/hooks_git_cmd.go` lines 152-173:
- When the user manually commits code (not the agent)
- The git `post-commit` hook triggers `triggerWingmanFromCommit()`
- This function extracts file changes from the HEAD commit using `git diff-tree`

**Preconditions Check** (`wingman.go` lines 183-266):
- Wingman must be enabled in settings (`settings.IsWingmanEnabled()`)
- Environment variable check: `ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY` must be empty (prevents recursion during auto-apply)
- No pending REVIEW.md file exists (won't create duplicate reviews)
- Lock file (``.entire/wingman.lock`) is acquired atomically to prevent concurrent reviews
- Deduplication check: Computes SHA256 hash of sorted file paths and compares to previous review

### 2. How the Diff is Generated for Review

Located in `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go` lines 205-239 (`computeDiff` function):

**Branch-Level Diff Strategy** (recent change, commit b5c8d265):
1. Finds the merge base with the default branch (tries `main` first, then `master`)
2. If merge base exists, performs `git diff <merge-base> HEAD` for a holistic branch view
3. **Fallback 1**: If merge-base diff fails or is empty, tries `git diff HEAD` (uncommitted changes)
4. **Fallback 2**: If no uncommitted changes, tries `git diff HEAD~1 HEAD` (latest commit)

**Timeout**: 60 seconds (`wingmanGitTimeout`)

**Why branch-level?** Gives reviewers a complete view of all changes relative to the main branch, not just individual commits.

### 3. How REVIEW.md is Written

Located in `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go` lines 153-164:

1. **Claude CLI Call** (lines 269-318):
   - Subprocess spawns `claude --print --output-format json --model sonnet`
   - Runs from repo root so reviewer has file access
   - Granted read-only tools: `Read`, `Glob`, `Grep`
   - Timeout: 5 minutes
   - Stdin receives the review prompt (see section 5 below)

2. **Response Parsing**:
   - Claude CLI outputs JSON with format: `{"result": "<markdown review>", ...}`
   - Extracts the `result` field as the review text

3. **File Write** (lines 154-164):
   - Writes review to `.entire/REVIEW.md` (644 permissions)
   - Creates directory structure as needed
   - File stays in repo (user sees it) until deleted by the agent or user

### 4. How wingman-state.json is Managed

Located in `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go`:

**WingmanState Structure** (lines 38-44):
```go
type WingmanState struct {
    SessionID     string    `json:"session_id"`      // Session that was reviewed
    FilesHash     string    `json:"files_hash"`      // SHA256 of sorted file paths
    ReviewedAt    time.Time `json:"reviewed_at"`     // Timestamp of review
    ReviewApplied bool      `json:"review_applied"`  // Whether suggestions were applied
}
```

**Lifecycle:**

1. **Saved After Review Generated** (wingman_review.go lines 173-179):
   - When REVIEW.md is written, state file is immediately updated
   - Captures files hash and timestamp for deduplication
   - `ReviewApplied` defaults to `false`

2. **Deduplication** (wingman.go lines 216-230):
   - On next review trigger, previous state is loaded
   - If `FilesHash` matches current files AND `SessionID` matches, skip review
   - Prevents reviewing the same files twice within a session

3. **Location**: `.entire/wingman-state.json` (absolute path resolved via `paths.AbsPath()`)

4. **Load/Save Functions**:
   - `loadWingmanState()` - reads and parses JSON
   - `saveWingmanState()` - writes indented JSON (644 permissions)
   - `saveWingmanStateDirect()` - version used in detached subprocess (avoids `os.Chdir`)

### 5. Prompt-Submit Hook Injection - The Review Instruction

Located in `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` lines 79-91:

**When:**
- User submits a prompt (hook: `user-prompt-submit`)
- Called during `captureInitialState()` in the Stop hook handler
- Fires BEFORE the agent processes the prompt

**What Happens:**
1. Checks if wingman is enabled AND a REVIEW.md file exists
2. If both true, **injects the wingman instruction** into agent context
3. Uses `outputHookResponse()` to send JSON to Claude Code:
   ```json
   {
     "systemMessage": "<embedded markdown instruction>"
   }
   ```

**The Instruction** (embedded in `wingman_instruction.md`):
```markdown
A code review is available at `.entire/REVIEW.md`.

Read `.entire/REVIEW.md` and address each suggestion you agree with. 
Be critical. When done, delete `.entire/REVIEW.md`.
```

**Delivery Mechanism** (`outputHookResponse` in hooks.go lines 308-317):
- Sends JSON response to stdout
- Claude Code parses this and injects the message into the agent's system context
- Agent sees it as a system directive before processing the user's next prompt

**Recursion Prevention** (wingman.go line 187):
- Before reviewing, checks `ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY` environment variable
- During auto-apply (`triggerAutoApply` line 362), sets `ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY=1`
- This prevents the auto-apply session from triggering another review

### 6. Complete End-to-End Flow Diagram

```
User Action or Agent Turn Ends
    ↓
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ TRIGGER DECISION (wingman.go)                               │
│ - Check: Wingman enabled? ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY set?        │
│ - Check: Pending REVIEW.md? Can acquire lock?              │
│ - Check: Files hash matches previous review?                │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
    ↓ (if all checks pass)
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ SPAWN DETACHED SUBPROCESS (wingman_spawn_unix.go)           │
│ - Executable: same binary as parent                         │
│ - Command: `entire wingman __review <payload-path>`        │
│ - Detach: SysProcAttr with Setpgid=true (Unix)            │
│ - Stderr: Redirected to .entire/logs/wingman.log           │
│ - Payload: Written to .entire/wingman-payload.json          │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
    ↓ (in background process)
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ REVIEW SUBPROCESS (wingman_review.go - runWingmanReview)   │
│ 1. Wait 10 seconds (settle time)                           │
│ 2. Compute diff (branch-level vs merge base)               │
│ 3. Build review prompt with:                               │
│    - User prompts from session                             │
│    - Commit message                                        │
│    - Session context (context.md)                          │
│    - File list and full diff                               │
│ 4. Call claude --print --model sonnet                      │
│    - Strips GIT_* env vars (prevent hook loops)            │
│    - Grants Read/Glob/Grep tools                           │
│ 5. Write REVIEW.md                                         │
│ 6. Save wingman-state.json (FilesHash, timestamp)          │
│ 7. Wait 30 seconds                                         │
│ 8. Check if session is IDLE (not actively working)         │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
    ↓ (if session idle)
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ AUTO-APPLY (if idle)                                        │
│ 1. Trigger claude --continue                               │
│ 2. Pass wingman_instruction.md as context                  │
│ 3. Set ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY=1 (prevent recursion)         │
│ 4. Strip GIT_* env vars                                    │
│ 5. Agent reads REVIEW.md and applies suggestions           │
│ 6. Agent deletes REVIEW.md when done                       │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
    ↓ (back to main agent loop)
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ PROMPT-SUBMIT HOOK (captureInitialState)                   │
│ - Check: Does .entire/REVIEW.md exist?                    │
│ - If yes: Inject wingmanApplyInstruction into context      │
│ - Send via outputHookResponse() JSON to Claude Code        │
│ - Agent sees directive to read/apply review                │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### 7. Key Files and Their Responsibilities

| File | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| `/cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go` | Core trigger logic, state management, lock handling, deduplication |
| `/cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go` | Background review subprocess, diff generation, Claude CLI orchestration |
| `/cmd/entire/cli/wingman_prompt.go` | Review prompt construction with intent-aware instructions |
| `/cmd/entire/cli/wingman_instruction.md` | Embedded markdown directive for agent to apply suggestions |
| `/cmd/entire/cli/wingman_spawn_unix.go` | Unix-specific detached subprocess spawning with process group separation |
| `/cmd/entire/cli/wingman_spawn_other.go` | No-op for non-Unix platforms (Windows not supported) |
| `/cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` | Hook integration: injection on prompt-submit, triggering on stop |
| `/cmd/entire/cli/hooks_git_cmd.go` | Post-commit hook handler for manual-commit strategy |
| `/cmd/entire/cli/settings/settings.go` | Settings: `IsWingmanEnabled()` check |
| `/cmd/entire/cli/wingman_test.go` | Comprehensive test coverage |

### 8. Configuration & Enablement

Wingman is controlled via settings (`settings.json`):
```json
{
  "strategyOptions": {
    "wingman": {
      "enabled": true
    }
  }
}
```

**Commands:**
- `entire wingman enable` - Enable wingman auto-review
- `entire wingman disable` - Disable and clean up pending review
- `entire wingman status` - Show wingman status and last review info
- `entire wingman __review <payload>` - Internal: spawned by trigger (hidden command)

### 9. Timeouts and Delays

- **Initial Delay**: 10 seconds (let agent turn settle)
- **Diff Computation**: 60 seconds timeout
- **Claude Review**: 5 minutes timeout
- **Auto-Apply Wait**: 30 seconds (before checking if session is idle)
- **Auto-Apply Execution**: 15 minutes timeout
- **Stale Lock Threshold**: 30 minutes (assumes process crashed if lock older)

### 10. Safety Features

1. **Atomicity**: Lock file created with `O_CREATE|O_EXCL` (atomic on all platforms)
2. **Recursion Prevention**: `ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY` environment variable guard
3. **Environment Isolation**: `GIT_*` variables stripped to prevent hook interference
4. **Session State Validation**: Checks session phase (IDLE) before auto-apply
5. **Deduplication**: Prevents duplicate reviews of same files within session
6. **Stale Process Cleanup**: Removes lock file if older than 30 minutes

---

This comprehensive implementation provides automated, intelligent code review with background execution, deduplication, and safe auto-application of suggestions.

    34→	wingmanApplyDelay = 30 * time.Second
    35→
    36→	// wingmanReviewModel is the Claude model used for reviews.
    37→	wingmanReviewModel = "sonnet"
    38→
    39→	// wingmanGitTimeout is the timeout for git diff operations.
    40→	wingmanGitTimeout = 60 * time.Second
    41→
    42→	// wingmanReviewTimeout is the timeout for the claude --print review call.
    43→	wingmanReviewTimeout = 5 * time.Minute
    44→
    45→	// wingmanApplyTimeout is the timeout for the claude --continue auto-apply call.
    46→	wingmanApplyTimeout = 15 * time.Minute
    47→)
    48→
    49→// wingmanCLIResponse represents the JSON response from the Claude CLI --output-format json.
    50→type wingmanCLIResponse struct {
    51→	Result string `json:"result"`
    52→}
    53→
    54→func newWingmanReviewCmd() *cobra.Command {
    55→	return &cobra.Command{
    56→		Use:    "__review",
    57→		Hidden: true,
    58→		Args:   cobra.ExactArgs(1),
    59→		RunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
    60→			return runWingmanReview(args[0])
    61→		},
    62→	}
    63→}
    64→
    65→func runWingmanReview(payloadPath string) error {
    66→	wingmanLog("review process started (pid=%d)", os.Getpid())
    67→	wingmanLog("reading payload from %s", payloadPath)
    68→
    69→	// Read payload from file (avoids OS argv limits with large payloads)
    70→	payloadJSON, err := os.ReadFile(payloadPath) //nolint:gosec // path is from our own spawn
    71→	if err != nil {
    72→		wingmanLog("ERROR reading payload: %v", err)
    73→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read payload file: %w", err)
    74→	}
    75→	// Clean up payload file after reading
    76→	_ = os.Remove(payloadPath)
    77→
    78→	var payload WingmanPayload
    79→	if err := json.Unmarshal(payloadJSON, &payload); err != nil {
    80→		wingmanLog("ERROR unmarshaling payload: %v", err)
    81→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal payload: %w", err)
    82→	}
    83→
    84→	repoRoot := payload.RepoRoot
    85→	if repoRoot == "" {
    86→		wingmanLog("ERROR repo_root is empty in payload")
    87→		return errors.New("repo_root is required in payload")
    88→	}
    89→
    90→	totalFiles := len(payload.ModifiedFiles) + len(payload.NewFiles) + len(payload.DeletedFiles)
    91→	wingmanLog("session=%s repo=%s base_commit=%s files=%d",
    92→		payload.SessionID, repoRoot, payload.BaseCommit, totalFiles)
    93→
    94→	// Clean up lock file when review completes (regardless of success/failure)
    95→	lockPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanLockFile)
    96→	defer func() {
    97→		_ = os.Remove(lockPath)
    98→		wingmanLog("lock file removed")
    99→	}()
   100→
   101→	// Initial delay: let the agent turn fully settle
   102→	wingmanLog("waiting %s for agent turn to settle", wingmanInitialDelay)
   103→	time.Sleep(wingmanInitialDelay)
   104→
   105→	// Compute diff using the base commit captured at trigger time
   106→	wingmanLog("computing diff against %s", payload.BaseCommit)
   107→	diffStart := time.Now()
   108→	diff, err := computeDiff(repoRoot, payload.BaseCommit)
   109→	if err != nil {
   110→		wingmanLog("ERROR computing diff: %v", err)
   111→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to compute diff: %w", err)
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	if strings.TrimSpace(diff) == "" {
   115→		wingmanLog("no changes found in diff, exiting")
   116→		return nil // No changes to review
   117→	}
   118→	wingmanLog("diff computed: %d bytes in %s", len(diff), time.Since(diffStart).Round(time.Millisecond))
   119→
   120→	// Build file list for the prompt
   121→	allFiles := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))
   122→	for _, f := range payload.ModifiedFiles {
   123→		allFiles = append(allFiles, f+" (modified)")
   124→	}
   125→	for _, f := range payload.NewFiles {
   126→		allFiles = append(allFiles, f+" (new)")
   127→	}
   128→	for _, f := range payload.DeletedFiles {
   129→		allFiles = append(allFiles, f+" (deleted)")
   130→	}
   131→	fileList := strings.Join(allFiles, ", ")
   132→
   133→	// Read session context from checkpoint data (best-effort)
   134→	sessionContext := readSessionContext(repoRoot, payload.SessionID)
   135→	if sessionContext != "" {
   136→		wingmanLog("session context loaded: %d bytes", len(sessionContext))
   137→	}
   138→
   139→	// Build review prompt
   140→	prompt := buildReviewPrompt(payload.Prompts, payload.CommitMessage, sessionContext, payload.SessionID, fileList, diff)
   141→	wingmanLog("review prompt built: %d bytes", len(prompt))
   142→
   143→	// Call Claude CLI for review
   144→	wingmanLog("calling claude CLI (model=%s, timeout=%s)", wingmanReviewModel, wingmanReviewTimeout)
   145→	reviewStart := time.Now()
   146→	reviewText, err := callClaudeForReview(repoRoot, prompt)
   147→	if err != nil {
   148→		wingmanLog("ERROR claude CLI failed after %s: %v", time.Since(reviewStart).Round(time.Millisecond), err)
   149→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get review from Claude: %w", err)
   150→	}
   151→	wingmanLog("review received: %d bytes in %s", len(reviewText), time.Since(reviewStart).Round(time.Millisecond))
   152→
   153→	// Write REVIEW.md
   154→	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
   155→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(reviewPath), 0o750); err != nil {
   156→		wingmanLog("ERROR creating directory: %v", err)
   157→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create directory: %w", err)
   158→	}
   159→	//nolint:gosec // G306: review file is not secrets
   160→	if err := os.WriteFile(reviewPath, []byte(reviewText), 0o644); err != nil {
   161→		wingmanLog("ERROR writing REVIEW.md: %v", err)
   162→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write REVIEW.md: %w", err)
   163→	}
   164→	wingmanLog("REVIEW.md written to %s", reviewPath)
   165→
   166→	// Update dedup state — write directly to known path instead of using
   167→	// os.Chdir (which mutates process-wide state).
   168→	allFilePaths := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))
   169→	allFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.ModifiedFiles...)
   170→	allFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.NewFiles...)
   171→	allFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.DeletedFiles...)
   172→
   173→	saveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot, &WingmanState{
   174→		SessionID:     payload.SessionID,
   175→		FilesHash:     computeFilesHash(allFilePaths),
   176→		ReviewedAt:    time.Now(),
   177→		ReviewApplied: false,
   178→	})
   179→	wingmanLog("dedup state saved")
   180→
   181→	// Auto-apply phase: wait then check if session is idle
   182→	wingmanLog("waiting %s before auto-apply check", wingmanApplyDelay)
   183→	time.Sleep(wingmanApplyDelay)
   184→
   185→	idle := isSessionIdle(payload.SessionID)
   186→	wingmanLog("session idle check: idle=%v", idle)
   187→
   188→	if !idle {
   189→		wingmanLog("session is active, leaving REVIEW.md for notification")
   190→		return nil // User is active, leave REVIEW.md for notification
   191→	}
   192→
   193→	// Trigger auto-apply via claude --continue
   194→	wingmanLog("triggering auto-apply via claude --continue")
   195→	applyStart := time.Now()
   196→	if err := triggerAutoApply(repoRoot); err != nil {
   197→		wingmanLog("ERROR auto-apply failed after %s: %v", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond), err)
   198→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to trigger auto-apply: %w", err)
   199→	}
   200→	wingmanLog("auto-apply completed in %s", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond))
   201→
   202→	return nil
   203→}
   204→
   205→// computeDiff gets the full branch diff for review. It diffs the current HEAD
   206→// against the merge base with the default branch (main/master), giving the
   207→// reviewer a holistic view of all branch changes rather than just one commit.
   208→func computeDiff(repoRoot string, _ string) (string, error) {
   209→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), wingmanGitTimeout)
   210→	defer cancel()
   211→
   212→	// Find the merge base with the default branch for a holistic branch diff.
   213→	mergeBase := findMergeBase(ctx, repoRoot)
   214→	if mergeBase != "" {
   215→		wingmanLog("using merge-base %s for branch diff", mergeBase)
   216→		diff, err := gitDiff(ctx, repoRoot, mergeBase)
   217→		if err == nil && strings.TrimSpace(diff) != "" {
   218→			return diff, nil
   219→		}
   220→		// Fall through to HEAD diff if merge-base diff fails or is empty
   221→	}
   222→
   223→	// Fallback: diff uncommitted changes against HEAD
   224→	wingmanLog("falling back to HEAD diff")
   225→	diff, err := gitDiff(ctx, repoRoot, "HEAD")
   226→	if err != nil {
   227→		return "", fmt.Errorf("git diff failed: %w", err)
   228→	}
   229→
   230→	// If no uncommitted changes, try the latest commit itself
   231→	if strings.TrimSpace(diff) == "" {
   232→		diff, err = gitDiff(ctx, repoRoot, "HEAD~1", "HEAD")
   233→		if err != nil {
   234→			return "", nil //nolint:nilerr // no diff is not an error
   235→		}
   236→	}
   237→
   238→	return diff, nil
   239→}
   240→
   241→// findMergeBase returns the merge-base commit between HEAD and the default
   242→// branch (tries main, then master). Returns empty string if not found.
   243→func findMergeBase(ctx context.Context, repoRoot string) string {
   244→	for _, branch := range []string{"main", "master"} {
   245→		cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "merge-base", branch, "HEAD")
   246→		cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   247→		out, err := cmd.Output()
   248→		if err == nil {
   249→			return strings.TrimSpace(string(out))
   250→		}
   251→	}
   252→	return ""
   253→}
   254→
   255→// gitDiff runs git diff with the given args and returns stdout.
   256→func gitDiff(ctx context.Context, repoRoot string, args ...string) (string, error) {
   257→	fullArgs := append([]string{"diff"}, args...)
   258→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", fullArgs...) //nolint:gosec // args are from internal logic
   259→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   260→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   261→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   262→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   263→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   264→		return "", fmt.Errorf("git diff %v: %w (stderr: %s)", args, err, stderr.String())
   265→	}
   266→	return stdout.String(), nil
   267→}
   268→
   269→// callClaudeForReview calls the claude CLI to perform the review.
   270→// repoRoot is the repository root so the reviewer can access the full codebase.
   271→func callClaudeForReview(repoRoot, prompt string) (string, error) {
   272→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), wingmanReviewTimeout)
   273→	defer cancel()
   274→
   275→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "claude",
   276→		"--print",
   277→		"--output-format", "json",
   278→		"--model", wingmanReviewModel,
   279→		"--setting-sources", "",
   280→		// Grant read-only tool access so the reviewer can inspect source files
   281→		// beyond just the diff. Permission bypass is safe here since tools are
   282→		// restricted to read-only operations.
   283→		"--allowedTools", "Read,Glob,Grep",
   284→		"--permission-mode", "bypassPermissions",
   285→	)
   286→
   287→	// Run from repo root so the reviewer can read source files for context.
   288→	// Loop-breaking is handled by --setting-sources "" (disables hooks) and
   289→	// wingmanStripGitEnv (prevents git index pollution).
   290→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   291→	cmd.Env = wingmanStripGitEnv(os.Environ())
   292→
   293→	cmd.Stdin = strings.NewReader(prompt)
   294→
   295→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   296→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   297→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   298→
   299→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   300→		var execErr *exec.Error
   301→		if errors.As(err, &execErr) {
   302→			return "", fmt.Errorf("claude CLI not found: %w", err)
   303→		}
   304→		var exitErr *exec.ExitError
   305→		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
   306→			return "", fmt.Errorf("claude CLI failed (exit %d): %s", exitErr.ExitCode(), stderr.String())
   307→		}
   308→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to run claude CLI: %w", err)
   309→	}
   310→
   311→	// Parse the CLI response
   312→	var cliResponse wingmanCLIResponse
   313→	if err := json.Unmarshal(stdout.Bytes(), &cliResponse); err != nil {
   314→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to parse claude CLI response: %w", err)
   315→	}
   316→
   317→	return cliResponse.Result, nil
   318→}
   319→
   320→// readSessionContext reads the context.md file from the session's checkpoint
   321→// metadata directory. Returns empty string if unavailable.
   322→func readSessionContext(repoRoot, sessionID string) string {
   323→	if sessionID == "" {
   324→		return ""
   325→	}
   326→	contextPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".entire", "metadata", sessionID, "context.md")
   327→	data, err := os.ReadFile(contextPath) //nolint:gosec // path constructed from repoRoot + session ID
   328→	if err != nil {
   329→		return ""
   330→	}
   331→	return string(data)
   332→}
   333→
   334→// isSessionIdle checks if the given session is in the IDLE phase.
   335→func isSessionIdle(sessionID string) bool {
   336→	state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   337→	if err != nil || state == nil {
   338→		return false
   339→	}
   340→	return state.Phase == session.PhaseIdle
   341→}
   342→
   343→// triggerAutoApply spawns claude --continue to apply the review suggestions.
   344→func triggerAutoApply(repoRoot string) error {
   345→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), wingmanApplyTimeout)
   346→	defer cancel()
   347→
   348→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "claude",
   349→		"--continue",
   350→		"--print",
   351→		"--setting-sources", "",
   352→		// Auto-accept edits so the agent can modify files and delete REVIEW.md
   353→		// without requiring user consent (this runs in a background process).
   354→		"--permission-mode", "acceptEdits",
   355→		wingmanApplyInstruction,
   356→	)
   357→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   358→	// Strip GIT_* env vars to prevent hook interference, and set
   359→	// ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY=1 so git hooks (post-commit) know not to
   360→	// trigger another wingman review (prevents infinite recursion).
   361→	env := wingmanStripGitEnv(os.Environ())
   362→	env = append(env, "ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY=1")
   363→	cmd.Env = env
   364→
   365→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   366→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   367→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   368→
   369→	return cmd.Run() //nolint:wrapcheck // caller wraps
   370→}
   371→
   372→// wingmanStripGitEnv returns a copy of env with all GIT_* variables removed.
   373→// This prevents a subprocess from discovering or modifying the parent's git repo
   374→// when we want isolation (e.g., running claude --print for review).
   375→func wingmanStripGitEnv(env []string) []string {
   376→	filtered := make([]string, 0, len(env))
   377→	for _, e := range env {
   378→		if !strings.HasPrefix(e, "GIT_") {
   379→			filtered = append(filtered, e)
   380→		}
   381→	}
   382→	return filtered
   383→}
   384→
   385→// saveWingmanStateDirect writes the wingman state file directly to a known path
   386→// under repoRoot, avoiding os.Chdir (which mutates process-wide state).
   387→func saveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot string, state *WingmanState) {
   388→	statePath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanStateFile)
   389→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(statePath), 0o750); err != nil {
   390→		return
   391→	}
   392→	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(state, "", "  ")
   393→	if err != nil {
   394→		return
   395→	}
   396→	//nolint:gosec,errcheck // G306: state file is config, not secrets; best-effort write
   397→	_ = os.WriteFile(statePath, data, 0o644)
   398→}
   399→

    12→// reviewPromptTemplate is the prompt sent to Claude for code review.
    13→//
    14→// Security note: User content (diff, prompts, context) is wrapped in XML tags
    15→// to provide clear boundary markers, similar to the summarization prompt pattern.
    16→const reviewPromptTemplate = `You are a senior code reviewer performing an intent-aware review. Your job is not just to find bugs in the code — it is to evaluate whether the changes correctly and completely fulfill what the developer was trying to accomplish.
    17→
    18→## Session Context
    19→
    20→This review is part of an automated development workflow. The developer works with an AI coding agent that makes changes on their behalf. Below is the checkpoint data captured during the session, which tells you WHY these changes were made.
    21→
    22→### Developer's Prompts

    25→%s
    26→</prompts>

    34→%s
    35→</session-context>
    36→

    40→- ` + "`%s`" + ` — session transcript (JSONL format)
    41→- ` + "`%s`" + ` — user prompts collected during session

    47→

    49→%s
    50→</diff>
    51→

    53→
    54→Use the session context above to understand the developer's intent. Then review the code changes with that intent in mind.
    55→
    56→**Intent alignment** (most important):
    57→- Do the changes actually accomplish what the developer asked for?
    58→- Are there any prompts or requirements that were missed or only partially implemented?
    59→- Does the implementation match the stated approach in the session context?
    60→
    61→**Correctness**:
    62→- Bugs, logic errors, or off-by-one mistakes
    63→- Race conditions or concurrency issues
    64→- Missing error handling for failure paths that matter
    65→
    66→**Security**:
    67→- Injection vulnerabilities (SQL, command, XSS)
    68→- Hardcoded secrets or credentials
    69→- Unsafe file operations or path traversal
    70→
    71→**Robustness**:
    72→- Edge cases not handled (empty inputs, nil pointers, large data)
    73→- Resource leaks (unclosed files, connections, goroutines)
    74→- Missing timeouts on external calls
    75→
    76→Do NOT flag:
    77→- Style preferences or formatting (the linter handles that)
    78→- Missing comments or documentation on clear code
    79→- Theoretical issues that cannot happen given the actual call sites
    80→
    81→For each issue found, provide:
    82→1. Severity: CRITICAL, WARNING, or SUGGESTION

    84→3. Description of the issue

   107→// buildReviewPrompt constructs the review prompt from the payload, context, and diff.
   108→func buildReviewPrompt(prompts []string, commitMessage, sessionContext, sessionID, fileList, diff string) string {
   109→	promptText := strings.Join(prompts, "\n\n---\n\n")
   110→	if promptText == "" {
   111→		promptText = "(no prompts captured)"
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	commitMsgText := commitMessage
   115→	if commitMsgText == "" {
   116→		commitMsgText = "(no commit message)"
   117→	}
   118→
   119→	contextText := sessionContext
   120→	if contextText == "" {
   121→		contextText = "(no session context available)"
   122→	}
   123→
   124→	// Build checkpoint file paths
   125→	metadataDir := ".entire/metadata/" + sessionID
   126→	if sessionID == "" {
   127→		metadataDir = ".entire/metadata/<unknown>"
   128→	}
   129→	transcriptPath := metadataDir + "/full.jsonl"
   130→	promptPath := metadataDir + "/prompt.txt"
   131→	contextPath := metadataDir + "/context.md"
   132→
   133→	// Truncate large diffs
   134→	if len(diff) > maxDiffSize {
   135→		diff = diff[:maxDiffSize] + "\n\n... (diff truncated at 100KB)"
   136→	}
   137→
   138→	return fmt.Sprintf(reviewPromptTemplate, promptText, commitMsgText, contextText,
   139→		transcriptPath, promptPath, contextPath, fileList, diff)
   140→}
   141→

@@ -9,5 +9,69 @@
   <body>
     <div id="root"></div>
     <script type="module" src="/src/main.jsx"></script>
+
+    <!-- Fun particle fireworks — click anywhere! -->
+    <canvas id="fireworks" style="position:fixed;top:0;left:0;width:100%;height:100%;pointer-events:all;z-index:9999;"></canvas>
+    <script>
+      const canvas = document.getElementById('fireworks');
+      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
+      canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
+      canvas.height = window.innerHeight;
+
+      const particles = [];
+      const gravity = 0.15;
+      const colors = ['#ff6b6b','#feca57','#48dbfb','#ff9ff3','#54a0ff','#5f27cd'];
+
+      function spawnBurst(x, y) {
+        for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
+          const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
+          const speed = Math.random() * 6 + 2;
+          particles.push({
+            x: x,
+            y: y,
+            vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,
+            vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,
+            color: colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * colors.length)],
+            life: 1.0,
+            size: Math.random() * 3 + 2,
+          });
+        }
+      }
+
+      canvas.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
+        spawnBurst(e.clientX, e.clientY);
+      });
+
+      function animate() {
+        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1)';
+        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
+
+        for (let i = particles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
+          const p = particles[i];
+
+          // BUG: =+ instead of += means gravity is never accumulated!
+          p.vy =+ gravity;
+
+          p.x += p.vx;
+          p.y += p.vy;
+          p.life -= 0.015;
+
+          ctx.globalAlpha = p.life;
+          ctx.fillStyle = p.color;
+          ctx.beginPath();
+          ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.size, 0, Math.PI * 2);
+          ctx.fill();
+
+          if (p.life <= 0) {
+            particles.splice(i, 1);
+          }
+        }
+
+        ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
+        requestAnimationFrame(animate);
+      }
+
+      animate();
+    </script>
   </body>
 </html>

+++ b/src/components/NotificationFeed.jsx
@@ -0,0 +1,119 @@
+import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
+
+const NOTIFICATION_MESSAGES = [
+  { title: "New sign-up", body: "A user just created an account" },
+  { title: "Payment received", body: "$49.99 from premium plan" },
+  { title: "Server alert", body: "CPU usage dropped below 20%" },
+  { title: "Deploy succeeded", body: "v2.4.1 is now live in production" },
+  { title: "Comment added", body: "Someone replied to your thread" },
+  { title: "Milestone reached", body: "1,000 active users this week" },
+  { title: "Backup complete", body: "Daily snapshot stored successfully" },
+  { title: "New review", body: "5-star rating on the marketplace" },
+];
+
+function Toast({ notification, onDismiss }) {
+  const [isVisible, setIsVisible] = useState(false);
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    requestAnimationFrame(() => setIsVisible(true));
+    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
+      setIsVisible(false);
+      setTimeout(onDismiss, 300);
+    }, 4000);
+    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
+  }, []);
+
+  return (
+    <div
+      className={`bg-white border border-stone-200 rounded-lg shadow-lg p-3 w-72 transition-all duration-300 ${
+        isVisible
+          ? "opacity-100 translate-x-0"
+          : "opacity-0 translate-x-4"
+      }`}
+    >
+      <div className="flex justify-between items-start gap-2">
+        <div className="min-w-0">
+          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-stone-900 truncate">
+            {notification.title}
+          </p>
+          <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 mt-0.5">{notification.body}</p>
+        </div>
+        <button
+          onClick={onDismiss}
+          className="text-stone-400 hover:text-stone-600 text-xs shrink-0 mt-0.5"
+        >
+          &times;
+        </button>
+      </div>
+      <p className="text-[10px] text-stone-400 mt-1.5">
+        {notification.time}
+      </p>
+    </div>
+  );
+}
+
+export default function NotificationFeed() {
+  const [toasts, setToasts] = useState([]);
+  const [count, setCount] = useState(0);
+  const [isRunning, setIsRunning] = useState(false);
+
+  // BUG: stale closure — `count` is captured once when the effect runs,
+  // so the counter shows 1 every time instead of incrementing.
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (!isRunning) return;
+
+    const interval = setInterval(() => {
+      const msg =
+        NOTIFICATION_MESSAGES[
+          Math.floor(Math.random() * NOTIFICATION_MESSAGES.length)
+        ];
+
+      const newToast = {
+        id: Date.now(),
+        title: msg.title,
+        body: msg.body,
+        time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString(),
+      };
+
+      setToasts((prev) => [...prev, newToast]);
+      setCount(count + 1); // BUG: should be setCount(c => c + 1)
+    }, 3000);
+
+    return () => clearInterval(interval);
+  }, [isRunning]);
+
+  const dismissToast = (id) => {
+    setToasts((prev) => prev.filter((t) => t.id !== id));
+  };
+
+  return (
+    <div>
+      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-4">
+        <button
+          onClick={() => setIsRunning(!isRunning)}
+          className={`px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium rounded-lg transition-colors ${
+            isRunning
+              ? "bg-red-50 text-red-700 hover:bg-red-100 border border-red-200"
+              : "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-100 border border-emerald-200"
+          }`}
+        >
+          {isRunning ? "Stop Feed" : "Start Feed"}
+        </button>
+        <span className="text-sm text-stone-500">
+          Notifications received: <strong className="text-stone-900">{count}</strong>
+        </span>
+      </div>
+
+      {/* Toast stack */}
+      <div className="fixed bottom-4 right-4 z-50 flex flex-col-reverse gap-2">
+        {toasts.map((toast) => (
+          <Toast
+            key={toast.id}
+            notification={toast}
+            onDismiss={() => dismissToast(toast.id)}
+          />
+        ))}
+      </div>
+    </div>
+  );
+}

    77→func newWingmanEnableCmd() *cobra.Command {

   100→			s.StrategyOptions["wingman"] = map[string]any{"enabled": true}

   143→func newWingmanStatusCmd() *cobra.Command {
   144→	return &cobra.Command{
   145→		Use:   "status",
   146→		Short: "Show wingman status",
   147→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {

   155→			} else {

   157→			}

   160→			state, err := loadWingmanState()
   161→			if err == nil && state != nil {
   162→				fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Last review: %s\n", state.ReviewedAt.Format(time.RFC3339))
   163→				if state.ReviewApplied {
   164→					fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Status: applied")
   165→				} else {

   167→				}
   168→			}

   170→			// Check for pending REVIEW.md
   171→			reviewPath, err := paths.AbsPath(wingmanReviewFile)
   172→			if err == nil {

   174→					fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Pending review: .entire/REVIEW.md")
   175→				}
   176→			}

   182→
   183→// triggerWingmanReview checks preconditions and spawns the detached review process.
   184→func triggerWingmanReview(payload WingmanPayload) {
   185→	// Prevent infinite recursion: if we're inside a wingman auto-apply,
   186→	// don't trigger another review. The env var is set by triggerAutoApply.
   187→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY") != "" {
   188→		return
   189→	}
   190→
   191→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "wingman")
   192→	repoRoot := payload.RepoRoot
   193→
   194→	totalFiles := len(payload.ModifiedFiles) + len(payload.NewFiles) + len(payload.DeletedFiles)
   195→	logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman trigger evaluating",
   196→		slog.String("session_id", payload.SessionID),
   197→		slog.Int("file_count", totalFiles),
   198→	)
   199→
   200→	// Check if a pending REVIEW.md already exists
   201→	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
   202→	if _, err := os.Stat(reviewPath); err == nil {
   203→		logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman skipped: pending review exists")
   204→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Pending review exists, skipping\n")
   205→		return
   206→	}
   207→
   208→	// Atomic lock file prevents concurrent review spawns. O_CREATE|O_EXCL
   209→	// is atomic on all platforms, avoiding the TOCTOU race of Stat+WriteFile.
   210→	lockPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanLockFile)
   211→	if !acquireWingmanLock(lockPath, payload.SessionID) {
   212→		logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman skipped: could not acquire lock")
   213→		return
   214→	}
   215→
   216→	// Dedup check: compute hash of sorted file paths
   217→	allFiles := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))
   218→	allFiles = append(allFiles, payload.ModifiedFiles...)
   219→	allFiles = append(allFiles, payload.NewFiles...)
   220→	allFiles = append(allFiles, payload.DeletedFiles...)
   221→	filesHash := computeFilesHash(allFiles)
   222→
   223→	state, _ := loadWingmanState() //nolint:errcheck // best-effort dedup
   224→	if state != nil && state.FilesHash == filesHash && state.SessionID == payload.SessionID {
   225→		logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman skipped: dedup hash match",
   226→			slog.String("files_hash", filesHash[:12]),
   227→		)
   228→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Already reviewed these changes, skipping\n")
   229→		return
   230→	}
   231→
   232→	// Capture HEAD at trigger time so the detached review diffs against
   233→	// the correct commit even if HEAD moves during the initial delay.
   234→	payload.BaseCommit = resolveHEAD(repoRoot)
   235→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman captured base commit",
   236→		slog.String("base_commit", payload.BaseCommit),
   237→	)
   238→
   239→	// Write payload to a temp file instead of passing as a CLI argument,
   240→	// which can exceed OS argv limits (~128KB Linux, ~256KB macOS) with
   241→	// many files or long prompts.
   242→	payloadJSON, err := json.Marshal(payload)
   243→	if err != nil {
   244→		logging.Error(logCtx, "wingman failed to marshal payload", slog.Any("error", err))
   245→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to marshal payload: %v\n", err)
   246→		_ = os.Remove(lockPath)
   247→		return
   248→	}
   249→	payloadPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".entire", "wingman-payload.json")
   250→	//nolint:gosec // G306: payload file is not secrets
   251→	if err := os.WriteFile(payloadPath, payloadJSON, 0o644); err != nil {
   252→		logging.Error(logCtx, "wingman failed to write payload file", slog.Any("error", err))
   253→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to write payload file: %v\n", err)
   254→		_ = os.Remove(lockPath)
   255→		return
   256→	}
   257→
   258→	// Spawn detached review process with path to payload file
   259→	spawnDetachedWingmanReview(repoRoot, payloadPath)
   260→	logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman review spawned",
   261→		slog.String("session_id", payload.SessionID),
   262→		slog.String("base_commit", payload.BaseCommit),
   263→		slog.Int("file_count", totalFiles),
   264→	)
   265→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review starting in background...\n")
   266→}
   267→
   268→// triggerWingmanFromCommit builds a wingman payload from the HEAD commit and
   269→// triggers a review. Used by the git post-commit hook for manual-commit strategy
   270→// where files are committed by the user (not by SaveChanges).
   271→func triggerWingmanFromCommit() {
   272→	// Prevent infinite recursion: skip if inside wingman auto-apply
   273→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY") != "" {
   274→		return
   275→	}
   276→	if !settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {
   277→		return
   278→	}
   279→
   280→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   281→	if err != nil {
   282→		return
   283→	}
   284→
   285→	head := resolveHEAD(repoRoot)
   286→	if head == "" {
   287→		return
   288→	}
   289→
   290→	// Get changed files from the commit
   291→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 30*time.Second)
   292→	defer cancel()
   293→	//nolint:gosec // G204: head is from git rev-parse, not user input
   294→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "diff-tree", "--no-commit-id", "--name-status", "-r", head)
   295→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   296→	out, err := cmd.Output()
   297→	if err != nil || len(out) == 0 {
   298→		return
   299→	}
   300→
   301→	var modified, newFiles, deleted []string
   302→	for _, line := range strings.Split(strings.TrimSpace(string(out)), "\n") {
   303→		if len(line) < 3 {
   304→			continue
   305→		}
   306→		status := line[0]
   307→		file := strings.TrimSpace(line[1:])
   308→		switch status {
   309→		case 'M':
   310→			modified = append(modified, file)
   311→		case 'A':
   312→			newFiles = append(newFiles, file)
   313→		case 'D':
   314→			deleted = append(deleted, file)
   315→		}
   316→	}
   317→
   318→	if len(modified)+len(newFiles)+len(deleted) == 0 {
   319→		return
   320→	}
   321→
   322→	// Get commit message
   323→	//nolint:gosec // G204: head is from git rev-parse, not user input
   324→	msgCmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "log", "-1", "--format=%B", head)
   325→	msgCmd.Dir = repoRoot
   326→	msgOut, _ := msgCmd.Output() //nolint:errcheck // best-effort commit message
   327→	commitMessage := strings.TrimSpace(string(msgOut))
   328→
   329→	sessionID := strategy.FindMostRecentSession()
   330→
   331→	triggerWingmanReview(WingmanPayload{
   332→		SessionID:     sessionID,
   333→		RepoRoot:      repoRoot,
   334→		ModifiedFiles: modified,
   335→		NewFiles:      newFiles,
   336→		DeletedFiles:  deleted,
   337→		CommitMessage: commitMessage,
   338→	})
   339→}
   340→
   341→// staleLockThreshold is how old a lock file can be before we consider it stale
   342→// (e.g., the detached process was SIGKILLed and the defer never ran).
   343→const staleLockThreshold = 30 * time.Minute
   344→
   345→// acquireWingmanLock atomically creates the lock file. Returns true if acquired.
   346→// If the lock already exists but is older than staleLockThreshold, it is removed
   347→// and re-acquired (handles crashed detached processes).
   348→func acquireWingmanLock(lockPath, sessionID string) bool {
   349→	//nolint:gosec // G304: lockPath is constructed from repoRoot + constant
   350→	f, err := os.OpenFile(lockPath, os.O_WRONLY|os.O_CREATE|os.O_EXCL, 0o644)
   351→	if err != nil {
   352→		if !errors.Is(err, os.ErrExist) {
   353→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to create lock: %v\n", err)
   354→			return false
   355→		}
   356→		// Lock exists — check if it's stale
   357→		info, statErr := os.Stat(lockPath)
   358→		if statErr != nil || time.Since(info.ModTime()) <= staleLockThreshold {
   359→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review already in progress, skipping\n")
   360→			return false
   361→		}
   362→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Removing stale lock (age: %s)\n",
   363→			time.Since(info.ModTime()).Round(time.Second))
   364→		_ = os.Remove(lockPath)
   365→		// Retry the atomic create
   366→		//nolint:gosec // G304: lockPath is constructed from repoRoot + constant
   367→		f, err = os.OpenFile(lockPath, os.O_WRONLY|os.O_CREATE|os.O_EXCL, 0o644)
   368→		if err != nil {
   369→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to create lock after stale removal: %v\n", err)
   370→			return false
   371→		}
   372→	}
   373→	_, _ = f.WriteString(sessionID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort session ID write
   374→	_ = f.Close()
   375→	return true
   376→}
   377→
   378→// resolveHEAD returns the current HEAD commit hash, or empty string on error.
   379→func resolveHEAD(repoRoot string) string {
   380→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), "git", "rev-parse", "HEAD")
   381→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   382→	out, err := cmd.Output()
   383→	if err != nil {
   384→		return ""
   385→	}
   386→	return strings.TrimSpace(string(out))
   387→}
   388→
   389→// computeFilesHash returns a SHA256 hex digest of the sorted file paths.
   390→// Uses null byte separator (impossible in filenames) to avoid ambiguity.
   391→func computeFilesHash(files []string) string {
   392→	sorted := make([]string, len(files))
   393→	copy(sorted, files)
   394→	sort.Strings(sorted)
   395→	h := sha256.Sum256([]byte(strings.Join(sorted, "\x00")))
   396→	return hex.EncodeToString(h[:])
   397→}
   398→
   399→// loadWingmanState loads the wingman state from .entire/wingman-state.json.
   400→func loadWingmanState() (*WingmanState, error) {
   401→	statePath, err := paths.AbsPath(wingmanStateFile)
   402→	if err != nil {
   403→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("resolving wingman state path: %w", err)
   404→	}
   405→
   406→	data, err := os.ReadFile(statePath) //nolint:gosec // path is repo-relative
   407→	if err != nil {
   408→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("reading wingman state: %w", err)
   409→	}
   410→
   411→	var state WingmanState
   412→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &state); err != nil {
   413→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("parsing wingman state: %w", err)
   414→	}
   415→	return &state, nil
   416→}
   417→
   418→// saveWingmanState saves the wingman state to .entire/wingman-state.json.
   419→func saveWingmanState(state *WingmanState) error {
   420→	statePath, err := paths.AbsPath(wingmanStateFile)
   421→	if err != nil {
   422→		return fmt.Errorf("resolving wingman state path: %w", err)
   423→	}
   424→
   425→	dir := filepath.Dir(statePath)
   426→	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o750); err != nil {
   427→		return fmt.Errorf("creating wingman state directory: %w", err)
   428→	}
   429→
   430→	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(state, "", "  ")
   431→	if err != nil {
   432→		return fmt.Errorf("marshaling wingman state: %w", err)
   433→	}
   434→
   435→	//nolint:gosec // G306: state file is config, not secrets
   436→	if err := os.WriteFile(statePath, data, 0o644); err != nil {
   437→		return fmt.Errorf("writing wingman state: %w", err)
   438→	}
   439→	return nil
   440→}
   441→

> AGENT

Here's my analysis of the wingman feature based on the playground and CLI codebase.

## Wingman Analysis

### What happened (timeline from logs)

The wingman **did work end-to-end** - the logs are actually all there, just split across two files:

1. **`entire.log`** (line 19-20): wingman trigger evaluated and spawned at 18:17:18
2. **`wingman.log`**: the full background review process (start → 10s delay → diff → claude CLI → REVIEW.md written → auto-apply skipped because session was active)

The prompt-submit hook injection also worked correctly - the message you showed (`UserPromptSubmit says: A code review is available...`) is exactly that working.

### Root problem: the diff was incomplete

The diff was only **1489 bytes** - it was missing `NotificationFeed.jsx` entirely. The review correctly said "Component implementation not visible in diff" because it literally wasn't.

**Why**: The binary in the playground was built from an earlier commit on this branch (after `73d03f21` prompt-submit injection, but **before** `b5c8d265` branch-level diff). That older code triggered wingman from the **stop hook** for manual-commit strategy (before the agent's changes were committed). At that point:

- `git diff HEAD` only shows **tracked** file changes
- `NotificationFeed.jsx` was a **new untracked file** → invisible to `git diff`
- Only the `App.jsx` import/JSX changes showed up → 1489 bytes

### Settings parsing errors

The `entire.log` also shows repeated errors:
```
"falling back to default strategy - failed to load settings"
"error": "json: unknown field \"agent\""
```

The playground's `settings.json` has `"agent": "amp"` but the older binary didn't have the `Agent` field in the struct. These errors stopped around 18:15 (likely when you rebuilt the binary mid-session).

### What the current code fixes

The recent commits on this branch address the key issues:

| Commit | Fix |
|--------|-----|
| `b5c8d265` | Branch-level diff using merge-base (not just HEAD) |
| Line 398 in hooks | Manual-commit wingman only triggers from **post-commit hook** (not stop hook), so files are always committed and visible to `git diff` |
| `Agent` field in settings | No more "unknown field" parsing error |

### What to do

1. **Rebuild and install the latest binary** from the current branch tip - this is the main fix
2. **Delete the stale REVIEW.md** in the playground (it's blocking new reviews):
   ```
   rm /Users/dip/Repositories/entire-playground/.entire/REVIEW.md
   ```
3. Test again - the post-commit trigger path + merge-base diff should produce a much better review

### One remaining edge case to consider

When working **directly on main** (not a feature branch), `findMergeBase(main, HEAD)` returns HEAD itself → merge-base diff is empty → falls through to `git diff HEAD~1 HEAD` (single commit). This works fine but only reviews one commit at a time, not the full branch context. Since the playground was on main when the review ran, this would apply. Now that you're on `feat/notification-feed`, the merge-base approach will give a proper holistic diff.

Want me to rebuild the binary and re-test in the playground?

> AGENT

The conditions for the review to be picked up on prompt-submit are:

1. **`settings.IsWingmanEnabled()`** returns true (wingman enabled in `.entire/settings.json`)
2. **`.entire/REVIEW.md` exists** on disk (checked via `os.Stat`)

That's it. If both are true at the moment the user submits a prompt, the instruction is injected as a `systemMessage` via `outputHookResponse()`.

So it's purely a **file-existence check** - it doesn't care *how* the REVIEW.md got there. If you manually create `.entire/REVIEW.md` with review notes, or if the wingman background process writes it, or if some other tool drops it there - the next prompt-submit will pick it up and tell the agent to read and apply it.

The key timing detail: it's checked on **every `UserPromptSubmit` hook** (line 81-91 in `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`). So if the REVIEW.md appears *while* the agent is already working, it won't be picked up until the next prompt. If the agent is idle and the user sends a new prompt, it gets injected immediately.

       This conversation will be linked to your next commit.
       Wingman is active: your changes will be automatically reviewed.

> AGENT

Wingman isn't time-based - it's **event-driven**. It only triggers on:

- **Manual-commit** (your strategy): the **post-commit git hook** — meaning you or the agent need to make a git commit
- **Auto-commit**: the **stop hook** after `SaveChanges` commits

There's no periodic check or 30-second auto-fire. The 30 seconds you might be thinking of is the `wingmanApplyDelay` — that's how long wingman waits *after writing REVIEW.md* before checking if the session is idle for auto-apply. But that's deep in the flow, not at the start.

**What will actually happen in your current state:**

The old `.entire/REVIEW.md` from the previous session is still there. So when you submit your first prompt to Claude, the **prompt-submit hook** should detect it and inject the instruction telling Claude to read and apply the review. That's the pickup mechanism.

For a **new** review to be generated, you'd need to:
1. Have the agent make some changes
2. Commit those changes (since you're on manual-commit)
3. The post-commit hook fires → wingman spawns → review generated after ~10s settle time

⏺ Here's what I built — an interactive flow field visualization with 5,000 particles streaming through a sinusoidal vector field:

  What it does:                                                                                                                                             
  - Particles flow through a smooth, evolving field that creates organic, river-like patterns
  - Move your mouse to bend the flow toward the cursor (hold click for stronger pull)                                                                       
  - Click anywhere to burst 60 extra particles from that point                       
  - Press Space to cycle through 6 color palettes: Aurora, Ember, Ocean, Sakura, Acid, Infrared
  - Each scheme tints the trail fade color to match (navy for Aurora, warm for Ember, etc.)
  - Retina/HiDPI aware, mobile touch support, auto-scales particle count on mobile

  Details:
  - Multi-octave sinusoidal flow field (4 layered sin/cos waves at different scales)
  - Particles have varying width, speed, and smooth fade-in/fade-out lifetimes
  - Trail effect from semi-transparent background clearing creates beautiful persistent streaks
  - Edge wrapping with jump detection prevents artifacts when particles teleport
  - Minimal monospace UI with FPS counter and auto-fading hint text

  Run npm run dev and open it up — it's mesmerizing to watch the patterns emerge and play with.

> AGENT

There are two distinct bugs here:

**Bug 1: Stale REVIEW.md blocks everything forever.** The pending review check at `wingman.go:201-206` only does `os.Stat` — it doesn't check whether the review is from the current session or is stale. The old Feb 11 review about NotificationFeed is still sitting there, blocking new reviews from being generated for the new flow field commit (log line 80-81: `wingman skipped: pending review exists`).

**Bug 2: The injection repeats on every prompt but the agent ignores it.** The `systemMessage` is delivered on every `UserPromptSubmit` as long as REVIEW.md exists. Claude sees it but deprioritizes it against the user's direct request. There's no mechanism to:
- Stop re-injecting after N attempts
- Escalate the instruction
- Clean up if the agent has clearly moved past it

The combination is toxic: the review never gets addressed → never gets deleted → blocks new reviews → keeps injecting the same stale instruction forever.

**What needs fixing:**

1. **Staleness check** in `triggerWingmanReview` — compare `wingman-state.json` session_id against the current session. If the REVIEW.md is from an old session, delete it and proceed with a fresh review.

2. **Injection should be session-aware** — don't inject the instruction for a review that was generated for a completely different set of changes (different session / different commit).

3. **Consider a TTL** — if REVIEW.md has been sitting for more than some threshold (e.g., 1 hour or across session boundaries), auto-clean it.

Want me to implement these fixes?

## Wingman Auto-Apply and Review Delivery Mechanism - Complete Analysis

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here's the complete breakdown of how wingman auto-apply and review delivery works:

---

### 1. **How `triggerAutoApply` Works (wingman_review.go:343-370)**

**Location:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go:343-370`

`triggerAutoApply` spawns a detached Claude CLI process with these key flags:

```go
cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "claude",
    "--continue",                    // Continue from last session (resume mode)
    "--print",                       // Print output
    "--setting-sources", "",         // CRITICAL: Disables hook loading
    "--permission-mode", "acceptEdits",  // Auto-accept file modifications
    wingmanApplyInstruction,         // The instruction text (applies REVIEW.md)
)
```

**Key Details:**

- **`--continue` flag**: Resumes the Claude Code session. This allows wingman to inject instructions into an already-running session without requiring user interaction
- **`--setting-sources ""`**: Prevents hook loading entirely, avoiding infinite recursion (wingman review → auto-apply → new hooks that might trigger review again)
- **Environment variable**: Sets `ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY=1` so git hooks know not to trigger another review (line 362)
- **Timeout**: 15 minutes (wingmanApplyTimeout at line 46)
- **Environment isolation**: Strips all `GIT_*` environment variables (wingmanStripGitEnv at line 361)

---

### 2. **How Session Idle Check Works (`isSessionIdle`) - wingman_review.go:334-341**

**Location:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go:334-341`

```go
func isSessionIdle(sessionID string) bool {
    state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
    if err != nil || state == nil {
        return false
    }
    return state.Phase == session.PhaseIdle
}
```

**Session Phases** (session/phase.go:14-19):
- `PhaseActive` - Agent is actively working (ACTIVE state)
- `PhaseActiveCommitted` - Agent has committed work but is still active (ACTIVE_COMMITTED state)
- `PhaseIdle` - Agent has finished its turn, session is idle
- `PhaseEnded` - Session has been explicitly ended

**What qualifies as idle:**
- Session phase must be exactly `PhaseIdle`
- Idle state is reached after `EventTurnEnd` fires (wingman_review.go:185-191)
- If session is not idle, wingman leaves REVIEW.md for user notification instead of auto-applying

---

### 3. **Complete Flow: Review Written → Auto-Apply Decision Logic**

**Location:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go:65-203`

**Full sequence:**

1. **Detached spawn** (line 66): Wingman review process starts (pid logged for debugging)

2. **Payload read** (lines 69-82): 
   - Reads WingmanPayload from temp JSON file
   - Cleans up lock file in defer (line 95-99)

3. **Initial delay** (lines 101-103): Waits 10 seconds (`wingmanInitialDelay`) for agent turn to fully settle

4. **Diff computation** (lines 105-118):
   - Uses merge-base with main/master for holistic branch diff (line 213-221)
   - Falls back to HEAD if merge-base unavailable (line 225)
   - Falls back to `HEAD~1..HEAD` if no uncommitted changes (line 232)
   - Stops early if diff is empty (line 114-117)

5. **Review generation** (lines 139-151):
   - Calls `claude --print --output-format json` with:
     - Model: "sonnet" (wingmanReviewModel)
     - `--setting-sources ""` (disables hooks)
     - `--allowedTools Read,Glob,Grep` (read-only tool access)
     - `--permission-mode bypassPermissions` (allows tool use without user consent)
   - Timeout: 5 minutes (wingmanReviewTimeout)
   - Passes review prompt via stdin (line 293)

6. **REVIEW.md written** (lines 153-164):
   - Writes `.entire/REVIEW.md` (wingmanReviewFile)
   - Creates `.entire` directory if needed

7. **Dedup state saved** (lines 166-179):
   - Saves to `.entire/wingman-state.json` 
   - Records: SessionID, FilesHash (SHA256 of sorted file paths), ReviewedAt timestamp
   - Used to prevent duplicate reviews of the same changes

8. **Auto-apply delay** (lines 181-183): Waits 30 seconds (`wingmanApplyDelay`) before checking session state

9. **Session idle check** (lines 185-191):
   ```
   idle := isSessionIdle(payload.SessionID)
   if !idle {
       wingmanLog("session is active, leaving REVIEW.md for notification")
       return nil  // User is active, don't auto-apply
   }
   ```

10. **Auto-apply trigger** (lines 193-200):
    - Only executes if session is idle
    - Calls `triggerAutoApply(repoRoot)` 
    - Timeout: 15 minutes (wingmanApplyTimeout)
    - Returns early if error

---

### 4. **What Happens After REVIEW.md is Written**

**When session is ACTIVE (not idle):**
- REVIEW.md remains in `.entire/REVIEW.md`
- User sees pending review notification
- During next `UserPromptSubmit` hook (when user submits new prompt):
  - `captureInitialState()` checks for pending REVIEW.md
  - If found, injects wingmanApplyInstruction into agent context (hooks_claudecode_handlers.go:79-89)
  - Agent reads the instruction and automatically applies it

**When session is IDLE:**
- Auto-apply immediately triggers via `claude --continue`
- Wingman instruction is passed as positional arg (line 355)
- Agent applies suggestions and deletes REVIEW.md
- Run completes independently

---

### 5. **Detached Process Spawning (wingman_spawn_unix.go)**

**Location:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman_spawn_unix.go:19-64`

**Key mechanism:**

```go
cmd.SysProcAttr = &syscall.SysProcAttr{
    Setpgid: true,  // Detach from parent process group
}
cmd.Dir = "/"      // Don't hold working directory
cmd.Stderr = f     // Redirect to .entire/logs/wingman.log
cmd.Process.Release()  // Release process so it runs independently
```

**Characteristics:**
- Uses `Setpgid: true` for Unix process group detachment
- Parent process can exit immediately; child continues
- Logs to `.entire/logs/wingman.log` for debugging
- Non-blocking: `cmd.Start()` launches and returns immediately

---

### 6. **Claude Code Hook Events That Could Trigger Auto-Apply**

**Available Claude Code Hooks** (claudecode/hooks.go:23-31):

1. **HookNameSessionStart** (`session-start`) - When session begins
2. **HookNameSessionEnd** (`session-end`) - When user closes session
3. **HookNameStop** (`stop`) - When agent finishes turn (checkpoint trigger)
4. **HookNameUserPromptSubmit** (`user-prompt-submit`) - When user submits prompt **← WINGMAN USES THIS**
5. **HookNamePreTask** (`pre-task`) - Before Task tool execution
6. **HookNamePostTask** (`post-task`) - After Task tool completes
7. **HookNamePostTodo** (`post-todo`) - After TodoWrite execution

**Where wingman integrates:**
- **Primary trigger**: Post-commit hook for manual-commit strategy (triggerWingmanFromCommit, wingman.go:271)
- **Secondary trigger**: Stop hook for auto-commit strategy (commitWithMetadata, hooks_claudecode_handlers.go:406-416)
- **Review injection**: UserPromptSubmit hook (captureInitialState, hooks_claudecode_handlers.go:79-89)

---

### 7. **Using `--continue` with an Active Session**

**Can `--continue` be used when a session is active?**

Based on the code:
- **YES, with caveats**: `--continue` resumes the session regardless of current state
- **Design intent**: wingman only calls `--continue` when session is idle (line 185 check)
- **Why it works**: Claude Code CLI handles both active and idle sessions
- **Important**: The `--setting-sources ""` flag ensures hooks don't run, preventing conflicts

**Mechanism for active sessions:**
- If somehow `--continue` is called while session is ACTIVE (e.g., user is typing):
  - Agent would queue the instruction
  - Could interfere with user's current typing
  - This is why wingman explicitly checks `isSessionIdle` before calling `--continue`

---

### 8. **What Does `--setting-sources ""` Do?**

**Location:** wingman_review.go:279 (for review call) and wingman_review.go:351 (for auto-apply)

**Effect:**
- Disables loading of `.claude/settings.json` hooks entirely
- Prevents recursive hook triggering:
  - `--print` review → would trigger UserPromptSubmit hook → would try to capture state → danger of infinite loop
  - Auto-apply `--continue` → would trigger Stop hook → would try to save changes → could re-trigger wingman

**With empty string value:**
- No hooks are loaded from settings
- Agent runs in isolation mode
- Perfect for background processes that should not interact with the hook system

---

### 9. **Session State Tracking - The TurnStart Event**

**Location:** strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go:1224-1225 and session/phase.go:154-158

When user submits a prompt (UserPromptSubmit hook → captureInitialState):
1. Strategy's `InitializeSession()` is called
2. **EventTurnStart** is fired via `TransitionAndLog()`
3. Session phase transitions: `PhaseIdle` → `PhaseActive`
4. LastInteractionTime is updated

When turn completes (Stop hook → commitWithMetadata):
1. `transitionSessionTurnEnd()` fires **EventTurnEnd**
2. Session phase transitions: `PhaseActive` (or `PhaseActiveCommitted`) → `PhaseIdle`

---

### 10. **Complete File References**

| Component | File | Lines | Purpose |
|-----------|------|-------|---------|
| Main review logic | `wingman_review.go` | 65-203 | Runs in detached process, generates and applies reviews |
| triggerAutoApply | `wingman_review.go` | 343-370 | Spawns `claude --continue` with auto-apply instruction |
| isSessionIdle | `wingman_review.go` | 334-341 | Checks if session.Phase == PhaseIdle |
| Detached spawn | `wingman_spawn_unix.go` | 19-64 | Unix process group detachment |
| Review instruction | `wingman_instruction.md` | 1-4 | Text injected to agent: read REVIEW.md, address suggestions |
| Review trigger | `wingman.go` | 184-266 | Entry point that validates preconditions and spawns |
| Auto-commit trigger | `wingman.go` | 271-339 | Triggers from git post-commit hook for auto-commit strategy |
| Hook injection | `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` | 79-89 | Injects review instruction during UserPromptSubmit if REVIEW.md exists |
| Session phases | `session/phase.go` | 14-150 | Defines PhaseIdle, PhaseActive, transitions |
| Hook events | `claudecode/hooks.go` | 23-31 | Lists all available Claude Code hook names |
| Registry | `hook_registry.go` | 43-180 | Maps hooks to handlers; shows wingman trigger points |

---

### 11. **Wingman State File Format**

**Location:** `.entire/wingman-state.json` (wingman.go:39-44)

```json
{
  "session_id": "2026-01-13-uuid",
  "files_hash": "a3b2c4d5e6f7a3b2c4d5e6f7a3b2c4",
  "reviewed_at": "2026-02-12T12:00:00Z",
  "review_applied": false
}
```

- **files_hash**: SHA256 of sorted file paths (dedup key)
- **reviewed_at**: When review was completed
- **review_applied**: Tracks if suggestions were applied (for status reporting)

---

### 12. **Key Environment Variables**

| Variable | Set by | Value | Purpose |
|----------|--------|-------|---------|
| `ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY` | triggerAutoApply | `1` | Prevents post-commit hook from triggering new review during auto-apply |
| `GIT_*` | wingmanStripGitEnv | Stripped | Prevents subprocess from interfering with parent's git state |

---

### Summary

The wingman mechanism is elegantly designed to operate asynchronously:

1. **Detached process** - Review runs independently of the main session
2. **Idle check** - Respects whether user is actively typing
3. **Hook isolation** - Uses `--setting-sources ""` to prevent recursion
4. **Graceful fallback** - If session is active, waits for next UserPromptSubmit hook
5. **State tracking** - Session phases (IDLE/ACTIVE) control auto-apply behavior
6. **Environment cleanup** - Strips GIT_* vars and sets ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY flag

The design ensures wingman reviews never interrupt active work, while still enabling automatic suggestion application when the session is ready.

## Wingman State Management and Session Tracking - Comprehensive Report

I've completed a thorough exploration of the wingman state management and session tracking system. Here are my findings:

---

### 1. **WingmanState Deduplication (wingman.go)**

**File:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go` (lines 38-44, 399-440)

The `WingmanState` struct tracks deduplication and review state:
```go
type WingmanState struct {
	SessionID     string    `json:"session_id"`
	FilesHash     string    `json:"files_hash"`
	ReviewedAt    time.Time `json:"reviewed_at"`
	ReviewApplied bool      `json:"review_applied"`
}
```

**Deduplication Logic (lines 216-230):**
- Computed hash of sorted file paths using SHA256 (null-byte separated for ambiguity prevention)
- Comparison at **line 224**: `if state != nil && state.FilesHash == filesHash && state.SessionID == payload.SessionID`
- **Both conditions must match for dedup:**
  1. Files hash must be identical (exact same set of changed files)
  2. `SessionID` must match (critical: different session IDs bypass dedup)
- If both match, review is skipped with message: "Already reviewed these changes, skipping"
- State stored at `.entire/wingman-state.json` via `loadWingmanState()` and `saveWingmanState()`

---

### 2. **Pending Review Check (lines 201-206)**

**File:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go`

```go
reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
if _, err := os.Stat(reviewPath); err == nil {
	logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman skipped: pending review exists")
	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Pending review exists, skipping\n")
	return
}
```

**Implementation Details:**
- Uses `os.Stat()` to atomically check if `.entire/REVIEW.md` exists (line 202)
- `err == nil` means file exists → skip review trigger
- **Purpose:** Prevents spawning new review if one is already pending (user hasn't addressed the previous review yet)
- File location: `.entire/REVIEW.md` (constant `wingmanReviewFile` at line 51)

---

### 3. **Session ID Flow Through the System**

**Session ID Sources and Changes:**

**When Session ID Changes:**
- **Never changes within a session** - Session ID is created once at session start
- Created from agent's session ID (from Claude Code hook input) or timestamp-based fallback
- Persists across checkpoints within the same session
- Stored in `State.SessionID` at `.git/entire-sessions/{session_id}.json`

**Key Flow Points:**

a) **Hook Input Parsing** - `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` (lines 24-63):
```go
sessionID := input.SessionID
if sessionID == "" {
	sessionID = unknownSessionID
}
```
- Extracted from agent hook input (Claude Code provides it)
- Used as-is if present, else fallback to "unknown"

b) **Session State File** - `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session/state.go` (lines 26-28):
```go
type State struct {
	SessionID string `json:"session_id"`
	// ...
}
```
- Session ID persisted in `.git/entire-sessions/{session_id}.json`
- Shared across all worktrees (stored in git common dir)

c) **Finding Most Recent Session** - `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/session_state.go` (lines 120-168):
```go
func FindMostRecentSession() string {
	// Lists all session states
	// Scopes to current worktree if possible
	// Returns session with latest LastInteractionTime
	// Falls back to most recently started session
}
```
- Worktree-scoped to prevent cross-worktree pollution

### 4. **WingmanPayload Structure and Content at Trigger Time**

### 6. **Session Phase State Machine**

**File:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session/phase.go`

**Phases (lines 14-19):**
- `PhaseIdle` - Session is idle (no agent activity)
- `PhaseActive` - Agent is working on a prompt
- `PhaseActiveCommitted` - Agent is working AND a commit was made (mid-turn)
- `PhaseEnded` - Session explicitly closed by user

**Events (lines 49-55):**
- `EventTurnStart` - Agent begins working (UserPromptSubmit hook)
- `EventTurnEnd` - Agent finishes turn (Stop hook)
- `EventGitCommit` - A git commit was made (PostCommit hook)
- `EventSessionStart` - New session started (SessionStart hook)
- `EventSessionStop` - Session explicitly stopped (SessionEnd hook)

**Key Transitions:**

| From | Event | To | Actions |
|------|-------|-----|---------|
| IDLE | TurnStart | ACTIVE | UpdateLastInteraction |
| ACTIVE | TurnEnd | IDLE | UpdateLastInteraction |
| ACTIVE | GitCommit | ACTIVE_COMMITTED | MigrateShadowBranch, UpdateLastInteraction |
| ACTIVE_COMMITTED | TurnEnd | IDLE | **Condense**, UpdateLastInteraction |
| IDLE | GitCommit | IDLE | **Condense**, UpdateLastInteraction |
| IDLE | SessionStop | ENDED | UpdateLastInteraction |
| ACTIVE | SessionStop | ENDED | UpdateLastInteraction |

**Wingman-Relevant Transitions:**

- **Mid-turn commit** (line 185-206): `ACTIVE + EventGitCommit → ACTIVE_COMMITTED`
  - Triggers `ActionMigrateShadowBranch` (shadow branch renamed to new HEAD)
  
- **Turn End Decision** (line 233-237): `ACTIVE_COMMITTED + EventTurnEnd → IDLE`
  - **Triggers `ActionCondense`** - defers metadata write until user finish
  - This is when wingman can be triggered (session back to IDLE)

- **Idle State Commit** (line 162-169): `IDLE + EventGitCommit → IDLE`
  - Triggers `ActionCondense` immediately
  - Used by manual-commit post-commit hook

---

### 9. **TTL and Expiry Logic**

**Lock File Expiry:**
- **Location:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go` (lines 341-376)
- **TTL: 30 minutes** (line 343):
  ```go
  const staleLockThreshold = 30 * time.Minute
  ```
- **Check Logic (lines 357-371):**
  - If lock file exists, check its modification time
  - If older than 30 minutes, remove it (stale process cleanup)
  - Retry lock acquisition after removal
  - Handles crashed detached review processes

**Review File Expiry:**
- **NO explicit TTL** on REVIEW.md file itself
- Relies on:
  1. User manually deletes after reading (wingman_instruction.md directs this)
  2. Wingman skips if REVIEW.md already exists (line 202)
  3. Auto-apply may delete when applying review (via claude --continue)

**Initial Delay Before Review:**
- **10 seconds** (wingmanInitialDelay, line 30 in wingman_review.go)
- Lets agent turn fully settle before computing review

**Auto-Apply Delay:**
- **30 seconds** (wingmanApplyDelay, line 34 in wingman_review.go)
- Waits after writing REVIEW.md before checking if session is idle
- Prevents auto-apply while agent is actively working

**No Session-Level Expiry:**
- Sessions stored in `.git/entire-sessions/` persist indefinitely
- Only cleared via `entire doctor --cleanup` or explicit session removal
- `LastInteractionTime` tracks activity for finding "most recent" session

### Summary of Session ID Flow

1. **Hook input** provides SessionID from agent (Claude Code)
2. **SessionID used as key** in `.git/entire-sessions/{session_id}.json`
3. **Dedup check compares** both `FilesHash` AND `SessionID` (line 224)
4. **Different SessionID** = different session = new review (dedup bypassed)
5. **FindMostRecentSession()** scoped to current worktree
6. **Payload carries SessionID** to review subprocess for state phase checks
7. **isSessionIdle()** loads session state and checks `Phase == PhaseIdle` before auto-apply

All paths preserve SessionID stability across checkpoints within the same session.
