Here is the comprehensive implementation plan for improving the wingman auto-review feature.

## Implementation Plan: Wingman Reliability Improvements

### Overview of Current Flow and Problems

The current wingman flow has three critical issues:

1. **Stale REVIEW.md**: `triggerWingmanReview()` at line 201-206 of `wingman.go` checks `os.Stat(reviewPath)` and returns immediately if REVIEW.md exists, with no consideration of age or session. A REVIEW.md from a previous session permanently blocks all future reviews.

2. **30s delay**: In `wingman_review.go` line 182-183, after writing REVIEW.md, the process sleeps for 30 seconds before checking if the session is idle. This is unnecessary latency.

3. **systemMessage injection unreliable**: In `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` lines 79-89, the `captureInitialState()` function injects a `systemMessage` via `outputHookResponse()` when REVIEW.md exists. The agent sees this but deprioritizes it versus the user's actual prompt, so the review never gets addressed, REVIEW.md never gets deleted, and new reviews are permanently blocked.

### Change A: Stale Review Cleanup

**Goal**: When REVIEW.md exists but is from a different session or older than 1 hour, delete it and proceed with a new review.

**File**: `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go`

**Implementation**:

1. Extract the REVIEW.md existence check at lines 200-206 of `triggerWingmanReview()` into a new function `shouldSkipExistingReview(repoRoot, currentSessionID string) bool`.

2. The new function logic:
   ```
   func shouldSkipExistingReview(repoRoot, currentSessionID string) bool:
     reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
     if _, err := os.Stat(reviewPath); err != nil:
       return false  // No REVIEW.md exists, don't skip
     
     // Load wingman state to check session ownership
     state := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)
     if state == nil:
       // No state file but REVIEW.md exists -- stale orphan, delete and proceed
       os.Remove(reviewPath)
       return false
     
     // Same session: keep existing review (skip)
     if state.SessionID == currentSessionID:
       return true
     
     // Different session: delete stale review
     os.Remove(reviewPath)
     return false
   ```

3. Also add a TTL check: if `state.ReviewedAt` is older than 1 hour (`time.Since(state.ReviewedAt) > 1*time.Hour`), delete and proceed regardless of session.

4. Replace lines 200-206 in `triggerWingmanReview()`:
   ```go
   if shouldSkipExistingReview(repoRoot, payload.SessionID) {
       logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman skipped: pending review exists for current session")
       fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Pending review exists, skipping\n")
       return
   }
   ```

5. Apply the same cleanup logic in `triggerWingmanFromCommit()`. Since `triggerWingmanFromCommit()` calls `triggerWingmanReview()`, the fix propagates automatically.

6. Add a helper `loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot string) *WingmanState` that reads state from `repoRoot/.entire/wingman-state.json` without using `paths.AbsPath()` (matching the pattern of `saveWingmanStateDirect` at line 387). This is needed because `triggerWingmanReview` already has `repoRoot` available and runs in the stop hook context where cwd may not be the repo root.

**Constant to add**:
```go
const wingmanStaleReviewTTL = 1 * time.Hour
```

### Change B: Remove 30s Delay, Immediate Auto-Apply in Review Process

**Goal**: After writing REVIEW.md, check idle immediately and trigger auto-apply without the 30s wait.

**File**: `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go`

**Implementation**:

1. Remove the `wingmanApplyDelay` constant (line 34) or keep it but stop using it.

2. In `runWingmanReview()`, replace lines 181-191:
   ```go
   // OLD:
   // wingmanLog("waiting %s before auto-apply check", wingmanApplyDelay)
   // time.Sleep(wingmanApplyDelay)
   // idle := isSessionIdle(payload.SessionID)
   
   // NEW:
   idle := isSessionIdle(payload.SessionID)
   wingmanLog("session idle check: idle=%v", idle)
   
   if !idle {
       wingmanLog("session is active, stop hook will handle auto-apply when turn ends")
       return nil
   }
   ```

3. When idle, proceed immediately to `triggerAutoApply(repoRoot)` as before (no delay).

4. When not idle, return nil. The stop hook (Change C) will pick up the pending REVIEW.md when the turn ends.

### Change C: Stop Hook Auto-Apply

**Goal**: After the agent turn ends and the session transitions to IDLE, check for a pending REVIEW.md and spawn a detached auto-apply process.

**Files to modify**:
- `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` -- add auto-apply trigger in stop hook
- `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go` -- add `__apply` subcommand and spawner
- `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go` -- add `runWingmanApply()` function
- `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman_spawn_unix.go` -- add `spawnDetachedWingmanApply()`
- `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman_spawn_other.go` -- add no-op `spawnDetachedWingmanApply()`

**Step 1: Add `__apply` subcommand**

In `wingman.go`, inside `newWingmanCmd()`, add:
```go
cmd.AddCommand(newWingmanApplyCmd())
```

Add a new function:
```go
func newWingmanApplyCmd() *cobra.Command {
    return &cobra.Command{
        Use:    "__apply",
        Hidden: true,
        Args:   cobra.ExactArgs(1), // repo root path
        RunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
            return runWingmanApply(args[0])
        },
    }
}
```

**Step 2: Implement `runWingmanApply(repoRoot string)` in `wingman_review.go`**

```go
func runWingmanApply(repoRoot string) error {
    wingmanLog("apply process started (pid=%d)", os.Getpid())
    
    reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
    if _, err := os.Stat(reviewPath); err != nil {
        wingmanLog("no REVIEW.md found, nothing to apply")
        return nil
    }
    
    // Check retry prevention
    state := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)
    if state != nil && state.ApplyAttemptedAt != nil {
        wingmanLog("apply already attempted at %s, skipping", state.ApplyAttemptedAt.Format(time.RFC3339))
        return nil
    }
    
    // Mark apply as attempted BEFORE triggering
    if state != nil {
        now := time.Now()
        state.ApplyAttemptedAt = &now
        saveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot, state)
    }
    
    wingmanLog("triggering auto-apply via claude --continue")
    applyStart := time.Now()
    if err := triggerAutoApply(repoRoot); err != nil {
        wingmanLog("ERROR auto-apply failed after %s: %v", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond), err)
        return fmt.Errorf("failed to trigger auto-apply: %w", err)
    }
    wingmanLog("auto-apply completed in %s", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond))
    
    return nil
}
```

**Step 3: Add `spawnDetachedWingmanApply()` in spawn files**

In `wingman_spawn_unix.go`, add a function mirroring `spawnDetachedWingmanReview`:
```go
func spawnDetachedWingmanApply(repoRoot string) {
    executable, err := os.Executable()
    if err != nil {
        return
    }
    
    cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), executable, "wingman", "__apply", repoRoot)
    cmd.SysProcAttr = &syscall.SysProcAttr{Setpgid: true}
    cmd.Dir = "/"
    cmd.Env = os.Environ()
    cmd.Stdout = io.Discard
    
    logDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".entire", "logs")
    if mkErr := os.MkdirAll(logDir, 0o750); mkErr == nil {
        if f, openErr := os.OpenFile(filepath.Join(logDir, "wingman.log"),
            os.O_CREATE|os.O_APPEND|os.O_WRONLY, 0o600); openErr == nil {
            cmd.Stderr = f
        } else {
            cmd.Stderr = io.Discard
        }
    } else {
        cmd.Stderr = io.Discard
    }
    
    if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
        return
    }
    _ = cmd.Process.Release()
}
```

In `wingman_spawn_other.go`, add no-op:
```go
func spawnDetachedWingmanApply(_ string) {}
```

**Step 4: Trigger from stop hook**

In `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`, in `commitWithMetadata()`, AFTER the `transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)` call at line 401 and AFTER the existing wingman trigger (lines 403-416), add a new block:

```go
// Trigger wingman auto-apply if a pending review exists.
// This fires AFTER transitionSessionTurnEnd (session is now IDLE),
// so the agent won't interfere with the apply process.
if settings.IsWingmanEnabled() && os.Getenv("ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY") == "" {
    reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
    if _, statErr := os.Stat(reviewPath); statErr == nil {
        // Check retry prevention
        wingmanState, _ := loadWingmanState()
        if wingmanState == nil || wingmanState.ApplyAttemptedAt == nil {
            fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Pending review found, spawning auto-apply\n")
            spawnDetachedWingmanApply(repoRoot)
        }
    }
}
```

**Important sequencing note**: This auto-apply trigger in the stop hook must come AFTER the existing `triggerWingmanReview()` call. The review trigger spawns the background review process (which writes REVIEW.md ~10s later). The auto-apply trigger here handles the case where a REVIEW.md ALREADY exists from a previous turn's review. These are two separate scenarios:
- Turn produces new changes AND a review already exists from last turn -> apply the old review now; the new review will be generated in background
- Turn produces new changes AND no review exists -> spawn review process; review process handles its own auto-apply if session is still idle when it finishes

### Change D: Retry Prevention

**Goal**: Prevent infinite loops where auto-apply repeatedly fires if the agent fails to delete REVIEW.md.

**File**: `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go`

**Implementation**:

1. Add `ApplyAttemptedAt *time.Time` field to `WingmanState`:
   ```go
   type WingmanState struct {
       SessionID        string     `json:"session_id"`
       FilesHash        string     `json:"files_hash"`
       ReviewedAt       time.Time  `json:"reviewed_at"`
       ReviewApplied    bool       `json:"review_applied"`
       ApplyAttemptedAt *time.Time `json:"apply_attempted_at,omitempty"`
   }
   ```

2. In `runWingmanReview()`, when saving state after writing REVIEW.md (line 173-178), ensure `ApplyAttemptedAt` is nil (reset for new review):
   ```go
   saveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot, &WingmanState{
       SessionID:        payload.SessionID,
       FilesHash:        computeFilesHash(allFilePaths),
       ReviewedAt:       time.Now(),
       ReviewApplied:    false,
       ApplyAttemptedAt: nil,  // Reset: new review, new apply opportunity
   })
   ```

3. Before auto-apply in BOTH paths (review process and stop hook), check `state.ApplyAttemptedAt != nil`:
   - In `runWingmanReview()` auto-apply path (after idle check):
     ```go
     // Mark as attempted before triggering
     now := time.Now()
     state := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)
     if state != nil {
         state.ApplyAttemptedAt = &now
         saveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot, state)

4. In stop hook auto-apply trigger (Change C Step 4), check `ApplyAttemptedAt` before spawning.

### Change E: Improved Prompt-Submit Injection as Backup

**Goal**: Keep systemMessage injection but make it session-aware and retry-aware.

**File**: `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`

**Implementation**:

Replace lines 79-91 in `captureInitialState()`:

```go
// If a wingman review is pending, inject instruction into the agent's context
// so it automatically reads and applies the suggestions.
// This is a backup mechanism; the primary path is auto-apply via __apply.
if settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {
    repoRoot, rootErr := paths.RepoRoot()
    if rootErr == nil {
        if _, statErr := os.Stat(filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)); statErr == nil {
            // Only inject if review is from current session and auto-apply
            // hasn't been attempted yet
            state, _ := loadWingmanState()
            shouldInject := true
            if state != nil {
                // Skip injection if from a different session (stale review)
                if state.SessionID != hookData.sessionID {
                    shouldInject = false
                }
                // Skip injection if auto-apply was already attempted
                if state.ApplyAttemptedAt != nil {
                    shouldInject = false
                }
            }
            
            if shouldInject {
                fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review available: .entire/REVIEW.md\n")
                if err := outputHookResponse(wingmanApplyInstruction); err != nil {
                    fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Warning: failed to inject review instruction: %v\n", err)
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

Note: This requires access to `hookData.sessionID` from `parseAndLogHookInput()`. Currently, lines 79-91 are after line 77 where `CapturePrePromptState` is called, and the `hookData` variable from line 69 is in scope. So the sessionID is available.

### Edge Cases and Race Conditions

1. **Race: Review process writes REVIEW.md while stop hook checks for it**
   - The 10-second initial delay in the review process makes this unlikely for the same turn. The stop hook completes in seconds, while review process hasn't written REVIEW.md yet.
   - For a PREVIOUS turn's review that's still being generated: the lock file prevents concurrent review spawns, and the `__apply` subcommand re-checks REVIEW.md existence before calling `claude --continue`.

2. **Race: Two stop hooks fire in quick succession (multi-agent)**
   - The `ApplyAttemptedAt` guard prevents duplicate applies. The first stop hook marks it attempted; the second one sees it and skips.
   - File-level atomicity: `saveWingmanStateDirect` uses `os.WriteFile` which is not atomic, but the window is very small and the worst case is two applies (which is harmless since `claude --continue` is idempotent from the agent's perspective).

3. **Edge: Agent session ends (SessionEnd hook) with pending REVIEW.md**
   - `handleClaudeCodeSessionEnd()` only transitions to ENDED. It doesn't trigger auto-apply.
   - This is correct: once the session ends, there's no interactive session to `--continue` into. The REVIEW.md will be cleaned up by the next session's stale review check (Change A).

4. **Edge: `claude --continue --print` fails or times out**
   - `ApplyAttemptedAt` is set before triggering, so it won't retry endlessly.
   - The REVIEW.md remains on disk. On the next user prompt, the injection backup (Change E) is skipped because `ApplyAttemptedAt` is set.
   - On the next agent turn that produces changes, a new review replaces the old one (Change A cleans up stale reviews from the same session after TTL, or from different sessions immediately).

5. **Edge: ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY=1 propagation**
   - Already handled: both `triggerWingmanReview()` (line 187-189) and `triggerWingmanFromCommit()` (line 273-275) check this env var. The stop hook auto-apply trigger (Change C) also checks it.
   - The `__apply` command inherits the parent environment, and `triggerAutoApply()` already sets `ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY=1` on the spawned `claude --continue` process (line 362).

6. **Edge: loadWingmanState vs loadWingmanStateDirect**
   - `loadWingmanState()` uses `paths.AbsPath()` which requires being in a git repo context (uses `git rev-parse`). This works in hook handlers because they run inside the repo.
   - `loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)` takes an explicit path. Used in detached processes (`__review`, `__apply`) where cwd is `/`.
   - Need to add `loadWingmanStateDirect()` (read counterpart of `saveWingmanStateDirect`).

### Files to Create/Modify

| File | Action | Description |
|------|--------|-------------|
| `cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go` | Modify | Add `ApplyAttemptedAt` to `WingmanState`; add `shouldSkipExistingReview()`; add `loadWingmanStateDirect()`; add `newWingmanApplyCmd()`; register `__apply` subcommand; add `wingmanStaleReviewTTL` constant |
| `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go` | Modify | Remove 30s delay; remove `wingmanApplyDelay` constant; add `runWingmanApply()`; update auto-apply in `runWingmanReview()` to set `ApplyAttemptedAt`; reset `ApplyAttemptedAt` when saving new review state |
| `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_spawn_unix.go` | Modify | Add `spawnDetachedWingmanApply()` function |

| `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` | Modify | Add stop hook auto-apply trigger after `transitionSessionTurnEnd`; improve prompt-submit injection with session/retry checks |
| `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_test.go` | Modify | Add tests for `shouldSkipExistingReview()`, `WingmanState` with `ApplyAttemptedAt` roundtrip, stale review cleanup |

### Testing Approach

**Unit tests** (in `wingman_test.go`):

1. `TestShouldSkipExistingReview_NoReviewFile` -- returns false when no REVIEW.md exists
2. `TestShouldSkipExistingReview_SameSession` -- returns true when REVIEW.md exists and state has same session ID
3. `TestShouldSkipExistingReview_DifferentSession` -- returns false (deletes REVIEW.md) when session IDs differ
4. `TestShouldSkipExistingReview_StaleReview` -- returns false (deletes REVIEW.md) when review is older than 1 hour even with same session
5. `TestShouldSkipExistingReview_NoStateFile` -- returns false (deletes orphan REVIEW.md) when no wingman-state.json exists
6. `TestWingmanState_ApplyAttemptedAt_RoundTrip` -- JSON serialization roundtrip with the new field
7. `TestWingmanState_ApplyAttemptedAt_Nil` -- Verify nil omitempty behavior

**Integration tests** (if feasible, in `integration_test/`):

1. Test full flow: stop hook fires -> REVIEW.md exists -> auto-apply spawned
2. Test retry prevention: stop hook fires twice -> only one apply spawned
3. Test stale cleanup: REVIEW.md from different session -> cleaned up -> new review triggered

**Manual testing checklist**:

1. Enable wingman, make changes, verify review is generated
2. While review is pending, start new turn -- verify auto-apply fires from stop hook
3. Kill session, start new session -- verify stale REVIEW.md is cleaned up
4. Verify `ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY=1` prevents recursion
5. Verify non-Unix platforms degrade gracefully (no-op spawners)

### Implementation Order

1. **Change D first** (add `ApplyAttemptedAt` to `WingmanState`) -- no behavioral change, just adds the field
2. **Change A** (stale review cleanup) -- fixes the blocking problem, adds `loadWingmanStateDirect` and `shouldSkipExistingReview`
3. **Change B** (remove 30s delay) -- simple removal, improves latency
4. **Change C** (stop hook auto-apply) -- the main new feature, depends on A and D
5. **Change E** (improved injection) -- backup mechanism, depends on D
6. **Tests** -- add tests throughout, but especially after C is complete

     1→//go:build unix
     2→
     3→package cli
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"context"
     7→	"io"
     8→	"os"
     9→	"os/exec"
    10→	"path/filepath"
    11→	"syscall"
    12→)
    13→
    14→// spawnDetachedWingmanReview spawns a detached subprocess to run the wingman review.
    15→// repoRoot is the repository root used to locate the log file.
    16→// payloadPath is the path to the JSON payload file.
    17→// On Unix, this uses process group detachment so the subprocess continues
    18→// after the parent exits.
    19→func spawnDetachedWingmanReview(repoRoot, payloadPath string) {
    20→	executable, err := os.Executable()
    21→	if err != nil {
    22→		return
    23→	}
    24→
    25→	//nolint:gosec // G204: payloadPath is controlled internally, not user input
    26→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), executable, "wingman", "__review", payloadPath)
    27→
    28→	// Detach from parent process group so subprocess survives parent exit
    29→	cmd.SysProcAttr = &syscall.SysProcAttr{
    30→		Setpgid: true,
    31→	}
    32→
    33→	// Don't hold the working directory
    34→	cmd.Dir = "/"
    35→
    36→	// Inherit environment (needed for PATH, git config, etc.)
    37→	cmd.Env = os.Environ()
    38→
    39→	// Redirect stderr to a log file for debugging the background process.
    40→	// This catches panics, errors, and all wingmanLog() output.
    41→	cmd.Stdout = io.Discard
    42→	logDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".entire", "logs")
    43→	if mkErr := os.MkdirAll(logDir, 0o750); mkErr == nil {
    44→		//nolint:gosec // G304: path is constructed from repoRoot + constants
    45→		if f, openErr := os.OpenFile(filepath.Join(logDir, "wingman.log"),
    46→			os.O_CREATE|os.O_APPEND|os.O_WRONLY, 0o600); openErr == nil {
    47→			cmd.Stderr = f
    48→			// f stays open in the child process; the OS closes it on exit
    49→		} else {
    50→			cmd.Stderr = io.Discard
    51→		}
    52→	} else {
    53→		cmd.Stderr = io.Discard
    54→	}
    55→
    56→	// Start the process (non-blocking)
    57→	if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
    58→		return
    59→	}
    60→
    61→	// Release the process so it can run independently
    62→	//nolint:errcheck // Best effort - process should continue regardless
    63→	_ = cmd.Process.Release()
    64→}
    65→

   225→	}
   226→}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/dip/.claude/plans/velvet-strolling-rose.md",
  "content": "# Wingman: Reliable Auto-Apply and Stale Review Cleanup\n\n## Context\n\nThe wingman auto-review feature has two reliability problems observed in the `entire-playground` repo:\n\n1. **Stale REVIEW.md blocks everything** - A review from a previous session persists indefinitely. The `os.Stat` check in `triggerWingmanReview()` blocks new reviews, and the same stale instruction gets re-injected on every prompt-submit.\n\n2. **Review never gets addressed** - The `systemMessage` injection on prompt-submit is unreliable: the agent deprioritizes it vs the user's actual request. Once ignored, REVIEW.md stays forever, blocking new reviews.\n\nThe fix: proactively trigger `claude --continue` to apply reviews instead of hoping the agent acts on a system message. Two delivery paths ensure \"always addressed\":\n- **Idle path**: review process triggers `--continue` immediately after writing REVIEW.md (no 30s wait)\n- **Active path**: stop hook detects pending REVIEW.md when turn ends, spawns detached auto-apply\n\n## Changes\n\n### A. Stale Review Cleanup (`wingman.go`)\n\nIn `triggerWingmanReview()`, replace the simple `os.Stat` check (lines 200-206) with session-aware logic:\n\n1. Add `loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot string) *WingmanState` helper (read counterpart of existing `saveWingmanStateDirect`)\n2. Add `shouldSkipPendingReview(repoRoot, currentSessionID string) bool`:\n   - No REVIEW.md → return false (don't skip)\n   - REVIEW.md exists + no state file → orphan, delete REVIEW.md, return false\n   - Same session ID → return true (skip, current session review is pending)\n   - Different session ID → delete stale REVIEW.md, return false\n   - Same session but older than 1 hour → delete stale REVIEW.md, return false\n3. Add constant `wingmanStaleReviewTTL = 1 * time.Hour`\n\n### B. Remove 30s Delay, Immediate Auto-Apply (`wingman_review.go`)\n\nIn `runWingmanReview()`:\n1. Remove `wingmanApplyDelay` constant and the `time.Sleep(wingmanApplyDelay)` (lines 181-183)\n2. Check `isSessionIdle()` immediately after writing REVIEW.md and saving state\n3. If idle → mark apply attempted, trigger `triggerAutoApply()` immediately\n4. If active → return nil (stop hook handles it via Change C)\n\n### C. Stop Hook Auto-Apply (`hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`, `wingman_review.go`, spawn files)\n\n**New `__apply` subcommand** (mirrors `__review` pattern):\n\n1. In `wingman.go`: add `newWingmanApplyCmd()` returning hidden `__apply` subcommand, register in `newWingmanCmd()`\n2. In `wingman_review.go`: add `runWingmanApply(repoRoot string) error`:\n   - Check REVIEW.md exists\n   - Check `ApplyAttemptedAt` not set (retry prevention)\n   - Mark `ApplyAttemptedAt` in state\n   - Call existing `triggerAutoApply(repoRoot)`\n\n**Detached spawner:**\n\n3. In `wingman_spawn_unix.go`: add `spawnDetachedWingmanApply(repoRoot string)` - same pattern as `spawnDetachedWingmanReview`, spawning `entire wingman __apply <repoRoot>`, logs to `wingman.log`\n4. In `wingman_spawn_other.go`: add no-op `spawnDetachedWingmanApply(_ string)`\n\n**Stop hook trigger:**\n\n5. In `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`, in `commitWithMetadata()`, AFTER `transitionSessionTurnEnd` (line 401) and AFTER the existing wingman review trigger (line 416), add:\n   - Guard: `settings.IsWingmanEnabled() && os.Getenv(\"ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY\") == \"\"`\n   - Check REVIEW.md exists\n   - Load state, check `ApplyAttemptedAt` is nil\n   - If all pass → `spawnDetachedWingmanApply(repoRoot)`\n\n### D. Retry Prevention (`wingman.go`)\n\n1. Add `ApplyAttemptedAt *time.Time `json:\"apply_attempted_at,omitempty\"`` to `WingmanState`\n2. When saving state after new review (in `runWingmanReview`), set `ApplyAttemptedAt: nil` (reset)\n3. Before triggering auto-apply (both paths), check `ApplyAttemptedAt != nil` → skip if already attempted\n4. Mark `ApplyAttemptedAt` before calling `triggerAutoApply`\n\n### E. Session-Aware Prompt-Submit Injection (`hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`)\n\nKeep as belt-and-suspenders backup, but smarten it (lines 79-91):\n- Load `wingman-state.json`, only inject if:\n  - `state.SessionID` matches current session\n  - `state.ApplyAttemptedAt` is nil (auto-apply not yet attempted)\n- Skip injection otherwise (stale review or already being handled)\n\n## Files to Modify\n\n| File | Changes |\n|------|---------|\n| `cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go` | `ApplyAttemptedAt` field, `shouldSkipPendingReview()`, `loadWingmanStateDirect()`, `newWingmanApplyCmd()`, `wingmanStaleReviewTTL` constant |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go` | Remove 30s delay, immediate idle check, `runWingmanApply()`, mark `ApplyAttemptedAt` |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_spawn_unix.go` | `spawnDetachedWingmanApply()` |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_spawn_other.go` | No-op `spawnDetachedWingmanApply()` |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` | Stop hook auto-apply trigger, session-aware prompt-submit injection |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_test.go` | Tests for `shouldSkipPendingReview`, `WingmanState` with `ApplyAttemptedAt` roundtrip |\n\n## Verification\n\n1. `mise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci`\n2. Manual test in `entire-playground`:\n   - Enable wingman, make agent produce changes, verify review generated\n   - While agent is active: verify stop hook triggers auto-apply when turn ends\n   - Start new session with stale REVIEW.md from old session: verify it gets cleaned up\n   - Verify `ApplyAttemptedAt` prevents infinite retry loops\n"
}

The user doesn't want to proceed with this tool use. The tool use was rejected (eg. if it was a file edit, the new_string was NOT written to the file). To tell you how to proceed, the user said:
One question remaining, what if the we do --continue but the user quickly send another prompt, will we just append?
```

> AGENT

Good question. Here's what happens with `claude --continue --print`:

It's a **separate process** with its own API session. It reads the conversation transcript, sends the wingman instruction as a new turn, gets a response, writes it to the transcript, and exits. The interactive terminal session doesn't know about it in real-time.

**If the user sends a prompt while auto-apply is running:**

Both processes would be writing to the same conversation transcript concurrently. The result depends on Claude Code's internal handling — the messages likely get appended in whatever order they complete. The user would see the auto-apply turn appear in their conversation history, potentially interleaved.

**Practical risk is low because:**
- The stop hook fires the instant the agent finishes a turn (IDLE transition). There's a natural pause before the user types the next prompt.
- The detached process spawn + `claude` startup adds a few seconds of overhead, which is still within that natural pause.
- But if the user types immediately after the agent finishes, there IS a race window.

**Possible mitigation:** Before triggering `--continue`, do a brief re-check of the session phase. If it's already ACTIVE again (user submitted a new prompt during our spawn delay), abort the auto-apply and let the prompt-submit injection handle it instead. This could be done in `runWingmanApply()`:

```go
// Re-check session phase before triggering (user may have typed)
if !isSessionIdle(sessionID) {
    wingmanLog("session became active, aborting auto-apply")
    return nil
}
```

Want me to add this guard to the plan, or are you OK with accepting the race window given the natural pause?

> DEVELOPER

Mh... the user makes a commit, that triggers the reviewer to look at things, which takes a while, then eventually, maybe, writes in the README.md only at this point we would --continue and that's long after the commit happened.

> AGENT

You're right, I was conflating the timing. Let me retrace the actual sequence:

1. Agent makes changes → user commits (or agent commits)
2. Post-commit hook fires → wingman spawns background process
3. Background: **10s delay + diff computation + Claude review call = 30-60+ seconds**
4. REVIEW.md written
5. **NOW** we want to `--continue`

By step 5, the user has very likely already sent their next prompt and the agent is deep into a new turn. The session is almost certainly ACTIVE, not IDLE.

So the idle path (immediate `--continue` from the review process) is the rare case. The common case is: REVIEW.md lands while the user is working, and we need the **stop hook** to pick it up when the current turn ends.

And the stop hook fires right at the IDLE transition — that's the safe moment before the user types again. The `--continue` from the stop hook is the primary delivery mechanism, not a fallback.

This also means the race concern is minimal: the stop hook fires at a natural break point, and the `--continue` spawns quickly.

Let me update the plan to reflect this correctly.

> TOOL

    64→1. Add `ApplyAttemptedAt *time.Time `json:"apply_attempted_at,omitempty"`` to `WingmanState`
    65→2. When saving state after new review (in `runWingmanReview`), set `ApplyAttemptedAt: nil` (reset)
    66→3. Before triggering auto-apply (both paths), check `ApplyAttemptedAt != nil` → skip if already attempted
    67→4. Mark `ApplyAttemptedAt` before calling `triggerAutoApply`

{
  "file_path": "/Users/dip/.claude/plans/velvet-strolling-rose.md",
  "content": "# Wingman: Reliable Auto-Apply and Stale Review Cleanup\n\n## Context\n\nThe wingman auto-review feature has two reliability problems observed in the `entire-playground` repo:\n\n1. **Stale REVIEW.md blocks everything** - A review from a previous session persists indefinitely. The `os.Stat` check in `triggerWingmanReview()` blocks new reviews, and the same stale instruction gets re-injected on every prompt-submit.\n\n2. **Review never gets addressed** - The `systemMessage` injection on prompt-submit is unreliable: the agent deprioritizes it vs the user's actual request. Once ignored, REVIEW.md stays forever, blocking new reviews.\n\n## Timing Reality\n\nThe review process takes 30-60+ seconds (10s settle + diff + Claude API call). By the time REVIEW.md is written, the user has almost always started a new prompt and the session is ACTIVE. The idle path (immediate `--continue` from review process) is the **rare** case.\n\nThe **primary delivery mechanism** is the stop hook: when the agent finishes whatever turn it's on, the session transitions to IDLE — that's the natural safe moment to trigger `--continue` before the user types again.\n\n```\nTurn 1 ends → post-commit hook → wingman spawns background review\n                                   ↓ (30-60s)\nUser sends prompt → Turn 2 → ... → REVIEW.md written (session ACTIVE)\n                                   ↓\nTurn 2 ends → stop hook → REVIEW.md exists → spawn auto-apply via --continue\n                                   ↓\nAuto-apply turn → agent reads REVIEW.md → applies suggestions → deletes REVIEW.md\n```\n\n## Changes\n\n### A. Stale Review Cleanup (`wingman.go`)\n\nIn `triggerWingmanReview()`, replace the simple `os.Stat` check (lines 200-206) with session-aware logic:\n\n1. Add `loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot string) *WingmanState` helper (read counterpart of existing `saveWingmanStateDirect`)\n2. Add `shouldSkipPendingReview(repoRoot, currentSessionID string) bool`:\n   - No REVIEW.md → return false (don't skip)\n   - REVIEW.md exists + no state file → orphan, delete REVIEW.md, return false\n   - Same session ID → return true (skip, current session review is pending)\n   - Different session ID → delete stale REVIEW.md, return false\n   - Same session but older than 1 hour → delete stale REVIEW.md, return false\n3. Add constant `wingmanStaleReviewTTL = 1 * time.Hour`\n\n### B. Remove 30s Delay in Review Process (`wingman_review.go`)\n\nIn `runWingmanReview()`:\n1. Remove `wingmanApplyDelay` constant and `time.Sleep(wingmanApplyDelay)` (lines 181-183)\n2. Check `isSessionIdle()` immediately after writing REVIEW.md and saving state\n3. If idle (rare — user hasn't typed yet) → mark apply attempted, trigger `triggerAutoApply()` immediately\n4. If active (common — user already in next turn) → return nil, stop hook handles it\n\n### C. Stop Hook Auto-Apply — Primary Delivery Path\n\nThis is the main mechanism that ensures the review is \"always addressed.\" Every agent turn ends with a stop hook → IDLE transition. At that moment we check for pending REVIEW.md and trigger auto-apply.\n\n**New `__apply` subcommand** (mirrors `__review` pattern):\n\n1. In `wingman.go`: add `newWingmanApplyCmd()` returning hidden `__apply <repoRoot>` subcommand, register in `newWingmanCmd()`\n2. In `wingman_review.go`: add `runWingmanApply(repoRoot string) error`:\n   - Check REVIEW.md exists\n   - Check `ApplyAttemptedAt` not set (retry prevention)\n   - Re-check session is still idle (guard against user typing during spawn delay)\n   - Mark `ApplyAttemptedAt` in state\n   - Call existing `triggerAutoApply(repoRoot)`\n\n**Detached spawner:**\n\n3. In `wingman_spawn_unix.go`: add `spawnDetachedWingmanApply(repoRoot string)` — same pattern as `spawnDetachedWingmanReview`, spawning `entire wingman __apply <repoRoot>`, logs to `wingman.log`\n4. In `wingman_spawn_other.go`: add no-op `spawnDetachedWingmanApply(_ string)`\n\n**Stop hook trigger:**\n\n5. In `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`, in `commitWithMetadata()`, AFTER `transitionSessionTurnEnd` (line 401) and AFTER the existing wingman review trigger (line 416), add:\n   - Guard: `settings.IsWingmanEnabled() && os.Getenv(\"ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY\") == \"\"`\n   - Check REVIEW.md exists\n   - Load state, check `ApplyAttemptedAt` is nil\n   - If all pass → `spawnDetachedWingmanApply(repoRoot)`\n\n**Race condition safety:** The `__apply` command re-checks `isSessionIdle()` before calling `triggerAutoApply()`. If the user typed a new prompt during the short spawn delay, it aborts. The next stop hook will retry.\n\n### D. Retry Prevention (`wingman.go`)\n\n1. Add `ApplyAttemptedAt *time.Time `json:\"apply_attempted_at,omitempty\"`` to `WingmanState`\n2. When saving state after new review (in `runWingmanReview`), set `ApplyAttemptedAt: nil` (reset)\n3. Before triggering auto-apply (both paths), check `ApplyAttemptedAt != nil` → skip if already attempted\n4. Mark `ApplyAttemptedAt` before calling `triggerAutoApply`\n\n### E. Session-Aware Prompt-Submit Injection (`hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`)\n\nKeep as belt-and-suspenders backup, but smarten it (lines 79-91):\n- Load `wingman-state.json`, only inject if:\n  - `state.SessionID` matches current session\n  - `state.ApplyAttemptedAt` is nil (auto-apply not yet attempted)\n- Skip injection otherwise (stale review or already being handled)\n\n## Files to Modify\n\n| File | Changes |\n|------|---------|\n| `cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go` | `ApplyAttemptedAt` field, `shouldSkipPendingReview()`, `loadWingmanStateDirect()`, `newWingmanApplyCmd()`, `wingmanStaleReviewTTL` constant |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go` | Remove 30s delay, immediate idle check, `runWingmanApply()`, mark `ApplyAttemptedAt` |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_spawn_unix.go` | `spawnDetachedWingmanApply()` |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_spawn_other.go` | No-op `spawnDetachedWingmanApply()` |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` | Stop hook auto-apply trigger, session-aware prompt-submit injection |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_test.go` | Tests for `shouldSkipPendingReview`, `WingmanState` with `ApplyAttemptedAt` roundtrip |\n\n## Verification\n\n1. `mise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci`\n2. Manual test in `entire-playground`:\n   - Enable wingman, make agent produce changes, verify review generated\n   - While agent is active: verify stop hook triggers auto-apply when turn ends\n   - Start new session with stale REVIEW.md from old session: verify it gets cleaned up\n   - Verify `ApplyAttemptedAt` prevents infinite retry loops\n"

## Approved Plan:
# Wingman: Reliable Auto-Apply and Stale Review Cleanup

## Context

The wingman auto-review feature has two reliability problems observed in the `entire-playground` repo:

1. **Stale REVIEW.md blocks everything** - A review from a previous session persists indefinitely. The `os.Stat` check in `triggerWingmanReview()` blocks new reviews, and the same stale instruction gets re-injected on every prompt-submit.

2. **Review never gets addressed** - The `systemMessage` injection on prompt-submit is unreliable: the agent deprioritizes it vs the user's actual request. Once ignored, REVIEW.md stays forever, blocking new reviews.

## Timing Reality

The review process takes 30-60+ seconds (10s settle + diff + Claude API call). By the time REVIEW.md is written, the user has almost always started a new prompt and the session is ACTIVE. The idle path (immediate `--continue` from review process) is the **rare** case.

The **primary delivery mechanism** is the stop hook: when the agent finishes whatever turn it's on, the session transitions to IDLE — that's the natural safe moment to trigger `--continue` before the user types again.

```
Turn 1 ends → post-commit hook → wingman spawns background review
                                   ↓ (30-60s)
User sends prompt → Turn 2 → ... → REVIEW.md written (session ACTIVE)
                                   ↓
Turn 2 ends → stop hook → REVIEW.md exists → spawn auto-apply via --continue
                                   ↓
Auto-apply turn → agent reads REVIEW.md → applies suggestions → deletes REVIEW.md
```

## Changes

### A. Stale Review Cleanup (`wingman.go`)

In `triggerWingmanReview()`, replace the simple `os.Stat` check (lines 200-206) with session-aware logic:

1. Add `loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot string) *WingmanState` helper (read counterpart of existing `saveWingmanStateDirect`)
2. Add `shouldSkipPendingReview(repoRoot, currentSessionID string) bool`:
   - No REVIEW.md → return false (don't skip)
   - REVIEW.md exists + no state file → orphan, delete REVIEW.md, return false
   - Same session ID → return true (skip, current session review is pending)
   - Different session ID → delete stale REVIEW.md, return false
   - Same session but older than 1 hour → delete stale REVIEW.md, return false
3. Add constant `wingmanStaleReviewTTL = 1 * time.Hour`

### B. Remove 30s Delay in Review Process (`wingman_review.go`)

In `runWingmanReview()`:
1. Remove `wingmanApplyDelay` constant and `time.Sleep(wingmanApplyDelay)` (lines 181-183)
2. Check `isSessionIdle()` immediately after writing REVIEW.md and saving state
3. If idle (rare — user hasn't typed yet) → mark apply attempted, trigger `triggerAutoApply()` immediately
4. If active (common — user already in next turn) → return nil, stop hook handles it

### C. Stop Hook Auto-Apply — Primary Delivery Path

This is the main mechanism that ensures the review is "always addressed." Every agent turn ends with a stop hook → IDLE transition. At that moment we check for pending REVIEW.md and trigger auto-apply.

**New `__apply` subcommand** (mirrors `__review` pattern):

1. In `wingman.go`: add `newWingmanApplyCmd()` returning hidden `__apply <repoRoot>` subcommand, register in `newWingmanCmd()`
2. In `wingman_review.go`: add `runWingmanApply(repoRoot string) error`:
   - Check REVIEW.md exists
   - Check `ApplyAttemptedAt` not set (retry prevention)
   - Re-check session is still idle (guard against user typing during spawn delay)
   - Mark `ApplyAttemptedAt` in state
   - Call existing `triggerAutoApply(repoRoot)`

**Detached spawner:**

3. In `wingman_spawn_unix.go`: add `spawnDetachedWingmanApply(repoRoot string)` — same pattern as `spawnDetachedWingmanReview`, spawning `entire wingman __apply <repoRoot>`, logs to `wingman.log`
4. In `wingman_spawn_other.go`: add no-op `spawnDetachedWingmanApply(_ string)`

**Stop hook trigger:**

5. In `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`, in `commitWithMetadata()`, AFTER `transitionSessionTurnEnd` (line 401) and AFTER the existing wingman review trigger (line 416), add:
   - Guard: `settings.IsWingmanEnabled() && os.Getenv("ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY") == ""`
   - Check REVIEW.md exists
   - Load state, check `ApplyAttemptedAt` is nil
   - If all pass → `spawnDetachedWingmanApply(repoRoot)`

**Race condition safety:** The `__apply` command re-checks `isSessionIdle()` before calling `triggerAutoApply()`. If the user typed a new prompt during the short spawn delay, it aborts. The next stop hook will retry.

### D. Retry Prevention (`wingman.go`)

1. Add `ApplyAttemptedAt *time.Time `json:"apply_attempted_at,omitempty"`` to `WingmanState`
2. When saving state after new review (in `runWingmanReview`), set `ApplyAttemptedAt: nil` (reset)
3. Before triggering auto-apply (both paths), check `ApplyAttemptedAt != nil` → skip if already attempted
4. Mark `ApplyAttemptedAt` before calling `triggerAutoApply`

### E. Session-Aware Prompt-Submit Injection (`hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`)

Keep as belt-and-suspenders backup, but smarten it (lines 79-91):
- Load `wingman-state.json`, only inject if:
  - `state.SessionID` matches current session
  - `state.ApplyAttemptedAt` is nil (auto-apply not yet attempted)
- Skip injection otherwise (stale review or already being handled)

| `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go` | Remove 30s delay, immediate idle check, `runWingmanApply()`, mark `ApplyAttemptedAt` |
| `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_spawn_unix.go` | `spawnDetachedWingmanApply()` |
| `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_spawn_other.go` | No-op `spawnDetachedWingmanApply()` |
| `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` | Stop hook auto-apply trigger, session-aware prompt-submit injection |
| `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_test.go` | Tests for `shouldSkipPendingReview`, `WingmanState` with `ApplyAttemptedAt` roundtrip |

   - While agent is active: verify stop hook triggers auto-apply when turn ends
   - Start new session with stale REVIEW.md from old session: verify it gets cleaned up
   - Verify `ApplyAttemptedAt` prevents infinite retry loops

   397→func computeFilesHash(files []string) string {
   398→	sorted := make([]string, len(files))
   399→	copy(sorted, files)
   400→	sort.Strings(sorted)
   401→	h := sha256.Sum256([]byte(strings.Join(sorted, "\x00")))
   402→	return hex.EncodeToString(h[:])

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go",
  "old_string": "\t//nolint:gosec // G306: state file is config, not secrets\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(statePath, data, 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"writing wingman state: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}",
  "new_string": "\t//nolint:gosec // G306: state file is config, not secrets\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(statePath, data, 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"writing wingman state: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc newWingmanApplyCmd() *cobra.Command {\n\treturn &cobra.Command{\n\t\tUse:    \"__apply\",\n\t\tHidden: true,\n\t\tArgs:   cobra.ExactArgs(1),\n\t\tRunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, args []string) error {\n\t\t\treturn runWingmanApply(args[0])\n\t\t},\n\t}\n}\n\n// shouldSkipPendingReview checks whether a pending REVIEW.md should block a new\n// review. Returns true if the review is from the current session and still fresh.\n// Deletes stale reviews (different session or older than TTL) and returns false.\nfunc shouldSkipPendingReview(repoRoot, currentSessionID string) bool {\n\treviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)\n\tif _, err := os.Stat(reviewPath); err != nil {\n\t\treturn false // No REVIEW.md exists\n\t}\n\n\t// Load state to check ownership\n\tstate := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)\n\tif state == nil {\n\t\t// REVIEW.md exists but no state file — orphan, clean up\n\t\t_ = os.Remove(reviewPath)\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\n\t// Different session — stale review from a previous session\n\tif state.SessionID != currentSessionID {\n\t\t_ = os.Remove(reviewPath)\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\n\t// Same session but older than TTL — stale\n\tif time.Since(state.ReviewedAt) > wingmanStaleReviewTTL {\n\t\t_ = os.Remove(reviewPath)\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\n\t// Same session, still fresh — skip (don't overwrite)\n\treturn true\n}"
}

   197→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "wingman")
   198→	repoRoot := payload.RepoRoot
   199→
   200→	totalFiles := len(payload.ModifiedFiles) + len(payload.NewFiles) + len(payload.DeletedFiles)
   201→	logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman trigger evaluating",
   202→		slog.String("session_id", payload.SessionID),
   203→		slog.Int("file_count", totalFiles),
   204→	)
   205→
   206→	// Check if a pending REVIEW.md already exists
   207→	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
   208→	if _, err := os.Stat(reviewPath); err == nil {
   209→		logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman skipped: pending review exists")
   210→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Pending review exists, skipping\n")
   211→		return
   212→	}
   213→
   214→	// Atomic lock file prevents concurrent review spawns. O_CREATE|O_EXCL
```

   337→// isSessionIdle checks if the given session is in the IDLE phase.
   338→func isSessionIdle(sessionID string) bool {
   339→	state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   340→	if err != nil || state == nil {
   341→		return false
   342→	}
   343→	return state.Phase == session.PhaseIdle
   344→}
   345→
   346→// triggerAutoApply spawns claude --continue to apply the review suggestions.
   347→func triggerAutoApply(repoRoot string) error {
   348→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), wingmanApplyTimeout)
   349→	defer cancel()
   350→
   351→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "claude",
   352→		"--continue",
   353→		"--print",
   354→		"--setting-sources", "",
   355→		// Auto-accept edits so the agent can modify files and delete REVIEW.md
   356→		// without requiring user consent (this runs in a background process).
   357→		"--permission-mode", "acceptEdits",
   358→		wingmanApplyInstruction,
   359→	)
   360→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   361→	// Strip GIT_* env vars to prevent hook interference, and set
   362→	// ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY=1 so git hooks (post-commit) know not to
   363→	// trigger another wingman review (prevents infinite recursion).
   364→	env := wingmanStripGitEnv(os.Environ())
   365→	env = append(env, "ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY=1")
   366→	cmd.Env = env
   367→
   368→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   369→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   370→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   371→
   372→	return cmd.Run() //nolint:wrapcheck // caller wraps
   373→}
   374→
   375→// wingmanStripGitEnv returns a copy of env with all GIT_* variables removed.
   376→// This prevents a subprocess from discovering or modifying the parent's git repo
   377→// when we want isolation (e.g., running claude --print for review).
   378→func wingmanStripGitEnv(env []string) []string {
   379→	filtered := make([]string, 0, len(env))
   380→	for _, e := range env {
   381→		if !strings.HasPrefix(e, "GIT_") {
   382→			filtered = append(filtered, e)
   383→		}
   384→	}
   385→	return filtered
   386→}
   387→
   388→// saveWingmanStateDirect writes the wingman state file directly to a known path
   389→// under repoRoot, avoiding os.Chdir (which mutates process-wide state).
   390→func saveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot string, state *WingmanState) {
   391→	statePath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanStateFile)
   392→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(statePath), 0o750); err != nil {
   393→		return
   394→	}
   395→	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(state, "", "  ")
   396→	if err != nil {
   397→		return
   398→	}
   399→	//nolint:gosec,errcheck // G306: state file is config, not secrets; best-effort write
   400→	_ = os.WriteFile(statePath, data, 0o644)
   401→}

  "new_string": "// runWingmanApply is the entrypoint for the __apply subcommand, spawned by the\n// stop hook when a pending REVIEW.md is detected. It re-checks preconditions\n// and triggers claude --continue to apply the review.\nfunc runWingmanApply(repoRoot string) error {\n\twingmanLog(\"apply process started (pid=%d)\", os.Getpid())\n\n\treviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)\n\tif _, err := os.Stat(reviewPath); err != nil {\n\t\twingmanLog(\"no REVIEW.md found, nothing to apply\")\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Retry prevention: check if apply was already attempted for this review\n\tstate := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)\n\tif state != nil && state.ApplyAttemptedAt != nil {\n\t\twingmanLog(\"apply already attempted at %s, skipping\", state.ApplyAttemptedAt.Format(time.RFC3339))\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Re-check session is still idle (user may have typed during spawn delay)\n\tif state != nil && state.SessionID != \"\" {\n\t\tif !isSessionIdle(state.SessionID) {\n\t\t\twingmanLog(\"session became active during spawn, aborting (next stop hook will retry)\")\n\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Mark apply as attempted BEFORE triggering\n\tif state != nil {\n\t\tnow := time.Now()\n\t\tstate.ApplyAttemptedAt = &now\n\t\tsaveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot, state)\n\t}\n\n\twingmanLog(\"triggering auto-apply via claude --continue\")\n\tapplyStart := time.Now()\n\tif err := triggerAutoApply(repoRoot); err != nil {\n\t\twingmanLog(\"ERROR auto-apply failed after %s: %v\", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond), err)\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to trigger auto-apply: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\twingmanLog(\"auto-apply completed in %s\", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond))\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// isSessionIdle checks if the given session is in the IDLE phase.\nfunc isSessionIdle(sessionID string) bool {"
}

     1→//go:build unix
     2→
     3→package cli
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"context"
     7→	"io"
     8→	"os"
     9→	"os/exec"
    10→	"path/filepath"
    11→	"syscall"
    12→)
    13→
    14→// spawnDetachedWingmanReview spawns a detached subprocess to run the wingman review.
    15→// repoRoot is the repository root used to locate the log file.
    16→// payloadPath is the path to the JSON payload file.
    17→// On Unix, this uses process group detachment so the subprocess continues
    18→// after the parent exits.
    19→func spawnDetachedWingmanReview(repoRoot, payloadPath string) {
    20→	executable, err := os.Executable()
    21→	if err != nil {
    22→		return
    23→	}
    24→
    25→	//nolint:gosec // G204: payloadPath is controlled internally, not user input
    26→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), executable, "wingman", "__review", payloadPath)
    27→
    28→	// Detach from parent process group so subprocess survives parent exit
    29→	cmd.SysProcAttr = &syscall.SysProcAttr{
    30→		Setpgid: true,
    31→	}
    32→
    33→	// Don't hold the working directory
    34→	cmd.Dir = "/"
    35→
    36→	// Inherit environment (needed for PATH, git config, etc.)
    37→	cmd.Env = os.Environ()
    38→
    39→	// Redirect stderr to a log file for debugging the background process.
    40→	// This catches panics, errors, and all wingmanLog() output.
    41→	cmd.Stdout = io.Discard
    42→	logDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".entire", "logs")
    43→	if mkErr := os.MkdirAll(logDir, 0o750); mkErr == nil {
    44→		//nolint:gosec // G304: path is constructed from repoRoot + constants
    45→		if f, openErr := os.OpenFile(filepath.Join(logDir, "wingman.log"),
    46→			os.O_CREATE|os.O_APPEND|os.O_WRONLY, 0o600); openErr == nil {
    47→			cmd.Stderr = f
    48→			// f stays open in the child process; the OS closes it on exit
    49→		} else {
    50→			cmd.Stderr = io.Discard
    51→		}
    52→	} else {
    53→		cmd.Stderr = io.Discard
    54→	}
    55→
    56→	// Start the process (non-blocking)
    57→	if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
    58→		return
    59→	}
    60→
    61→	// Release the process so it can run independently
    62→	//nolint:errcheck // Best effort - process should continue regardless
    63→	_ = cmd.Process.Release()
    64→}
    65→

  "new_string": "\t// Release the process so it can run independently\n\t//nolint:errcheck // Best effort - process should continue regardless\n\t_ = cmd.Process.Release()\n}\n\n// spawnDetachedWingmanApply spawns a detached subprocess to auto-apply the\n// pending REVIEW.md via claude --continue. Called from the stop hook when a\n// review is pending after the agent turn ends.\nfunc spawnDetachedWingmanApply(repoRoot string) {\n\texecutable, err := os.Executable()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t//nolint:gosec // G204: repoRoot is from paths.RepoRoot(), not user input\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), executable, \"wingman\", \"__apply\", repoRoot)\n\n\tcmd.SysProcAttr = &syscall.SysProcAttr{\n\t\tSetpgid: true,\n\t}\n\n\tcmd.Dir = \"/\"\n\tcmd.Env = os.Environ()\n\n\tcmd.Stdout = io.Discard\n\tlogDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, \".entire\", \"logs\")\n\tif mkErr := os.MkdirAll(logDir, 0o750); mkErr == nil {\n\t\t//nolint:gosec // G304: path is constructed from repoRoot + constants\n\t\tif f, openErr := os.OpenFile(filepath.Join(logDir, \"wingman.log\"),\n\t\t\tos.O_CREATE|os.O_APPEND|os.O_WRONLY, 0o600); openErr == nil {\n\t\t\tcmd.Stderr = f\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tcmd.Stderr = io.Discard\n\t\t}\n\t} else {\n\t\tcmd.Stderr = io.Discard\n\t}\n\n\tif err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t//nolint:errcheck // Best effort - process should continue regardless\n\t_ = cmd.Process.Release()\n}"
}

    66→// captureInitialState captures the initial state on user prompt submit.
    67→func captureInitialState() error {
    68→	// Parse hook input and setup logging
    69→	hookData, err := parseAndLogHookInput()
    70→	if err != nil {
    71→		return err
    72→	}
    73→
    74→	// CLI captures state directly (including transcript position)
    75→	if err := CapturePrePromptState(hookData.sessionID, hookData.input.SessionRef); err != nil {
    76→		return err
    77→	}
    78→
    79→	// If a wingman review is pending, inject instruction into the agent's context
    80→	// so it automatically reads and applies the suggestions.
    81→	if settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {
    82→		repoRoot, rootErr := paths.RepoRoot()
    83→		if rootErr == nil {
    84→			if _, statErr := os.Stat(filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)); statErr == nil {
    85→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review available: .entire/REVIEW.md\n")
    86→				if err := outputHookResponse(wingmanApplyInstruction); err != nil {
    87→					fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Warning: failed to inject review instruction: %v\n", err)
    88→				}
    89→			}
    90→		}
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	// If strategy implements SessionInitializer, call it to initialize session state
    94→	strat := GetStrategy()
    95→	if initializer, ok := strat.(strategy.SessionInitializer); ok {
    96→		agentType := hookData.agent.Type()
    97→		if err := initializer.InitializeSession(hookData.sessionID, agentType, hookData.input.SessionRef, hookData.input.UserPrompt); err != nil {
    98→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to initialize session state: %v\n", err)
    99→		}
   100→	}
   101→
   102→	return nil
   103→}

  "new_string": "\t// If a wingman review is pending, inject instruction into the agent's context.\n\t// This is a backup mechanism — the primary path is auto-apply via the stop hook.\n\t// Only inject if the review is from the current session and auto-apply hasn't\n\t// been attempted yet (avoids re-injecting stale or already-handled reviews).\n\tif settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {\n\t\trepoRoot, rootErr := paths.RepoRoot()\n\t\tif rootErr == nil {\n\t\t\tif _, statErr := os.Stat(filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)); statErr == nil {\n\t\t\t\twingmanState := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)\n\t\t\t\tshouldInject := wingmanState != nil &&\n\t\t\t\t\twingmanState.SessionID == hookData.sessionID &&\n\t\t\t\t\twingmanState.ApplyAttemptedAt == nil\n\t\t\t\tif shouldInject {\n\t\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Review available: .entire/REVIEW.md\\n\")\n\t\t\t\t\tif err := outputHookResponse(wingmanApplyInstruction); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Warning: failed to inject review instruction: %v\\n\", err)\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}"
}

   160→			state, err := loadWingmanState()

   163→				if state.ReviewApplied {
   164→					fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Status: applied")
   165→				} else {

   167→				}

   170→			// Check for pending REVIEW.md

   174→					fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Pending review: .entire/REVIEW.md")
   175→				}

   182→
   183→// triggerWingmanReview checks preconditions and spawns the detached review process.
   184→func triggerWingmanReview(payload WingmanPayload) {
   185→	// Prevent infinite recursion: if we're inside a wingman auto-apply,
   186→	// don't trigger another review. The env var is set by triggerAutoApply.
   187→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY") != "" {
   188→		return
   189→	}
   190→
   191→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "wingman")
   192→	repoRoot := payload.RepoRoot
   193→
   194→	totalFiles := len(payload.ModifiedFiles) + len(payload.NewFiles) + len(payload.DeletedFiles)
   195→	logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman trigger evaluating",
   196→		slog.String("session_id", payload.SessionID),
   197→		slog.Int("file_count", totalFiles),
   198→	)
   199→
   200→	// Check if a pending REVIEW.md already exists
   201→	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
   202→	if _, err := os.Stat(reviewPath); err == nil {
   203→		logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman skipped: pending review exists")
   204→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Pending review exists, skipping\n")
   205→		return
   206→	}
   207→
   208→	// Atomic lock file prevents concurrent review spawns. O_CREATE|O_EXCL
   209→	// is atomic on all platforms, avoiding the TOCTOU race of Stat+WriteFile.
   210→	lockPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanLockFile)
   211→	if !acquireWingmanLock(lockPath, payload.SessionID) {
   212→		logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman skipped: could not acquire lock")
   213→		return
   214→	}
   215→
   216→	// Dedup check: compute hash of sorted file paths
   217→	allFiles := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))
   218→	allFiles = append(allFiles, payload.ModifiedFiles...)
   219→	allFiles = append(allFiles, payload.NewFiles...)
   220→	allFiles = append(allFiles, payload.DeletedFiles...)
   221→	filesHash := computeFilesHash(allFiles)
   222→
   223→	state, _ := loadWingmanState() //nolint:errcheck // best-effort dedup
   224→	if state != nil && state.FilesHash == filesHash && state.SessionID == payload.SessionID {
   225→		logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman skipped: dedup hash match",
   226→			slog.String("files_hash", filesHash[:12]),
   227→		)
   228→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Already reviewed these changes, skipping\n")
   229→		return
   230→	}
   231→
   232→	// Capture HEAD at trigger time so the detached review diffs against
   233→	// the correct commit even if HEAD moves during the initial delay.
   234→	payload.BaseCommit = resolveHEAD(repoRoot)
   235→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman captured base commit",
   236→		slog.String("base_commit", payload.BaseCommit),
   237→	)
   238→
   239→	// Write payload to a temp file instead of passing as a CLI argument,
   240→	// which can exceed OS argv limits (~128KB Linux, ~256KB macOS) with
   241→	// many files or long prompts.
   242→	payloadJSON, err := json.Marshal(payload)
   243→	if err != nil {
   244→		logging.Error(logCtx, "wingman failed to marshal payload", slog.Any("error", err))
   245→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to marshal payload: %v\n", err)
   246→		_ = os.Remove(lockPath)
   247→		return
   248→	}
   249→	payloadPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".entire", "wingman-payload.json")
   250→	//nolint:gosec // G306: payload file is not secrets
   251→	if err := os.WriteFile(payloadPath, payloadJSON, 0o644); err != nil {
   252→		logging.Error(logCtx, "wingman failed to write payload file", slog.Any("error", err))
   253→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to write payload file: %v\n", err)
   254→		_ = os.Remove(lockPath)
   255→		return
   256→	}
   257→
   258→	// Spawn detached review process with path to payload file
   259→	spawnDetachedWingmanReview(repoRoot, payloadPath)
   260→	logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman review spawned",
   261→		slog.String("session_id", payload.SessionID),
   262→		slog.String("base_commit", payload.BaseCommit),
   263→		slog.Int("file_count", totalFiles),
   264→	)
   265→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review starting in background...\n")
   266→}
   267→
   268→// triggerWingmanFromCommit builds a wingman payload from the HEAD commit and
   269→// triggers a review. Used by the git post-commit hook for manual-commit strategy
   270→// where files are committed by the user (not by SaveChanges).
   271→func triggerWingmanFromCommit() {
   272→	// Prevent infinite recursion: skip if inside wingman auto-apply
   273→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY") != "" {
   274→		return
   275→	}
   276→	if !settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {
   277→		return
   278→	}
   279→
   280→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   281→	if err != nil {
   282→		return
   283→	}
   284→
   285→	head := resolveHEAD(repoRoot)
   286→	if head == "" {
   287→		return
   288→	}
   289→
   290→	// Get changed files from the commit
   291→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 30*time.Second)
   292→	defer cancel()
   293→	//nolint:gosec // G204: head is from git rev-parse, not user input
   294→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "diff-tree", "--no-commit-id", "--name-status", "-r", head)
   295→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   296→	out, err := cmd.Output()
   297→	if err != nil || len(out) == 0 {
   298→		return
   299→	}
   300→
   301→	var modified, newFiles, deleted []string
   302→	for _, line := range strings.Split(strings.TrimSpace(string(out)), "\n") {
   303→		if len(line) < 3 {
   304→			continue
   305→		}
   306→		status := line[0]
   307→		file := strings.TrimSpace(line[1:])
   308→		switch status {
   309→		case 'M':
   310→			modified = append(modified, file)
   311→		case 'A':
   312→			newFiles = append(newFiles, file)
   313→		case 'D':
   314→			deleted = append(deleted, file)
   315→		}
   316→	}
   317→
   318→	if len(modified)+len(newFiles)+len(deleted) == 0 {
   319→		return
   320→	}
   321→
   322→	// Get commit message
   323→	//nolint:gosec // G204: head is from git rev-parse, not user input
   324→	msgCmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "log", "-1", "--format=%B", head)
   325→	msgCmd.Dir = repoRoot
   326→	msgOut, _ := msgCmd.Output() //nolint:errcheck // best-effort commit message
   327→	commitMessage := strings.TrimSpace(string(msgOut))
   328→
   329→	sessionID := strategy.FindMostRecentSession()
   330→
   331→	triggerWingmanReview(WingmanPayload{
   332→		SessionID:     sessionID,
   333→		RepoRoot:      repoRoot,
   334→		ModifiedFiles: modified,
   335→		NewFiles:      newFiles,
   336→		DeletedFiles:  deleted,
   337→		CommitMessage: commitMessage,
   338→	})
   339→}
   340→
   341→// staleLockThreshold is how old a lock file can be before we consider it stale
   342→// (e.g., the detached process was SIGKILLed and the defer never ran).
   343→const staleLockThreshold = 30 * time.Minute
   344→
   345→// acquireWingmanLock atomically creates the lock file. Returns true if acquired.
   346→// If the lock already exists but is older than staleLockThreshold, it is removed
   347→// and re-acquired (handles crashed detached processes).
   348→func acquireWingmanLock(lockPath, sessionID string) bool {
   349→	//nolint:gosec // G304: lockPath is constructed from repoRoot + constant
   350→	f, err := os.OpenFile(lockPath, os.O_WRONLY|os.O_CREATE|os.O_EXCL, 0o644)
   351→	if err != nil {
   352→		if !errors.Is(err, os.ErrExist) {
   353→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to create lock: %v\n", err)
   354→			return false
   355→		}
   356→		// Lock exists — check if it's stale
   357→		info, statErr := os.Stat(lockPath)
   358→		if statErr != nil || time.Since(info.ModTime()) <= staleLockThreshold {
   359→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review already in progress, skipping\n")
   360→			return false
   361→		}
   362→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Removing stale lock (age: %s)\n",
   363→			time.Since(info.ModTime()).Round(time.Second))
   364→		_ = os.Remove(lockPath)
   365→		// Retry the atomic create
   366→		//nolint:gosec // G304: lockPath is constructed from repoRoot + constant
   367→		f, err = os.OpenFile(lockPath, os.O_WRONLY|os.O_CREATE|os.O_EXCL, 0o644)
   368→		if err != nil {
   369→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to create lock after stale removal: %v\n", err)
   370→			return false
   371→		}
   372→	}
   373→	_, _ = f.WriteString(sessionID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort session ID write
   374→	_ = f.Close()
   375→	return true
   376→}
   377→
   378→// resolveHEAD returns the current HEAD commit hash, or empty string on error.
   379→func resolveHEAD(repoRoot string) string {
   380→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), "git", "rev-parse", "HEAD")
   381→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   382→	out, err := cmd.Output()
   383→	if err != nil {
   384→		return ""
   385→	}
   386→	return strings.TrimSpace(string(out))
   387→}
   388→
   389→// computeFilesHash returns a SHA256 hex digest of the sorted file paths.
   390→// Uses null byte separator (impossible in filenames) to avoid ambiguity.
   391→func computeFilesHash(files []string) string {
   392→	sorted := make([]string, len(files))
   393→	copy(sorted, files)
   394→	sort.Strings(sorted)
   395→	h := sha256.Sum256([]byte(strings.Join(sorted, "\x00")))
   396→	return hex.EncodeToString(h[:])
   397→}
   398→
   399→// loadWingmanState loads the wingman state from .entire/wingman-state.json.
   400→func loadWingmanState() (*WingmanState, error) {
   401→	statePath, err := paths.AbsPath(wingmanStateFile)
   402→	if err != nil {
   403→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("resolving wingman state path: %w", err)
   404→	}
   405→
   406→	data, err := os.ReadFile(statePath) //nolint:gosec // path is repo-relative
   407→	if err != nil {
   408→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("reading wingman state: %w", err)
   409→	}
   410→
   411→	var state WingmanState
   412→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &state); err != nil {
   413→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("parsing wingman state: %w", err)
   414→	}
   415→	return &state, nil
   416→}
   417→
   418→// saveWingmanState saves the wingman state to .entire/wingman-state.json.
   419→func saveWingmanState(state *WingmanState) error {
   420→	statePath, err := paths.AbsPath(wingmanStateFile)
   421→	if err != nil {
   422→		return fmt.Errorf("resolving wingman state path: %w", err)
   423→	}
   424→
   425→	dir := filepath.Dir(statePath)
   426→	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o750); err != nil {
   427→		return fmt.Errorf("creating wingman state directory: %w", err)
   428→	}
   429→
   430→	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(state, "", "  ")
   431→	if err != nil {
   432→		return fmt.Errorf("marshaling wingman state: %w", err)
   433→	}
   434→
   435→	//nolint:gosec // G306: state file is config, not secrets
   436→	if err := os.WriteFile(statePath, data, 0o644); err != nil {
   437→		return fmt.Errorf("writing wingman state: %w", err)
   438→	}
   439→	return nil
   440→}

    65→func runWingmanReview(payloadPath string) error {
    66→	wingmanLog("review process started (pid=%d)", os.Getpid())
    67→	wingmanLog("reading payload from %s", payloadPath)
    68→
    69→	// Read payload from file (avoids OS argv limits with large payloads)
    70→	payloadJSON, err := os.ReadFile(payloadPath) //nolint:gosec // path is from our own spawn
    71→	if err != nil {
    72→		wingmanLog("ERROR reading payload: %v", err)
    73→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read payload file: %w", err)
    74→	}
    75→	// Clean up payload file after reading
    76→	_ = os.Remove(payloadPath)
    77→
    78→	var payload WingmanPayload
    79→	if err := json.Unmarshal(payloadJSON, &payload); err != nil {
    80→		wingmanLog("ERROR unmarshaling payload: %v", err)
    81→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal payload: %w", err)
    82→	}
    83→
    84→	repoRoot := payload.RepoRoot
    85→	if repoRoot == "" {
    86→		wingmanLog("ERROR repo_root is empty in payload")
    87→		return errors.New("repo_root is required in payload")
    88→	}
    89→
    90→	totalFiles := len(payload.ModifiedFiles) + len(payload.NewFiles) + len(payload.DeletedFiles)
    91→	wingmanLog("session=%s repo=%s base_commit=%s files=%d",
    92→		payload.SessionID, repoRoot, payload.BaseCommit, totalFiles)
    93→
    94→	// Clean up lock file when review completes (regardless of success/failure)
    95→	lockPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanLockFile)
    96→	defer func() {
    97→		_ = os.Remove(lockPath)
    98→		wingmanLog("lock file removed")
    99→	}()
   100→
   101→	// Initial delay: let the agent turn fully settle
   102→	wingmanLog("waiting %s for agent turn to settle", wingmanInitialDelay)
   103→	time.Sleep(wingmanInitialDelay)
   104→
   105→	// Compute diff using the base commit captured at trigger time
   106→	wingmanLog("computing diff against %s", payload.BaseCommit)
   107→	diffStart := time.Now()
   108→	diff, err := computeDiff(repoRoot, payload.BaseCommit)
   109→	if err != nil {
   110→		wingmanLog("ERROR computing diff: %v", err)
   111→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to compute diff: %w", err)
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	if strings.TrimSpace(diff) == "" {
   115→		wingmanLog("no changes found in diff, exiting")
   116→		return nil // No changes to review
   117→	}
   118→	wingmanLog("diff computed: %d bytes in %s", len(diff), time.Since(diffStart).Round(time.Millisecond))
   119→
   120→	// Build file list for the prompt
   121→	allFiles := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))
   122→	for _, f := range payload.ModifiedFiles {
   123→		allFiles = append(allFiles, f+" (modified)")
   124→	}
   125→	for _, f := range payload.NewFiles {
   126→		allFiles = append(allFiles, f+" (new)")
   127→	}
   128→	for _, f := range payload.DeletedFiles {
   129→		allFiles = append(allFiles, f+" (deleted)")
   130→	}
   131→	fileList := strings.Join(allFiles, ", ")
   132→
   133→	// Read session context from checkpoint data (best-effort)
   134→	sessionContext := readSessionContext(repoRoot, payload.SessionID)
   135→	if sessionContext != "" {
   136→		wingmanLog("session context loaded: %d bytes", len(sessionContext))
   137→	}
   138→
   139→	// Build review prompt
   140→	prompt := buildReviewPrompt(payload.Prompts, payload.CommitMessage, sessionContext, payload.SessionID, fileList, diff)
   141→	wingmanLog("review prompt built: %d bytes", len(prompt))
   142→
   143→	// Call Claude CLI for review
   144→	wingmanLog("calling claude CLI (model=%s, timeout=%s)", wingmanReviewModel, wingmanReviewTimeout)
   145→	reviewStart := time.Now()
   146→	reviewText, err := callClaudeForReview(repoRoot, prompt)
   147→	if err != nil {
   148→		wingmanLog("ERROR claude CLI failed after %s: %v", time.Since(reviewStart).Round(time.Millisecond), err)
   149→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get review from Claude: %w", err)
   150→	}
   151→	wingmanLog("review received: %d bytes in %s", len(reviewText), time.Since(reviewStart).Round(time.Millisecond))
   152→
   153→	// Write REVIEW.md
   154→	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
   155→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(reviewPath), 0o750); err != nil {
   156→		wingmanLog("ERROR creating directory: %v", err)
   157→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create directory: %w", err)
   158→	}
   159→	//nolint:gosec // G306: review file is not secrets

   161→		wingmanLog("ERROR writing REVIEW.md: %v", err)
   162→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write REVIEW.md: %w", err)

   166→	// Update dedup state — write directly to known path instead of using

   169→	allFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.ModifiedFiles...)

   171→	allFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.DeletedFiles...)

   173→	saveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot, &WingmanState{

   190→		return nil // User is active, leave REVIEW.md for notification

   193→	// Trigger auto-apply via claude --continue

   195→	applyStart := time.Now()
