   197→		wingmanLog("ERROR auto-apply failed after %s: %v", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond), err)
   198→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to trigger auto-apply: %w", err)
   199→	}
   200→	wingmanLog("auto-apply completed in %s", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond))
   201→
   202→	return nil
   203→}
   204→
   205→// computeDiff gets the full branch diff for review. It diffs the current HEAD
   206→// against the merge base with the default branch (main/master), giving the
   207→// reviewer a holistic view of all branch changes rather than just one commit.
   208→func computeDiff(repoRoot string, _ string) (string, error) {
   209→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), wingmanGitTimeout)
   210→	defer cancel()
   211→
   212→	// Find the merge base with the default branch for a holistic branch diff.
   213→	mergeBase := findMergeBase(ctx, repoRoot)
   214→	if mergeBase != "" {
   215→		wingmanLog("using merge-base %s for branch diff", mergeBase)
   216→		diff, err := gitDiff(ctx, repoRoot, mergeBase)
   217→		if err == nil && strings.TrimSpace(diff) != "" {
   218→			return diff, nil
   219→		}
   220→		// Fall through to HEAD diff if merge-base diff fails or is empty
   221→	}
   222→
   223→	// Fallback: diff uncommitted changes against HEAD
   224→	wingmanLog("falling back to HEAD diff")
   225→	diff, err := gitDiff(ctx, repoRoot, "HEAD")
   226→	if err != nil {
   227→		return "", fmt.Errorf("git diff failed: %w", err)
   228→	}
   229→
   230→	// If no uncommitted changes, try the latest commit itself
   231→	if strings.TrimSpace(diff) == "" {
   232→		diff, err = gitDiff(ctx, repoRoot, "HEAD~1", "HEAD")
   233→		if err != nil {
   234→			return "", nil //nolint:nilerr // no diff is not an error
   235→		}
   236→	}
   237→
   238→	return diff, nil
   239→}
   240→
   241→// findMergeBase returns the merge-base commit between HEAD and the default
   242→// branch (tries main, then master). Returns empty string if not found.
   243→func findMergeBase(ctx context.Context, repoRoot string) string {
   244→	for _, branch := range []string{"main", "master"} {
   245→		cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "merge-base", branch, "HEAD")
   246→		cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   247→		out, err := cmd.Output()
   248→		if err == nil {
   249→			return strings.TrimSpace(string(out))
   250→		}
   251→	}
   252→	return ""
   253→}
   254→
   255→// gitDiff runs git diff with the given args and returns stdout.
   256→func gitDiff(ctx context.Context, repoRoot string, args ...string) (string, error) {
   257→	fullArgs := append([]string{"diff"}, args...)
   258→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", fullArgs...) //nolint:gosec // args are from internal logic
   259→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   260→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   261→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   262→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   263→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   264→		return "", fmt.Errorf("git diff %v: %w (stderr: %s)", args, err, stderr.String())
   265→	}
   266→	return stdout.String(), nil
   267→}
   268→
   269→// callClaudeForReview calls the claude CLI to perform the review.
   270→// repoRoot is the repository root so the reviewer can access the full codebase.
   271→func callClaudeForReview(repoRoot, prompt string) (string, error) {
   272→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), wingmanReviewTimeout)
   273→	defer cancel()
   274→
   275→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "claude",
   276→		"--print",
   277→		"--output-format", "json",
   278→		"--model", wingmanReviewModel,
   279→		"--setting-sources", "",
   280→		// Grant read-only tool access so the reviewer can inspect source files
   281→		// beyond just the diff. Permission bypass is safe here since tools are
   282→		// restricted to read-only operations.
   283→		"--allowedTools", "Read,Glob,Grep",
   284→		"--permission-mode", "bypassPermissions",
   285→	)
   286→
   287→	// Run from repo root so the reviewer can read source files for context.
   288→	// Loop-breaking is handled by --setting-sources "" (disables hooks) and
   289→	// wingmanStripGitEnv (prevents git index pollution).
   290→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   291→	cmd.Env = wingmanStripGitEnv(os.Environ())
   292→
   293→	cmd.Stdin = strings.NewReader(prompt)
   294→
   295→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   296→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   297→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   298→
   299→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   300→		var execErr *exec.Error
   301→		if errors.As(err, &execErr) {
   302→			return "", fmt.Errorf("claude CLI not found: %w", err)
   303→		}
   304→		var exitErr *exec.ExitError
   305→		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
   306→			return "", fmt.Errorf("claude CLI failed (exit %d): %s", exitErr.ExitCode(), stderr.String())
   307→		}
   308→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to run claude CLI: %w", err)
   309→	}
   310→
   311→	// Parse the CLI response
   312→	var cliResponse wingmanCLIResponse
   313→	if err := json.Unmarshal(stdout.Bytes(), &cliResponse); err != nil {
   314→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to parse claude CLI response: %w", err)
   315→	}
   316→
   317→	return cliResponse.Result, nil
   318→}
   319→
   320→// readSessionContext reads the context.md file from the session's checkpoint
   321→// metadata directory. Returns empty string if unavailable.
   322→func readSessionContext(repoRoot, sessionID string) string {
   323→	if sessionID == "" {
   324→		return ""
   325→	}
   326→	contextPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".entire", "metadata", sessionID, "context.md")
   327→	data, err := os.ReadFile(contextPath) //nolint:gosec // path constructed from repoRoot + session ID
   328→	if err != nil {
   329→		return ""
   330→	}
   331→	return string(data)
   332→}
   333→
   334→// isSessionIdle checks if the given session is in the IDLE phase.
   335→func isSessionIdle(sessionID string) bool {
   336→	state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   337→	if err != nil || state == nil {
   338→		return false
   339→	}
   340→	return state.Phase == session.PhaseIdle
   341→}
   342→
   343→// triggerAutoApply spawns claude --continue to apply the review suggestions.
   344→func triggerAutoApply(repoRoot string) error {
   345→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), wingmanApplyTimeout)
   346→	defer cancel()
   347→
   348→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "claude",
   349→		"--continue",
   350→		"--print",
   351→		"--setting-sources", "",
   352→		// Auto-accept edits so the agent can modify files and delete REVIEW.md
   353→		// without requiring user consent (this runs in a background process).
   354→		"--permission-mode", "acceptEdits",
   355→		wingmanApplyInstruction,
   356→	)
   357→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   358→	// Strip GIT_* env vars to prevent hook interference, and set
   359→	// ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY=1 so git hooks (post-commit) know not to
   360→	// trigger another wingman review (prevents infinite recursion).
   361→	env := wingmanStripGitEnv(os.Environ())
   362→	env = append(env, "ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY=1")
   363→	cmd.Env = env
   364→
   365→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   366→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   367→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   368→
   369→	return cmd.Run() //nolint:wrapcheck // caller wraps
   370→}
   371→
   372→// wingmanStripGitEnv returns a copy of env with all GIT_* variables removed.
   373→// This prevents a subprocess from discovering or modifying the parent's git repo
   374→// when we want isolation (e.g., running claude --print for review).
   375→func wingmanStripGitEnv(env []string) []string {
   376→	filtered := make([]string, 0, len(env))
   377→	for _, e := range env {
   378→		if !strings.HasPrefix(e, "GIT_") {
   379→			filtered = append(filtered, e)
   380→		}
   381→	}
   382→	return filtered
   383→}
   384→
   385→// saveWingmanStateDirect writes the wingman state file directly to a known path
   386→// under repoRoot, avoiding os.Chdir (which mutates process-wide state).
   387→func saveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot string, state *WingmanState) {
   388→	statePath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanStateFile)
   389→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(statePath), 0o750); err != nil {
   390→		return
   391→	}
   392→	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(state, "", "  ")
   393→	if err != nil {
   394→		return
   395→	}
   396→	//nolint:gosec,errcheck // G306: state file is config, not secrets; best-effort write
   397→	_ = os.WriteFile(statePath, data, 0o644)
   398→}

     1→//go:build unix
     2→
     3→package cli
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"context"
     7→	"io"
     8→	"os"
     9→	"os/exec"
    10→	"path/filepath"
    11→	"syscall"
    12→)
    13→
    14→// spawnDetachedWingmanReview spawns a detached subprocess to run the wingman review.
    15→// repoRoot is the repository root used to locate the log file.
    16→// payloadPath is the path to the JSON payload file.
    17→// On Unix, this uses process group detachment so the subprocess continues
    18→// after the parent exits.
    19→func spawnDetachedWingmanReview(repoRoot, payloadPath string) {
    20→	executable, err := os.Executable()
    21→	if err != nil {
    22→		return
    23→	}
    24→
    25→	//nolint:gosec // G204: payloadPath is controlled internally, not user input
    26→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), executable, "wingman", "__review", payloadPath)
    27→
    28→	// Detach from parent process group so subprocess survives parent exit
    29→	cmd.SysProcAttr = &syscall.SysProcAttr{
    30→		Setpgid: true,
    31→	}
    32→
    33→	// Don't hold the working directory
    34→	cmd.Dir = "/"
    35→
    36→	// Inherit environment (needed for PATH, git config, etc.)
    37→	cmd.Env = os.Environ()
    38→
    39→	// Redirect stderr to a log file for debugging the background process.
    40→	// This catches panics, errors, and all wingmanLog() output.
    41→	cmd.Stdout = io.Discard
    42→	logDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".entire", "logs")
    43→	if mkErr := os.MkdirAll(logDir, 0o750); mkErr == nil {
    44→		//nolint:gosec // G304: path is constructed from repoRoot + constants
    45→		if f, openErr := os.OpenFile(filepath.Join(logDir, "wingman.log"),
    46→			os.O_CREATE|os.O_APPEND|os.O_WRONLY, 0o600); openErr == nil {
    47→			cmd.Stderr = f
    48→			// f stays open in the child process; the OS closes it on exit
    49→		} else {
    50→			cmd.Stderr = io.Discard
    51→		}
    52→	} else {
    53→		cmd.Stderr = io.Discard
    54→	}
    55→
    56→	// Start the process (non-blocking)
    57→	if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
    58→		return
    59→	}
    60→
    61→	// Release the process so it can run independently
    62→	//nolint:errcheck // Best effort - process should continue regardless
    63→	_ = cmd.Process.Release()
    64→}
    65→

     1→//go:build !unix
     2→
     3→package cli
     4→
     5→// spawnDetachedWingmanReview is a no-op on non-Unix platforms.
     6→// Windows support for detached processes would require different syscall flags
     7→// (CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP, DETACHED_PROCESS), but wingman is best-effort
     8→// so we simply skip it on unsupported platforms.
     9→func spawnDetachedWingmanReview(_, _ string) {
    10→	// No-op: detached subprocess spawning not implemented for this platform
    11→}

    24→// hookInputData contains parsed hook input and session identifiers.
    25→type hookInputData struct {
    26→	agent     agent.Agent
    27→	input     *agent.HookInput
    28→	sessionID string
    29→}
    30→
    31→// parseAndLogHookInput parses the hook input and sets up logging context.
    32→func parseAndLogHookInput() (*hookInputData, error) {
    33→	// Get the agent from the hook command context (e.g., "entire hooks claude-code ...")
    34→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
    35→	if err != nil {
    36→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
    37→	}
    38→
    39→	// Parse hook input using agent interface
    40→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookUserPromptSubmit, os.Stdin)
    41→	if err != nil {
    42→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
    43→	}
    44→
    45→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
    46→	logging.Info(logCtx, "user-prompt-submit",
    47→		slog.String("hook", "user-prompt-submit"),
    48→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
    49→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
    50→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
    51→	)
    52→
    53→	sessionID := input.SessionID
    54→	if sessionID == "" {
    55→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
    56→	}
    57→
    58→	// Get the Entire session ID, preferring the persisted value to handle midnight boundary
    59→	return &hookInputData{
    60→		agent:     ag,
    61→		input:     input,
    62→		sessionID: sessionID,
    63→	}, nil
    64→}
    65→
    66→// captureInitialState captures the initial state on user prompt submit.
    67→func captureInitialState() error {
    68→	// Parse hook input and setup logging
    69→	hookData, err := parseAndLogHookInput()
    70→	if err != nil {
    71→		return err
    72→	}
    73→
    74→	// CLI captures state directly (including transcript position)
    75→	if err := CapturePrePromptState(hookData.sessionID, hookData.input.SessionRef); err != nil {
    76→		return err
    77→	}
    78→
    79→	// If a wingman review is pending, inject instruction into the agent's context
    80→	// so it automatically reads and applies the suggestions.
    81→	if settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {
    82→		repoRoot, rootErr := paths.RepoRoot()
    83→		if rootErr == nil {
    84→			if _, statErr := os.Stat(filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)); statErr == nil {
    85→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review available: .entire/REVIEW.md\n")
    86→				if err := outputHookResponse(wingmanApplyInstruction); err != nil {
    87→					fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Warning: failed to inject review instruction: %v\n", err)
    88→				}
    89→			}
    90→		}
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	// If strategy implements SessionInitializer, call it to initialize session state
    94→	strat := GetStrategy()
    95→	if initializer, ok := strat.(strategy.SessionInitializer); ok {
    96→		agentType := hookData.agent.Type()
    97→		if err := initializer.InitializeSession(hookData.sessionID, agentType, hookData.input.SessionRef, hookData.input.UserPrompt); err != nil {
    98→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to initialize session state: %v\n", err)
    99→		}
   100→	}
   101→
   102→	return nil
   103→}

   105→// commitWithMetadata commits the session changes with metadata.
   106→func commitWithMetadata() error { //nolint:maintidx // already present in codebase
   107→	// Get the agent for hook input parsing and session ID transformation
   108→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   109→	if err != nil {
   110→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   111→	}
   112→
   113→	// Parse hook input using agent interface
   114→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookStop, os.Stdin)
   115→	if err != nil {
   116→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   117→	}
   118→
   119→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   120→	logging.Info(logCtx, "stop",
   121→		slog.String("hook", "stop"),
   122→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   123→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   124→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
   125→	)
   126→
   127→	sessionID := input.SessionID
   128→	if sessionID == "" {
   129→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
   130→	}
   131→
   132→	transcriptPath := input.SessionRef
   133→	if transcriptPath == "" || !fileExists(transcriptPath) {
   134→		return fmt.Errorf("transcript file not found or empty: %s", transcriptPath)
   135→	}
   136→
   137→	// Early check: bail out quickly if the repo has no commits yet.
   138→	// Without this, the function does a lot of work (transcript copy, prompt extraction, etc.)
   139→	// before eventually failing deep in strategy.SaveChanges().
   140→	if repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository(); err == nil && strategy.IsEmptyRepository(repo) {
   141→		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping checkpoint. Will activate after first commit.")
   142→		return NewSilentError(strategy.ErrEmptyRepository)
   143→	}
   144→
   145→	// Create session metadata folder using the entire session ID (preserves original date on resume)
   146→	// Use AbsPath to ensure we create at repo root, not relative to cwd
   147→	sessionDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   148→	sessionDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(sessionDir)
   149→	if err != nil {
   150→		sessionDirAbs = sessionDir // Fallback to relative
   151→	}
   152→	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessionDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
   153→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session directory: %w", err)
   154→	}
   155→
   156→	// Wait for Claude Code to flush the transcript file.
   157→	// The stop hook fires before the transcript is fully written to disk.
   158→	// We poll for our own hook_progress sentinel entry in the file tail,
   159→	// which guarantees all prior entries have been flushed.
   160→	waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath, time.Now())
   161→
   162→	// Copy transcript
   163→	logFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   164→	if err := copyFile(transcriptPath, logFile); err != nil {
   165→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to copy transcript: %w", err)
   166→	}
   167→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Copied transcript to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.TranscriptFileName)
   168→
   169→	// Load pre-prompt state (captured on UserPromptSubmit)
   170→	// Needed for transcript offset and file change detection
   171→	preState, err := LoadPrePromptState(sessionID)
   172→	if err != nil {
   173→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   174→	}
   175→
   176→	// Determine transcript offset: prefer pre-prompt state, fall back to session state.
   177→	// Pre-prompt state has the offset when the transcript path was available at prompt time.
   178→	// Session state has the offset updated after each successful checkpoint save (auto-commit).
   179→	var transcriptOffset int
   180→	if preState != nil && preState.StepTranscriptStart > 0 {
   181→		transcriptOffset = preState.StepTranscriptStart
   182→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state found: parsing transcript from line %d\n", transcriptOffset)
   183→	} else {
   184→		// Fall back to session state (e.g., auto-commit strategy updates it after each save)
   185→		sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   186→		if loadErr != nil {
   187→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   188→		}
   189→		if sessionState != nil && sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart > 0 {
   190→			transcriptOffset = sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart
   191→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session state found: parsing transcript from line %d\n", transcriptOffset)
   192→		}
   193→	}
   194→
   195→	// Parse transcript (optionally from offset for strategies that track transcript position)
   196→	// When transcriptOffset > 0, only parse NEW lines since the last checkpoint
   197→	var transcript []transcriptLine
   198→	var totalLines int
   199→	if transcriptOffset > 0 {
   200→		// Parse only NEW lines since last checkpoint
   201→		transcript, totalLines, err = parseTranscriptFromLine(transcriptPath, transcriptOffset)
   202→		if err != nil {
   203→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript from line %d: %w", transcriptOffset, err)
   204→		}
   205→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Parsed %d new transcript lines (total: %d)\n", len(transcript), totalLines)
   206→	} else {
   207→		// First prompt or no session state - parse entire transcript
   208→		// Use parseTranscriptFromLine with offset 0 to also get totalLines
   209→		transcript, totalLines, err = parseTranscriptFromLine(transcriptPath, 0)
   210→		if err != nil {
   211→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   212→		}
   213→	}
   214→
   215→	// Extract all prompts since last checkpoint for prompt file
   216→	allPrompts := extractUserPrompts(transcript)
   217→	promptFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.PromptFileName)
   218→	promptContent := strings.Join(allPrompts, "\n\n---\n\n")
   219→	if err := os.WriteFile(promptFile, []byte(promptContent), 0o600); err != nil {
   220→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write prompt file: %w", err)
   221→	}
   222→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted %d prompt(s) to: %s\n", len(allPrompts), sessionDir+"/"+paths.PromptFileName)
   223→
   224→	// Extract summary
   225→	summaryFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.SummaryFileName)
   226→	summary := extractLastAssistantMessage(transcript)
   227→	if err := os.WriteFile(summaryFile, []byte(summary), 0o600); err != nil {
   228→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write summary file: %w", err)
   229→	}
   230→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted summary to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.SummaryFileName)
   231→
   232→	// Get modified files from transcript
   233→	modifiedFiles := extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   234→
   235→	// Generate commit message from last user prompt
   236→	lastPrompt := ""
   237→	if len(allPrompts) > 0 {
   238→		lastPrompt = allPrompts[len(allPrompts)-1]
   239→	}
   240→	commitMessage := generateCommitMessage(lastPrompt)
   241→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Using commit message: %s\n", commitMessage)
   242→
   243→	// Get repo root for path conversion (not cwd, since Claude may be in a subdirectory)
   244→	// Using cwd would filter out files in sibling directories (paths starting with ..)
   245→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   246→	if err != nil {
   247→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   248→	}
   249→
   250→	if preState != nil {
   251→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state: %d pre-existing untracked files\n", len(preState.UntrackedFiles))
   252→	}
   253→
   254→	// Compute new and deleted files (single git status call)
   255→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preState.PreUntrackedFiles())
   256→	if err != nil {
   257→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   258→	}
   259→
   260→	// Filter and normalize all paths (CLI responsibility)
   261→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   262→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   263→	if changes != nil {
   264→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   265→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   266→	}
   267→
   268→	// Check if there are any changes to commit
   269→	totalChanges := len(relModifiedFiles) + len(relNewFiles) + len(relDeletedFiles)
   270→	if totalChanges == 0 {
   271→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No files were modified during this session\n")
   272→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipping commit\n")
   273→		// Still transition phase even when skipping commit — the turn is ending.
   274→		transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   275→		// Clean up state even when skipping
   276→		if err := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); err != nil {
   277→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   278→		}
   279→		return nil
   280→	}
   281→
   282→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files modified during session (%d):\n", len(relModifiedFiles))
   283→	for _, file := range relModifiedFiles {
   284→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   285→	}
   286→	if len(relNewFiles) > 0 {
   287→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "New files created (%d):\n", len(relNewFiles))
   288→		for _, file := range relNewFiles {
   289→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  + %s\n", file)
   290→		}
   291→	}
   292→	if len(relDeletedFiles) > 0 {
   293→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files deleted (%d):\n", len(relDeletedFiles))
   294→		for _, file := range relDeletedFiles {
   295→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   296→		}
   297→	}
   298→
   299→	// Create context file before saving changes
   300→	contextFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.ContextFileName)
   301→	if err := createContextFileMinimal(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID, promptFile, summaryFile, transcript); err != nil {
   302→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create context file: %w", err)
   303→	}
   304→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created context file: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.ContextFileName)
   305→
   306→	// Get git author from local/global config
   307→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   308→	if err != nil {
   309→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   310→	}
   311→
   312→	// Get the configured strategy
   313→	strat := GetStrategy()
   314→
   315→	// Ensure strategy setup is in place (auto-installs git hook, gitignore, etc. if needed)
   316→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   317→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   318→	}
   319→
   320→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   321→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   322→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   323→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   324→	}
   325→
   326→	// Get transcript position from pre-prompt state (captured at step/turn start)
   327→	var transcriptIdentifierAtStart string
   328→	var transcriptLinesAtStart int
   329→	if preState != nil {
   330→		transcriptIdentifierAtStart = preState.LastTranscriptIdentifier
   331→		transcriptLinesAtStart = preState.StepTranscriptStart
   332→	}
   333→
   334→	// Calculate token usage for this checkpoint (Claude Code specific)
   335→	var tokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage
   336→	if transcriptPath != "" {
   337→		// Subagents are stored in a subagents/ directory next to the main transcript
   338→		subagentsDir := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(transcriptPath), sessionID, "subagents")
   339→		usage, err := claudecode.CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptPath, transcriptLinesAtStart, subagentsDir)
   340→		if err != nil {
   341→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage: %v\n", err)
   342→		} else {
   343→			tokenUsage = usage
   344→		}
   345→	}
   346→
   347→	// Build fully-populated save context and delegate to strategy
   348→	ctx := strategy.SaveContext{
   349→		SessionID:                sessionID,
   350→		ModifiedFiles:            relModifiedFiles,
   351→		NewFiles:                 relNewFiles,
   352→		DeletedFiles:             relDeletedFiles,
   353→		MetadataDir:              sessionDir,
   354→		MetadataDirAbs:           sessionDirAbs,
   355→		CommitMessage:            commitMessage,
   356→		TranscriptPath:           transcriptPath,
   357→		AuthorName:               author.Name,
   358→		AuthorEmail:              author.Email,
   359→		AgentType:                agentType,
   360→		StepTranscriptIdentifier: transcriptIdentifierAtStart,
   361→		StepTranscriptStart:      transcriptLinesAtStart,
   362→		TokenUsage:               tokenUsage,
   363→	}
   364→
   365→	if err := strat.SaveChanges(ctx); err != nil {
   366→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save changes: %w", err)
   367→	}
   368→
   369→	// Update session state with new transcript position for strategies that create
   370→	// commits on the active branch (auto-commit strategy). This prevents parsing old transcript
   371→	// lines on subsequent checkpoints.
   372→	// Note: Shadow strategy tracks transcript position per-step via StepTranscriptStart in
   373→	// pre-prompt state, but doesn't advance CheckpointTranscriptStart in session state because
   374→	// its checkpoints accumulate all files touched across the entire session.
   375→	if strat.Name() == strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit {

   377→		sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   378→		if loadErr != nil {
   379→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   380→		}
   381→		// Create session state lazily if it doesn't exist (backward compat for resumed sessions
   382→		// or if InitializeSession was never called/failed)
   383→		if sessionState == nil {
   384→			sessionState = &strategy.SessionState{
   385→				SessionID: sessionID,
   386→			}
   387→		}
   388→		sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = totalLines
   389→		sessionState.StepCount++
   390→		if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); updateErr != nil {
   391→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", updateErr)
   392→		} else {
   393→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Updated session state: transcript position=%d, checkpoint=%d\n",
   394→				totalLines, sessionState.StepCount)
   395→		}
   396→	}

   398→	// Fire EventTurnEnd to transition session phase (all strategies).
   399→	// This moves ACTIVE → IDLE or ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE.
   400→	// For ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE, HandleTurnEnd dispatches ActionCondense.
   401→	transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)

   404→	// in SaveChanges). Manual-commit triggers wingman from the git post-commit hook
   405→	// instead, since the user commits manually.
   406→	if totalChanges > 0 && strat.Name() == strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit && settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {
   407→		triggerWingmanReview(WingmanPayload{
   408→			SessionID:     sessionID,
   409→			RepoRoot:      repoRoot,
   410→			ModifiedFiles: relModifiedFiles,
   411→			NewFiles:      relNewFiles,
   412→			DeletedFiles:  relDeletedFiles,
   413→			Prompts:       allPrompts,
   414→			CommitMessage: commitMessage,
   415→		})
   416→	}
   417→
   418→	// Clean up pre-prompt state (CLI responsibility)
   419→	if err := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); err != nil {
   420→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   421→	}
   422→
   423→	return nil
   424→}
   425→
   426→// handleClaudeCodePostTodo handles the PostToolUse[TodoWrite] hook for subagent checkpoints.
   427→// Creates a checkpoint if we're in a subagent context (active pre-task file exists).
   428→// Skips silently if not in subagent context (main agent).
   429→func handleClaudeCodePostTodo() error {
   430→	input, err := parseSubagentCheckpointHookInput(os.Stdin)
   431→	if err != nil {
   432→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PostToolUse[TodoWrite] input: %w", err)
   433→	}
   434→
   435→	// Get agent for logging context
   436→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   437→	if err != nil {
   438→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   439→	}
   440→
   441→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   442→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-todo",
   443→		slog.String("hook", "post-todo"),
   444→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   445→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   446→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.TranscriptPath),
   447→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   448→	)
   449→
   450→	// Check if we're in a subagent context by looking for an active pre-task file
   451→	taskToolUseID, found := FindActivePreTaskFile()
   452→	if !found {
   453→		// Not in subagent context - this is a main agent TodoWrite, skip
   454→		return nil
   455→	}
   456→
   457→	// Skip on default branch to avoid polluting main/master history
   458→	if skip, branchName := ShouldSkipOnDefaultBranch(); skip {
   459→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping incremental checkpoint on branch '%s'\n", branchName)
   460→		return nil
   461→	}
   462→
   463→	// Detect file changes since last checkpoint
   464→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(nil)
   465→	if err != nil {
   466→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to detect changed files: %v\n", err)
   467→		return nil
   468→	}
   469→
   470→	// If no file changes, skip creating a checkpoint
   471→	if len(changes.Modified) == 0 && len(changes.New) == 0 && len(changes.Deleted) == 0 {
   472→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping incremental checkpoint\n")
   473→		return nil
   474→	}
   475→
   476→	// Get git author
   477→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   478→	if err != nil {
   479→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to get git author: %v\n", err)
   480→		return nil
   481→	}
   482→
   483→	// Get the active strategy
   484→	strat := GetStrategy()
   485→
   486→	// Ensure strategy setup is complete
   487→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   488→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   489→		return nil
   490→	}
   491→
   492→	// Get the session ID from the transcript path or input, then transform to Entire session ID
   493→	sessionID := input.SessionID
   494→	if sessionID == "" {
   495→		sessionID = paths.ExtractSessionIDFromTranscriptPath(input.TranscriptPath)
   496→	}
   497→
   498→	// Get next checkpoint sequence
   499→	seq := GetNextCheckpointSequence(sessionID, taskToolUseID)
   500→
   501→	// Extract the todo content from the tool_input.
   502→	// PostToolUse receives the NEW todo list where the just-completed work is
   503→	// marked as "completed". The last completed item is the work that was just done.
   504→	todoContent := ExtractLastCompletedTodoFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
   505→	if todoContent == "" {
   506→		// No completed items - this is likely the first TodoWrite (planning phase).
   507→		// Check if there are any todos at all to avoid duplicate messages.
   508→		todoCount := CountTodosFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
   509→		if todoCount > 0 {
   510→			// Use "Planning: N todos" format for the first TodoWrite
   511→			todoContent = fmt.Sprintf("Planning: %d todos", todoCount)
   512→		}
   513→		// If todoCount == 0, todoContent remains empty and FormatIncrementalMessage
   514→		// will fall back to "Checkpoint #N" format
   515→	}
   516→
   517→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   518→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   519→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   520→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   521→	}
   522→
   523→	// Build incremental checkpoint context
   524→	ctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{
   525→		SessionID:           sessionID,
   526→		ToolUseID:           taskToolUseID,
   527→		ModifiedFiles:       changes.Modified,
   528→		NewFiles:            changes.New,
   529→		DeletedFiles:        changes.Deleted,
   530→		TranscriptPath:      input.TranscriptPath,
   531→		AuthorName:          author.Name,
   532→		AuthorEmail:         author.Email,
   533→		IsIncremental:       true,
   534→		IncrementalSequence: seq,
   535→		IncrementalType:     input.ToolName,
   536→		IncrementalData:     input.ToolInput,
   537→		TodoContent:         todoContent,
   538→		AgentType:           agentType,
   539→	}
   540→
   541→	// Save incremental checkpoint
   542→	if err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   543→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to save incremental checkpoint: %v\n", err)
   544→		return nil
   545→	}
   546→
   547→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Created incremental checkpoint #%d for %s (task: %s)\n",
   548→		seq, input.ToolName, taskToolUseID[:min(12, len(taskToolUseID))])
   549→	return nil
   550→}
   551→
   552→// handleClaudeCodePreTask handles the PreToolUse[Task] hook
   553→func handleClaudeCodePreTask() error {
   554→	input, err := parseTaskHookInput(os.Stdin)
   555→	if err != nil {
   556→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PreToolUse[Task] input: %w", err)
   557→	}
   558→
   559→	// Get agent for logging context
   560→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   561→	if err != nil {
   562→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   563→	}
   564→
   565→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   566→	logging.Info(logCtx, "pre-task",
   567→		slog.String("hook", "pre-task"),
   568→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   569→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   570→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.TranscriptPath),
   571→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   572→	)
   573→
   574→	// Log context to stdout
   575→	logPreTaskHookContext(os.Stdout, input)
   576→
   577→	// Capture pre-task state locally (for computing new files when task completes).
   578→	// We don't create a shadow branch commit here. Commits are created during
   579→	// task completion (handleClaudeCodePostTask/handleClaudeCodePostTodo) only if the task resulted
   580→	// in file changes.
   581→	if err := CapturePreTaskState(input.ToolUseID); err != nil {
   582→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to capture pre-task state: %w", err)
   583→	}
   584→
   585→	return nil
   586→}
   587→
   588→// handleClaudeCodePostTask handles the PostToolUse[Task] hook
   589→func handleClaudeCodePostTask() error {
   590→	input, err := parsePostTaskHookInput(os.Stdin)
   591→	if err != nil {
   592→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PostToolUse[Task] input: %w", err)
   593→	}
   594→
   595→	// Extract subagent type from tool_input for logging
   596→	subagentType, taskDescription := ParseSubagentTypeAndDescription(input.ToolInput)
   597→
   598→	// Get agent for logging context
   599→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   600→	if err != nil {
   601→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   602→	}
   603→
   604→	// Log parsed input context
   605→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   606→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-task",
   607→		slog.String("hook", "post-task"),
   608→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   609→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   610→		slog.String("agent_id", input.AgentID),
   611→		slog.String("subagent_type", subagentType),
   612→	)
   613→
   614→	// Determine subagent transcript path
   615→	transcriptDir := filepath.Dir(input.TranscriptPath)
   616→	var subagentTranscriptPath string
   617→	if input.AgentID != "" {
   618→		subagentTranscriptPath = AgentTranscriptPath(transcriptDir, input.AgentID)
   619→		if !fileExists(subagentTranscriptPath) {
   620→			subagentTranscriptPath = ""
   621→		}
   622→	}
   623→
   624→	// Log context to stdout
   625→	logPostTaskHookContext(os.Stdout, input, subagentTranscriptPath)
   626→
   627→	// Parse transcript to extract modified files
   628→	var modifiedFiles []string
   629→	if subagentTranscriptPath != "" {
   630→		// Use subagent transcript if available
   631→		transcript, err := parseTranscript(subagentTranscriptPath)
   632→		if err != nil {
   633→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to parse subagent transcript: %v\n", err)
   634→		} else {
   635→			modifiedFiles = extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   636→		}
   637→	} else {
   638→		// Fall back to main transcript
   639→		transcript, err := parseTranscript(input.TranscriptPath)
   640→		if err != nil {
   641→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to parse transcript: %v\n", err)
   642→		} else {
   643→			modifiedFiles = extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   644→		}
   645→	}
   646→
   647→	// Load pre-task state and compute new files
   648→	preState, err := LoadPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID)
   649→	if err != nil {
   650→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-task state: %v\n", err)
   651→	}
   652→
   653→	// Compute new and deleted files (single git status call)
   654→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preState.PreUntrackedFiles())
   655→	if err != nil {
   656→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   657→	}
   658→
   659→	// Get repo root for path conversion (not cwd, since Claude may be in a subdirectory)
   660→	// Using cwd would filter out files in sibling directories (paths starting with ..)
   661→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   662→	if err != nil {
   663→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   664→	}
   665→
   666→	// Filter and normalize paths
   667→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   668→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   669→	if changes != nil {
   670→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   671→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   672→	}
   673→
   674→	// If no file changes, skip creating a checkpoint
   675→	if len(relModifiedFiles) == 0 && len(relNewFiles) == 0 && len(relDeletedFiles) == 0 {
   676→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping task checkpoint\n")
   677→		// Cleanup pre-task state (ignore error - cleanup is best-effort)
   678→		_ = CleanupPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   679→		return nil
   680→	}
   681→
   682→	// Find checkpoint UUID from main transcript (best-effort, ignore errors)
   683→	transcript, _ := parseTranscript(input.TranscriptPath) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort extraction
   684→	checkpointUUID, _ := FindCheckpointUUID(transcript, input.ToolUseID)
   685→
   686→	// Get git author
   687→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   688→	if err != nil {
   689→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   690→	}
   691→
   692→	// Get the configured strategy
   693→	strat := GetStrategy()
   694→
   695→	// Ensure strategy setup is in place
   696→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   697→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   698→	}
   699→
   700→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   701→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   702→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   703→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   704→	}
   705→
   706→	// Build task checkpoint context - strategy handles metadata creation
   707→	// Note: Incremental checkpoints are now created during task execution via handleClaudeCodePostTodo,
   708→	// so we don't need to collect/cleanup staging area here.
   709→	ctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{
   710→		SessionID:              input.SessionID,
   711→		ToolUseID:              input.ToolUseID,
   712→		AgentID:                input.AgentID,
   713→		ModifiedFiles:          relModifiedFiles,
   714→		NewFiles:               relNewFiles,
   715→		DeletedFiles:           relDeletedFiles,
   716→		TranscriptPath:         input.TranscriptPath,
   717→		SubagentTranscriptPath: subagentTranscriptPath,
   718→		CheckpointUUID:         checkpointUUID,
   719→		AuthorName:             author.Name,
   720→		AuthorEmail:            author.Email,
   721→		SubagentType:           subagentType,
   722→		TaskDescription:        taskDescription,
   723→		AgentType:              agentType,
   724→	}
   725→
   726→	// Call strategy to save task checkpoint - strategy handles all metadata creation
   727→	if err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   728→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save task checkpoint: %w", err)
   729→	}
   730→
   731→	// Cleanup pre-task state (ignore error - cleanup is best-effort)
   732→	_ = CleanupPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   733→
   734→	return nil
   735→}
   736→
   737→// handleClaudeCodeSessionStart handles the SessionStart hook for Claude Code.
   738→func handleClaudeCodeSessionStart() error {
   739→	return handleSessionStartCommon()
   740→}
   741→
   742→// handleClaudeCodeSessionEnd handles the SessionEnd hook for Claude Code.
   743→// This fires when the user explicitly closes the session.
   744→// Updates the session state with EndedAt timestamp.
   745→func handleClaudeCodeSessionEnd() error {
   746→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   747→	if err != nil {
   748→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   749→	}
   750→
   751→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookSessionEnd, os.Stdin)
   752→	if err != nil {
   753→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   754→	}
   755→
   756→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   757→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-end",
   758→		slog.String("hook", "session-end"),
   759→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   760→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   761→	)
   762→
   763→	if input.SessionID == "" {
   764→		return nil // No session to update
   765→	}
   766→
   767→	// Best-effort cleanup - don't block session closure on failure
   768→	if err := markSessionEnded(input.SessionID); err != nil {
   769→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to mark session ended: %v\n", err)
   770→	}
   771→	return nil
   772→}
   773→
   774→// transitionSessionTurnEnd fires EventTurnEnd to move the session from
   775→// ACTIVE → IDLE (or ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE). Best-effort: logs warnings
   776→// on failure rather than returning errors.
   777→func transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID string) {
   778→	turnState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   779→	if loadErr != nil {
   780→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for turn end: %v\n", loadErr)
   781→		return
   782→	}
   783→	if turnState == nil {
   784→		return
   785→	}
   786→	remaining := strategy.TransitionAndLog(turnState, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})
   787→
   788→	// Dispatch strategy-specific actions (e.g., ActionCondense for ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE)
   789→	if len(remaining) > 0 {
   790→		strat := GetStrategy()
   791→		if handler, ok := strat.(strategy.TurnEndHandler); ok {
   792→			if err := handler.HandleTurnEnd(turnState, remaining); err != nil {
   793→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end action dispatch failed: %v\n", err)
   794→			}
   795→		}
   796→	}
   797→
   798→	if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(turnState); updateErr != nil {
   799→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session phase on turn end: %v\n", updateErr)
   800→	}
   801→}
   802→
   803→// markSessionEnded transitions the session to ENDED phase via the state machine.
   804→func markSessionEnded(sessionID string) error {
   805→	state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   806→	if err != nil {
   807→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   808→	}
   809→	if state == nil {
   810→		return nil // No state file, nothing to update
   811→	}
   812→
   813→	strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStop, session.TransitionContext{})
   814→
   815→	now := time.Now()
   816→	state.EndedAt = &now
   817→
   818→	if err := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   819→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   820→	}
   821→	return nil
   822→}
   823→
   824→// stopHookSentinel is the string that appears in Claude Code's hook_progress
   825→// transcript entry when it launches our stop hook. Used to detect that the
   826→// transcript file has been fully flushed before we copy it.
   827→const stopHookSentinel = "hooks claude-code stop"
   828→
   829→// waitForTranscriptFlush polls the transcript file tail for the hook_progress
   830→// sentinel entry that Claude Code writes when launching the stop hook.
   831→// Once this entry appears in the file, all prior entries (assistant replies,
   832→// tool results) are guaranteed to have been flushed.
   833→//
   834→// hookStartTime is the approximate time our process started, used to avoid
   835→// matching stale sentinel entries from previous stop hook invocations.
   836→//
   837→// Falls back silently after a timeout — the transcript copy will proceed
   838→// with whatever data is available.
   839→func waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath string, hookStartTime time.Time) {
   840→	const (
   841→		maxWait      = 3 * time.Second
   842→		pollInterval = 50 * time.Millisecond
   843→		tailBytes    = 4096 // Read last 4KB — sentinel is near the end
   844→		maxSkew      = 2 * time.Second
   845→	)
   846→
   847→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks")
   848→	deadline := time.Now().Add(maxWait)
   849→	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
   850→		if checkStopSentinel(transcriptPath, tailBytes, hookStartTime, maxSkew) {
   851→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel found",
   852→				slog.Duration("wait", time.Since(hookStartTime)),
   853→			)
   854→			return
   855→		}
   856→		time.Sleep(pollInterval)
   857→	}
   858→	// Timeout — proceed with whatever is on disk.
   859→	logging.Warn(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel not found within timeout, proceeding",
   860→		slog.Duration("timeout", maxWait),
   861→	)
   862→}
   863→
   864→// checkStopSentinel reads the tail of the transcript file and looks for a
   865→// hook_progress entry containing the stop hook sentinel, with a timestamp
   866→// close to hookStartTime.
   867→func checkStopSentinel(path string, tailBytes int64, hookStartTime time.Time, maxSkew time.Duration) bool {
   868→	f, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // path comes from agent hook input
   869→	if err != nil {
   870→		return false
   871→	}
   872→	defer f.Close()
   873→
   874→	// Seek to tail
   875→	info, err := f.Stat()
   876→	if err != nil {
   877→		return false
   878→	}
   879→	offset := info.Size() - tailBytes
   880→	if offset < 0 {
   881→		offset = 0
   882→	}
   883→	buf := make([]byte, info.Size()-offset)
   884→	if _, err := f.ReadAt(buf, offset); err != nil {
   885→		return false
   886→	}
   887→
   888→	// Scan lines from the tail for the sentinel
   889→	lines := strings.Split(string(buf), "\n")
   890→	for _, line := range lines {
   891→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   892→		if line == "" || !strings.Contains(line, stopHookSentinel) {
   893→			continue
   894→		}
   895→
   896→		// Parse timestamp to check recency
   897→		var entry struct {
   898→			Timestamp string `json:"timestamp"`
   899→		}
   900→		if json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry) != nil || entry.Timestamp == "" {
   901→			continue
   902→		}
   903→		ts, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, entry.Timestamp)
   904→		if err != nil {
   905→			ts, err = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, entry.Timestamp)
   906→			if err != nil {
   907→				continue
   908→			}
   909→		}
   910→
   911→		// Check timestamp is within skew window of our start time
   912→		if ts.After(hookStartTime.Add(-maxSkew)) && ts.Before(hookStartTime.Add(maxSkew)) {
   913→			return true
   914→		}
   915→	}
   916→	return false
   917→}
   918→

    13→func TestComputeFilesHash_Deterministic(t *testing.T) {
    14→	t.Parallel()
    15→
    16→	files := []string{"b.go", "a.go", "c.go"}
    17→	hash1 := computeFilesHash(files)
    18→	hash2 := computeFilesHash(files)
    19→
    20→	if hash1 != hash2 {
    21→		t.Errorf("expected deterministic hash, got %s and %s", hash1, hash2)
    22→	}
    23→
    24→	// Order shouldn't matter
    25→	files2 := []string{"c.go", "a.go", "b.go"}
    26→	hash3 := computeFilesHash(files2)
    27→
    28→	if hash1 != hash3 {
    29→		t.Errorf("expected order-independent hash, got %s and %s", hash1, hash3)
    30→	}
    31→}

    53→func TestWingmanPayload_RoundTrip(t *testing.T) {
    54→	t.Parallel()
    55→
    56→	payload := WingmanPayload{
    57→		SessionID:     "test-session-123",
    58→		RepoRoot:      "/tmp/repo",
    59→		BaseCommit:    "abc123def456",
    60→		ModifiedFiles: []string{"main.go", "util.go"},
    61→		NewFiles:      []string{"new.go"},
    62→		DeletedFiles:  []string{"old.go"},
    63→		Prompts:       []string{"Fix the bug", "Add tests"},
    64→		CommitMessage: "fix: resolve issue",
    65→	}
    66→
    67→	data, err := json.Marshal(payload)
    68→	if err != nil {
    69→		t.Fatalf("failed to marshal: %v", err)
    70→	}
    71→
    72→	var decoded WingmanPayload
    73→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &decoded); err != nil {
    74→		t.Fatalf("failed to unmarshal: %v", err)
    75→	}
    76→
    77→	if decoded.SessionID != payload.SessionID {
    78→		t.Errorf("session_id: got %q, want %q", decoded.SessionID, payload.SessionID)
    79→	}
    80→	if decoded.RepoRoot != payload.RepoRoot {
    81→		t.Errorf("repo_root: got %q, want %q", decoded.RepoRoot, payload.RepoRoot)
    82→	}
    83→	if decoded.BaseCommit != payload.BaseCommit {
    84→		t.Errorf("base_commit: got %q, want %q", decoded.BaseCommit, payload.BaseCommit)
    85→	}
    86→	if len(decoded.ModifiedFiles) != len(payload.ModifiedFiles) {
    87→		t.Errorf("modified_files: got %d, want %d", len(decoded.ModifiedFiles), len(payload.ModifiedFiles))
    88→	}
    89→	if len(decoded.NewFiles) != len(payload.NewFiles) {
    90→		t.Errorf("new_files: got %d, want %d", len(decoded.NewFiles), len(payload.NewFiles))
    91→	}
    92→	if len(decoded.DeletedFiles) != len(payload.DeletedFiles) {
    93→		t.Errorf("deleted_files: got %d, want %d", len(decoded.DeletedFiles), len(payload.DeletedFiles))
    94→	}
    95→	if len(decoded.Prompts) != len(payload.Prompts) {
    96→		t.Errorf("prompts: got %d, want %d", len(decoded.Prompts), len(payload.Prompts))
    97→	}
    98→	if decoded.CommitMessage != payload.CommitMessage {
    99→		t.Errorf("commit_message: got %q, want %q", decoded.CommitMessage, payload.CommitMessage)
   100→	}
   101→}

   103→func TestBuildReviewPrompt_IncludesAllSections(t *testing.T) {
   104→	t.Parallel()
   105→
   106→	prompts := []string{"Fix the authentication bug"}
   107→	fileList := "auth.go (modified), auth_test.go (new)"
   108→	diff := `diff --git a/auth.go b/auth.go
   109→--- a/auth.go
   110→+++ b/auth.go
   111→@@ -10,6 +10,8 @@ func Login(user, pass string) error {

   151→func TestBuildReviewPrompt_EmptyPrompts(t *testing.T) {
   152→	t.Parallel()
   153→
   154→	result := buildReviewPrompt(nil, "", "", "", "file.go (modified)", "some diff")
   155→
   156→	if !strings.Contains(result, "(no prompts captured)") {
   157→		t.Error("should show no-prompts placeholder for empty prompts")
   158→	}
   159→	if !strings.Contains(result, "(no commit message)") {
   160→		t.Error("should show placeholder for empty commit message")
   161→	}
   162→	if !strings.Contains(result, "(no session context available)") {
   163→		t.Error("should show placeholder for empty session context")
   164→	}
   165→}
   166→
   167→func TestBuildReviewPrompt_TruncatesLargeDiff(t *testing.T) {
   168→	t.Parallel()
   169→
   170→	// Create a diff larger than maxDiffSize
   171→	largeDiff := strings.Repeat("x", maxDiffSize+1000)
   172→
   173→	result := buildReviewPrompt([]string{"test"}, "", "", "test-session", "file.go", largeDiff)
   174→
   175→	if !strings.Contains(result, "diff truncated at 100KB") {
   176→		t.Error("should truncate large diffs")
   177→	}
   178→	// The prompt should not contain the full diff
   179→	if strings.Contains(result, strings.Repeat("x", maxDiffSize+1000)) {
   180→		t.Error("should not contain the full oversized diff")
   181→	}
   182→}

   184→func TestWingmanState_SaveLoad(t *testing.T) {
   185→	// Uses t.Chdir so cannot be parallel
   186→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   187→
   188→	// Initialize a real git repo (paths.AbsPath needs git rev-parse)
   189→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), "git", "init", tmpDir)
   190→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   191→		t.Fatalf("failed to git init: %v", err)
   192→	}
   193→
   194→	// Create .entire directory
   195→	entireDir := filepath.Join(tmpDir, ".entire")
   196→	if err := os.MkdirAll(entireDir, 0o755); err != nil {
   197→		t.Fatalf("failed to create .entire directory: %v", err)
   198→	}
   199→
   200→	t.Chdir(tmpDir)
   201→
   202→	state := &WingmanState{
   203→		SessionID:     "test-session",
   204→		FilesHash:     "abc123",
   205→		ReviewApplied: false,
   206→	}
   207→
   208→	if err := saveWingmanState(state); err != nil {
   209→		t.Fatalf("failed to save state: %v", err)
   210→	}
   211→
   212→	loaded, err := loadWingmanState()
   213→	if err != nil {
   214→		t.Fatalf("failed to load state: %v", err)
   215→	}
   216→
   217→	if loaded.SessionID != state.SessionID {
   218→		t.Errorf("session_id: got %q, want %q", loaded.SessionID, state.SessionID)
   219→	}
   220→	if loaded.FilesHash != state.FilesHash {
   221→		t.Errorf("files_hash: got %q, want %q", loaded.FilesHash, state.FilesHash)
   222→	}
   223→	if loaded.ReviewApplied != state.ReviewApplied {
   224→		t.Errorf("review_applied: got %v, want %v", loaded.ReviewApplied, state.ReviewApplied)
   225→	}
   226→}

   229→	t.Parallel()

   231→	env := []string{
   232→		"PATH=/usr/bin",
   233→		"HOME=/home/user",
   234→		"GIT_DIR=/repo/.git",

   240→

   244→		}
   245→	}
   246→
   247→	if len(filtered) != 3 {

   252→func TestIsWingmanEnabled_Settings(t *testing.T) {
   253→	t.Parallel()
   254→
   255→	tests := []struct {
   256→		name     string
   257→		options  map[string]any
   258→		expected bool
   259→	}{
   260→		{
   261→			name:     "nil options",
   262→			options:  nil,
   263→			expected: false,
   264→		},
   265→		{
   266→			name:     "empty options",
   267→			options:  map[string]any{},
   268→			expected: false,
   269→		},
   270→		{
   271→			name:     "wingman not present",
   272→			options:  map[string]any{"summarize": map[string]any{"enabled": true}},
   273→			expected: false,
   274→		},
   275→		{
   276→			name:     "wingman enabled",
   277→			options:  map[string]any{"wingman": map[string]any{"enabled": true}},
   278→			expected: true,
   279→		},
   280→		{
   281→			name:     "wingman disabled",
   282→			options:  map[string]any{"wingman": map[string]any{"enabled": false}},
   283→			expected: false,
   284→		},
   285→		{
   286→			name:     "wingman wrong type",
   287→			options:  map[string]any{"wingman": "invalid"},
   288→			expected: false,
   289→		},
   290→		{
   291→			name:     "wingman enabled wrong type",
   292→			options:  map[string]any{"wingman": map[string]any{"enabled": "yes"}},
   293→			expected: false,
   294→		},
   295→	}
   296→
   297→	for _, tt := range tests {
   298→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
   299→			t.Parallel()
   300→			s := &EntireSettings{
   301→				StrategyOptions: tt.options,
   302→			}
   303→			if got := s.IsWingmanEnabled(); got != tt.expected {
   304→				t.Errorf("IsWingmanEnabled() = %v, want %v", got, tt.expected)
   305→			}
   306→		})
   307→	}
   308→}
   309→

  "old_string": "// saveWingmanStateDirect writes the wingman state file directly to a known path\n// under repoRoot, avoiding os.Chdir (which mutates process-wide state).\nfunc saveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot string, state *WingmanState) {",
  "new_string": "// loadWingmanStateDirect reads the wingman state file directly from a known\n// path under repoRoot. Returns nil if the file doesn't exist or can't be parsed.\nfunc loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot string) *WingmanState {\n\tstatePath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanStateFile)\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(statePath) //nolint:gosec // path is repo-relative constant\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tvar state WingmanState\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &state); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\treturn &state\n}\n\n// newWingmanApplyCmd returns a hidden subcommand used by the stop hook to\n// apply a pending REVIEW.md via claude --continue in a detached subprocess.\nfunc newWingmanApplyCmd() *cobra.Command {\n\treturn &cobra.Command{\n\t\tUse:    \"__apply\",\n\t\tHidden: true,\n\t\tArgs:   cobra.ExactArgs(1),\n\t\tRunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, args []string) error {\n\t\t\treturn runWingmanApply(args[0])\n\t\t},\n\t}\n}\n\n// shouldSkipPendingReview checks whether a pending REVIEW.md should prevent\n// a new review from being triggered. It cleans up stale/orphaned reviews.\n//\n// Returns true only when REVIEW.md exists AND belongs to the current session\n// AND is younger than wingmanStaleReviewTTL.\nfunc shouldSkipPendingReview(repoRoot, currentSessionID string) bool {\n\treviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)\n\tif _, err := os.Stat(reviewPath); err != nil {\n\t\treturn false // No REVIEW.md, don't skip\n\t}\n\n\t// REVIEW.md exists — check state to determine if it's current or stale\n\tstate := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)\n\tif state == nil {\n\t\t// Orphan: REVIEW.md without state file — clean up\n\t\t_ = os.Remove(reviewPath)\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\n\tif state.SessionID != currentSessionID {\n\t\t// Different session — stale review, clean up\n\t\t_ = os.Remove(reviewPath)\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\n\tif time.Since(state.ReviewedAt) > wingmanStaleReviewTTL {\n\t\t// Same session but too old — clean up\n\t\t_ = os.Remove(reviewPath)\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\n\treturn true // Current session, fresh review — skip\n}\n\n// saveWingmanStateDirect writes the wingman state file directly to a known path\n// under repoRoot, avoiding os.Chdir (which mutates process-wide state).\nfunc saveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot string, state *WingmanState) {"
}

  "old_string": "\t// Auto-apply phase: wait then check if session is idle\n\twingmanLog(\"waiting %s before auto-apply check\", wingmanApplyDelay)\n\ttime.Sleep(wingmanApplyDelay)\n\n\tidle := isSessionIdle(payload.SessionID)\n\twingmanLog(\"session idle check: idle=%v\", idle)\n\n\tif !idle {\n\t\twingmanLog(\"session is active, leaving REVIEW.md for notification\")\n\t\treturn nil // User is active, leave REVIEW.md for notification\n\t}\n\n\t// Trigger auto-apply via claude --continue\n\twingmanLog(\"triggering auto-apply via claude --continue\")\n\tapplyStart := time.Now()\n\tif err := triggerAutoApply(repoRoot); err != nil {\n\t\twingmanLog(\"ERROR auto-apply failed after %s: %v\", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond), err)\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to trigger auto-apply: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\twingmanLog(\"auto-apply completed in %s\", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond))\n\n\treturn nil",
  "new_string": "\t// Check if session is idle right now (rare — user usually starts typing\n\t// during the 10s settle + review time). If idle, apply immediately.\n\t// Otherwise, the stop hook will handle auto-apply when the current turn ends.\n\tidle := isSessionIdle(payload.SessionID)\n\twingmanLog(\"session idle check: idle=%v\", idle)\n\n\tif !idle {\n\t\twingmanLog(\"session is active, stop hook will handle auto-apply when turn ends\")\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Mark apply as attempted before triggering (retry prevention)\n\tnow := time.Now()\n\tstate := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)\n\tif state != nil {\n\t\tstate.ApplyAttemptedAt = &now\n\t\tsaveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot, state)\n\t}\n\n\twingmanLog(\"triggering auto-apply via claude --continue (session idle)\")\n\tapplyStart := time.Now()\n\tif err := triggerAutoApply(repoRoot); err != nil {\n\t\twingmanLog(\"ERROR auto-apply failed after %s: %v\", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond), err)\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to trigger auto-apply: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\twingmanLog(\"auto-apply completed in %s\", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond))\n\n\treturn nil"
}

> TOOL

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go",
  "old_string": "// triggerAutoApply spawns claude --continue to apply the review suggestions.",
  "new_string": "// runWingmanApply is the entrypoint for the __apply subcommand, spawned by the\n// stop hook when a pending REVIEW.md is detected. It re-checks preconditions\n// and triggers claude --continue to apply the review.\nfunc runWingmanApply(repoRoot string) error {\n\twingmanLog(\"apply process started (pid=%d)\", os.Getpid())\n\n\treviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)\n\tif !fileExists(reviewPath) {\n\t\twingmanLog(\"no REVIEW.md found, nothing to apply\")\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Retry prevention: check if apply was already attempted for this review\n\tstate := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)\n\tif state != nil && state.ApplyAttemptedAt != nil {\n\t\twingmanLog(\"apply already attempted at %s, skipping\", state.ApplyAttemptedAt.Format(time.RFC3339))\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Re-check session is still idle (user may have typed during spawn delay)\n\tif state != nil && state.SessionID != \"\" {\n\t\tif !isSessionIdle(state.SessionID) {\n\t\t\twingmanLog(\"session became active during spawn, aborting (next stop hook will retry)\")\n\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Mark apply as attempted BEFORE triggering\n\tif state != nil {\n\t\tnow := time.Now()\n\t\tstate.ApplyAttemptedAt = &now\n\t\tsaveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot, state)\n\t}\n\n\twingmanLog(\"triggering auto-apply via claude --continue\")\n\tapplyStart := time.Now()\n\tif err := triggerAutoApply(repoRoot); err != nil {\n\t\twingmanLog(\"ERROR auto-apply failed after %s: %v\", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond), err)\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to trigger auto-apply: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\twingmanLog(\"auto-apply completed in %s\", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond))\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// triggerAutoApply spawns claude --continue to apply the review suggestions."
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman_spawn_unix.go",
  "old_string": "\t// Release the process so it can run independently\n\t//nolint:errcheck // Best effort - process should continue regardless\n\t_ = cmd.Process.Release()\n}",
  "new_string": "\t// Release the process so it can run independently\n\t//nolint:errcheck // Best effort - process should continue regardless\n\t_ = cmd.Process.Release()\n}\n\n// spawnDetachedWingmanApply spawns a detached subprocess to auto-apply the\n// pending REVIEW.md via claude --continue. Called from the stop hook when a\n// review is pending after the agent turn ends.\nfunc spawnDetachedWingmanApply(repoRoot string) {\n\texecutable, err := os.Executable()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t//nolint:gosec // G204: repoRoot is from paths.RepoRoot(), not user input\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), executable, \"wingman\", \"__apply\", repoRoot)\n\n\tcmd.SysProcAttr = &syscall.SysProcAttr{\n\t\tSetpgid: true,\n\t}\n\n\tcmd.Dir = \"/\"\n\tcmd.Env = os.Environ()\n\n\tcmd.Stdout = io.Discard\n\tlogDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, \".entire\", \"logs\")\n\tif mkErr := os.MkdirAll(logDir, 0o750); mkErr == nil {\n\t\t//nolint:gosec // G304: path is constructed from repoRoot + constants\n\t\tif f, openErr := os.OpenFile(filepath.Join(logDir, \"wingman.log\"),\n\t\t\tos.O_CREATE|os.O_APPEND|os.O_WRONLY, 0o600); openErr == nil {\n\t\t\tcmd.Stderr = f\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tcmd.Stderr = io.Discard\n\t\t}\n\t} else {\n\t\tcmd.Stderr = io.Discard\n\t}\n\n\tif err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t//nolint:errcheck // Best effort - process should continue regardless\n\t_ = cmd.Process.Release()\n}"
}
```

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go",
  "old_string": "\t// Trigger wingman review for auto-commit strategy (commit already happened\n\t// in SaveChanges). Manual-commit triggers wingman from the git post-commit hook\n\t// instead, since the user commits manually.\n\tif totalChanges > 0 && strat.Name() == strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit && settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {\n\t\ttriggerWingmanReview(WingmanPayload{\n\t\t\tSessionID:     sessionID,\n\t\t\tRepoRoot:      repoRoot,\n\t\t\tModifiedFiles: relModifiedFiles,\n\t\t\tNewFiles:      relNewFiles,\n\t\t\tDeletedFiles:  relDeletedFiles,\n\t\t\tPrompts:       allPrompts,\n\t\t\tCommitMessage: commitMessage,\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\n\t// Clean up pre-prompt state (CLI responsibility)",
  "new_string": "\t// Trigger wingman review for auto-commit strategy (commit already happened\n\t// in SaveChanges). Manual-commit triggers wingman from the git post-commit hook\n\t// instead, since the user commits manually.\n\tif totalChanges > 0 && strat.Name() == strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit && settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {\n\t\ttriggerWingmanReview(WingmanPayload{\n\t\t\tSessionID:     sessionID,\n\t\t\tRepoRoot:      repoRoot,\n\t\t\tModifiedFiles: relModifiedFiles,\n\t\t\tNewFiles:      relNewFiles,\n\t\t\tDeletedFiles:  relDeletedFiles,\n\t\t\tPrompts:       allPrompts,\n\t\t\tCommitMessage: commitMessage,\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\n\t// Auto-apply pending wingman review: the stop hook fires when the agent turn\n\t// ends (ACTIVE → IDLE), which is the natural safe moment to trigger --continue.\n\t// This is the PRIMARY delivery mechanism for wingman reviews.\n\tif settings.IsWingmanEnabled() && os.Getenv(\"ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY\") == \"\" {\n\t\treviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)\n\t\tif _, statErr := os.Stat(reviewPath); statErr == nil {\n\t\t\twingmanState := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)\n\t\t\tif wingmanState == nil || wingmanState.ApplyAttemptedAt == nil {\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Pending review found, spawning auto-apply\\n\")\n\t\t\t\tspawnDetachedWingmanApply(repoRoot)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Clean up pre-prompt state (CLI responsibility)"
}

  "new_string": "\t// If a wingman review is pending for the CURRENT session and auto-apply\n\t// hasn't been attempted yet, inject instruction as a belt-and-suspenders\n\t// backup. The primary delivery mechanism is the stop hook auto-apply.\n\tif settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {\n\t\trepoRoot, rootErr := paths.RepoRoot()\n\t\tif rootErr == nil {\n\t\t\tif _, statErr := os.Stat(filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)); statErr == nil {\n\t\t\t\twingmanState := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)\n\t\t\t\tshouldInject := wingmanState != nil &&\n\t\t\t\t\twingmanState.SessionID == hookData.sessionID &&\n\t\t\t\t\twingmanState.ApplyAttemptedAt == nil\n\t\t\t\tif shouldInject {\n\t\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Review available: .entire/REVIEW.md\\n\")\n\t\t\t\t\tif err := outputHookResponse(wingmanApplyInstruction); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Warning: failed to inject review instruction: %v\\n\", err)\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}"
}

err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to unmarshal: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif decoded.ApplyAttemptedAt == nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"ApplyAttemptedAt should not be nil after round-trip\")\n\t}\n\tif !decoded.ApplyAttemptedAt.Truncate(time.Second).Equal(now) {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"ApplyAttemptedAt: got %v, want %v\", decoded.ApplyAttemptedAt, now)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestWingmanState_ApplyAttemptedAt_OmitEmpty(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\tstate := &WingmanState{\n\t\tSessionID: \"sess-1\",\n\t\tFilesHash: \"hash1\",\n\t}\n\n\tdata, err := json.Marshal(state)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to marshal: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif strings.Contains(string(data), \"apply_attempted_at\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"ApplyAttemptedAt should be omitted when nil\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestLoadWingmanStateDirect(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\tt.Run(\"missing file returns nil\", func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\tt.Parallel()\n\t\ttmpDir := t.TempDir()\n\t\tgot := loadWingmanStateDirect(tmpDir)\n\t\tif got != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected nil for missing file, got %+v\", got)\n\t\t}\n\t})\n\n\tt.Run(\"valid file returns state\", func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\tt.Parallel()\n\t\ttmpDir := t.TempDir()\n\t\tentireDir := filepath.Join(tmpDir, \".entire\")\n\t\tif err := os.MkdirAll(entireDir, 0o755); err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tnow := time.Now()\n\t\tstateJSON := `{\"session_id\":\"sess-1\",\"files_hash\":\"hash1\",\"reviewed_at\":\"` + now.Format(time.RFC3339Nano) + `\",\"review_applied\":false}`\n\t\tif err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(entireDir, \"wingman-state.json\"), []byte(stateJSON), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tgot := loadWingmanStateDirect(tmpDir)\n\t\tif got == nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatal(\"expected non-nil state\")\n\t\t}\n\t\tif got.SessionID != \"sess-1\" {\n\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"SessionID: got %q, want %q\", got.SessionID, \"sess-1\")\n\t\t}\n\t})\n}\n\nfunc TestShouldSkipPendingReview_NoReviewFile(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\ttmpDir := t.TempDir()\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(tmpDir, \".entire\"), 0o755); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\tif shouldSkipPendingReview(tmpDir, \"sess-1\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"should not skip when no REVIEW.md exists\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestShouldSkipPendingReview_SameSession(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\ttmpDir := t.TempDir()\n\tentireDir := filepath.Join(tmpDir, \".entire\")\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(entireDir, 0o755); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Write REVIEW.md\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(entireDir, \"REVIEW.md\"), []byte(\"review\"), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Write state with same session\n\tsaveWingmanStateDirect(tmpDir, &WingmanState{\n\t\tSessionID:  \"sess-1\",\n\t\tFilesHash:  \"hash1\",\n\t\tReviewedAt: time.Now(),\n\t})\n\n\tif !shouldSkipPendingReview(tmpDir, \"sess-1\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"should skip when same session has fresh review\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestShouldSkipPendingReview_DifferentSession(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\ttmpDir := t.TempDir()\n\tentireDir := filepath.Join(tmpDir, \".entire\")\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(entireDir, 0o755); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\treviewPath := filepath.Join(entireDir, \"REVIEW.md\")\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(reviewPath, []byte(\"stale review\"), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\tsaveWingmanStateDirect(tmpDir, &WingmanState{\n\t\tSessionID:  \"old-session\",\n\t\tFilesHash:  \"hash1\",\n\t\tReviewedAt: time.Now(),\n\t})\n\n\tif shouldSkipPendingReview(tmpDir, \"new-session\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"should not skip when review is from different session\")\n\t}\n\n\t// REVIEW.md should have been cleaned up\n\tif _, err := os.Stat(reviewPath); err == nil {\n\t\tt.Error(\"stale REVIEW.md should have been deleted\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestShouldSkipPendingReview_StaleTTL(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\ttmpDir := t.TempDir()\n\tentireDir := filepath.Join(tmpDir, \".entire\")\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(entireDir, 0o755); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\treviewPath := filepath.Join(entireDir, \"REVIEW.md\")\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(reviewPath, []byte(\"old review\"), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\t// State with same session but old ReviewedAt\n\tsaveWingmanStateDirect(tmpDir, &WingmanState{\n\t\tSessionID:  \"sess-1\",\n\t\tFilesHash:  \"hash1\",\n\t\tReviewedAt: time.Now().Add(-2 * time.Hour), // 2 hours old\n\t})\n\n\tif shouldSkipPendingReview(tmpDir, \"sess-1\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"should not skip when review is older than TTL\")\n\t}\n\n\tif _, err := os.Stat(reviewPath); err == nil {\n\t\tt.Error(\"stale REVIEW.md should have been deleted\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestShouldSkipPendingReview_OrphanNoState(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\ttmpDir := t.TempDir()\n\tentireDir := filepath.Join(tmpDir, \".entire\")\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(entireDir, 0o755); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\treviewPath := filepath.Join(entireDir, \"REVIEW.md\")\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(reviewPath, []byte(\"orphan review\"), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\t// No state file\n\tif shouldSkipPendingReview(tmpDir, \"sess-1\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"should not skip when no state file exists (orphan)\")\n\t}\n\n\tif _, err := os.Stat(reviewPath); > AGENT

All changes have been re-implemented and verified:

- **fmt**: clean
- **lint**: 0 issues
- **test:ci**: all passing (unit + integration)

Here's a summary of what was implemented:

**A. Stale Review Cleanup** (`wingman.go`): `shouldSkipPendingReview()` replaces the naive `os.Stat` check — cleans up orphaned reviews, stale reviews from different sessions, and reviews older than 1 hour.

**B. Remove 30s Delay** (`wingman_review.go`): Removed `wingmanApplyDelay`. After writing REVIEW.md, checks if session is idle immediately. If active (the common case), returns and lets the stop hook handle it.

**C. Stop Hook Auto-Apply** (primary delivery path):
- `wingman.go`: `newWingmanApplyCmd()` hidden `__apply` subcommand
- `wingman_review.go`: `runWingmanApply()` with precondition re-checks
- `wingman_spawn_unix.go`: `spawnDetachedWingmanApply()` 
- `wingman_spawn_other.go`: no-op for non-Unix
- `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`: stop hook triggers auto-apply after turn end

**D. Retry Prevention**: `ApplyAttemptedAt` field on `WingmanState` prevents infinite retry loops.

**E. Session-Aware Prompt-Submit Injection** (`hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`): Only injects instruction when review belongs to current session and auto-apply hasn't been attempted.

**Tests**: 8 new test functions covering `ApplyAttemptedAt` serialization, `loadWingmanStateDirect`, and all `shouldSkipPendingReview` scenarios.

+
+// loadWingmanStateDirect reads the wingman state file directly from a known
+// path under repoRoot. Returns nil if the file doesn't exist or can't be parsed.
+func loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot string) *WingmanState {
+	statePath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanStateFile)
+	data, err := os.ReadFile(statePath) //nolint:gosec // path is repo-relative constant
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	var state WingmanState
+	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &state); err != nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	return &state
+}
+
+// newWingmanApplyCmd returns a hidden subcommand used by the stop hook to
+// apply a pending REVIEW.md via claude --continue in a detached subprocess.
+func newWingmanApplyCmd() *cobra.Command {
+	return &cobra.Command{
+		Use:    "__apply",
+		Hidden: true,
+		Args:   cobra.ExactArgs(1),
+		RunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
+			return runWingmanApply(args[0])
+		},
+	}
+}
+
+// shouldSkipPendingReview checks whether a pending REVIEW.md should prevent
+// a new review from being triggered. It cleans up stale/orphaned reviews.
+//
+// Returns true only when REVIEW.md exists AND belongs to the current session
+// AND is younger than wingmanStaleReviewTTL.
+func shouldSkipPendingReview(repoRoot, currentSessionID string) bool {
+	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
+	if _, err := os.Stat(reviewPath); err != nil {
+		return false // No REVIEW.md, don't skip
+	}
+
+	// REVIEW.md exists — check state to determine if it's current or stale
+	state := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)
+	if state == nil {
+		// Orphan: REVIEW.md without state file — clean up
+		_ = os.Remove(reviewPath)
+		return false
+	}
+
+	if state.SessionID != currentSessionID {
+		// Different session — stale review, clean up
+		_ = os.Remove(reviewPath)
+		return false
+	}
+
+	if time.Since(state.ReviewedAt) > wingmanStaleReviewTTL {
+		// Same session but too old — clean up
+		_ = os.Remove(reviewPath)
+		return false
+	}
+
+	return true // Current session, fresh review — skip
+}
