```
    25→
    26→const (
    27→	// wingmanInitialDelay is how long to wait before starting the review,
    28→	// letting the agent turn fully settle.
    29→	wingmanInitialDelay = 10 * time.Second
    30→
    31→	// wingmanReviewModel is the Claude model used for reviews.
    32→	wingmanReviewModel = "sonnet"
    33→
    34→	// wingmanGitTimeout is the timeout for git diff operations.
    35→	wingmanGitTimeout = 60 * time.Second
    36→
    37→	// wingmanReviewTimeout is the timeout for the claude --print review call.
    38→	wingmanReviewTimeout = 5 * time.Minute
    39→
    40→	// wingmanApplyTimeout is the timeout for the claude --continue auto-apply call.
    41→	wingmanApplyTimeout = 15 * time.Minute
    42→)
    43→
    44→// wingmanCLIResponse represents the JSON response from the Claude CLI --output-format json.
    45→type wingmanCLIResponse struct {
    46→	Result string `json:"result"`
    47→}
    48→
    49→func newWingmanReviewCmd() *cobra.Command {
    50→	return &cobra.Command{
    51→		Use:    "__review",
    52→		Hidden: true,
    53→		Args:   cobra.ExactArgs(1),
    54→		RunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
    55→			return runWingmanReview(args[0])
    56→		},
    57→	}
    58→}
    59→
    60→func runWingmanReview(payloadPath string) error {
    61→	wingmanLog("review process started (pid=%d)", os.Getpid())
    62→	wingmanLog("reading payload from %s", payloadPath)
    63→
    64→	// Read payload from file (avoids OS argv limits with large payloads)
    65→	payloadJSON, err := os.ReadFile(payloadPath) //nolint:gosec // path is from our own spawn
    66→	if err != nil {
    67→		wingmanLog("ERROR reading payload: %v", err)
    68→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read payload file: %w", err)
    69→	}
    70→	// Clean up payload file after reading
    71→	_ = os.Remove(payloadPath)
    72→
    73→	var payload WingmanPayload
    74→	if err := json.Unmarshal(payloadJSON, &payload); err != nil {
    75→		wingmanLog("ERROR unmarshaling payload: %v", err)
    76→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal payload: %w", err)
    77→	}
    78→
    79→	repoRoot := payload.RepoRoot
    80→	if repoRoot == "" {
    81→		wingmanLog("ERROR repo_root is empty in payload")
    82→		return errors.New("repo_root is required in payload")
    83→	}
    84→
    85→	totalFiles := len(payload.ModifiedFiles) + len(payload.NewFiles) + len(payload.DeletedFiles)
    86→	wingmanLog("session=%s repo=%s base_commit=%s files=%d",
    87→		payload.SessionID, repoRoot, payload.BaseCommit, totalFiles)
    88→
    89→	// Clean up lock file when review completes (regardless of success/failure)
    90→	lockPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanLockFile)
    91→	defer func() {
    92→		if err := os.Remove(lockPath); err != nil && !errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
    93→			wingmanLog("WARNING: failed to remove lock file: %v", err)
    94→		} else {
    95→			wingmanLog("lock file removed")
    96→		}
    97→	}()
    98→
    99→	// Initial delay: let the agent turn fully settle
   100→	wingmanLog("waiting %s for agent turn to settle", wingmanInitialDelay)
   101→	time.Sleep(wingmanInitialDelay)
   102→
   103→	// Compute diff using the base commit captured at trigger time
   104→	wingmanLog("computing diff against %s", payload.BaseCommit)
   105→	diffStart := time.Now()
   106→	diff, err := computeDiff(repoRoot, payload.BaseCommit)
   107→	if err != nil {
   108→		wingmanLog("ERROR computing diff: %v", err)
   109→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to compute diff: %w", err)
   110→	}
   111→
   112→	if strings.TrimSpace(diff) == "" {
   113→		wingmanLog("no changes found in diff, exiting")
   114→		return nil // No changes to review
   115→	}
   116→	wingmanLog("diff computed: %d bytes in %s", len(diff), time.Since(diffStart).Round(time.Millisecond))
   117→
   118→	// Build file list for the prompt
   119→	allFiles := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))
   120→	for _, f := range payload.ModifiedFiles {
   121→		allFiles = append(allFiles, f+" (modified)")
   122→	}
   123→	for _, f := range payload.NewFiles {
   124→		allFiles = append(allFiles, f+" (new)")
   125→	}
   126→	for _, f := range payload.DeletedFiles {
   127→		allFiles = append(allFiles, f+" (deleted)")
   128→	}
   129→	fileList := strings.Join(allFiles, ", ")
   130→
   131→	// Read session context from checkpoint data (best-effort)
   132→	sessionContext := readSessionContext(repoRoot, payload.SessionID)
   133→	if sessionContext != "" {
   134→		wingmanLog("session context loaded: %d bytes", len(sessionContext))
   135→	}
   136→
   137→	// Build review prompt
   138→	prompt := buildReviewPrompt(payload.Prompts, payload.CommitMessage, sessionContext, payload.SessionID, fileList, diff)
   139→	wingmanLog("review prompt built: %d bytes", len(prompt))
   140→
   141→	// Call Claude CLI for review
   142→	wingmanLog("calling claude CLI (model=%s, timeout=%s)", wingmanReviewModel, wingmanReviewTimeout)
   143→	reviewStart := time.Now()
   144→	reviewText, err := callClaudeForReview(repoRoot, prompt)
   145→	if err != nil {
   146→		wingmanLog("ERROR claude CLI failed after %s: %v", time.Since(reviewStart).Round(time.Millisecond), err)
   147→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get review from Claude: %w", err)
   148→	}
   149→	wingmanLog("review received: %d bytes in %s", len(reviewText), time.Since(reviewStart).Round(time.Millisecond))
   150→
   151→	// Write REVIEW.md
   152→	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
   153→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(reviewPath), 0o750); err != nil {
   154→		wingmanLog("ERROR creating directory: %v", err)
   155→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create directory: %w", err)
   156→	}
   157→	//nolint:gosec // G306: review file is not secrets
   158→	if err := os.WriteFile(reviewPath, []byte(reviewText), 0o644); err != nil {
   159→		wingmanLog("ERROR writing REVIEW.md: %v", err)
   160→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write REVIEW.md: %w", err)
   161→	}
   162→	wingmanLog("REVIEW.md written to %s", reviewPath)
   163→
   164→	// Update dedup state — write directly to known path instead of using
   165→	// os.Chdir (which mutates process-wide state).
   166→	allFilePaths := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))
   167→	allFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.ModifiedFiles...)
   168→	allFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.NewFiles...)
   169→	allFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.DeletedFiles...)
   170→
   171→	saveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot, &WingmanState{
   172→		SessionID:     payload.SessionID,
   173→		FilesHash:     computeFilesHash(allFilePaths),
   174→		ReviewedAt:    time.Now(),
   175→		ReviewApplied: false,
   176→	})
   177→	wingmanLog("dedup state saved")
   178→
   179→	// Check if session is idle right now (rare — user usually starts typing
   180→	// during the 10s settle + review time). If idle, apply immediately.
   181→	// Otherwise, the stop hook will handle auto-apply when the current turn ends.
   182→	idle := isSessionIdle(repoRoot, payload.SessionID)
   183→	wingmanLog("session idle check: idle=%v", idle)
   184→
   185→	if !idle {
   186→		wingmanLog("session is active, stop hook will handle auto-apply when turn ends")
   187→		return nil
   188→	}
   189→
   190→	// Mark apply as attempted before triggering (retry prevention)
   191→	now := time.Now()
   192→	state := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)
   193→	if state != nil {
   194→		state.ApplyAttemptedAt = &now
   195→		saveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot, state)
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	wingmanLog("triggering auto-apply via claude --continue (session idle)")
   199→	applyStart := time.Now()
   200→	if err := triggerAutoApply(repoRoot); err != nil {
   201→		wingmanLog("ERROR auto-apply failed after %s: %v", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond), err)
   202→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to trigger auto-apply: %w", err)
   203→	}
   204→	wingmanLog("auto-apply completed in %s", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond))
   205→
   206→	return nil
   207→}
   208→
   209→// computeDiff gets the full branch diff for review. It diffs the current HEAD
   210→// against the merge base with the default branch (main/master), giving the
   211→// reviewer a holistic view of all branch changes rather than just one commit.
   212→func computeDiff(repoRoot string, _ string) (string, error) {
   213→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), wingmanGitTimeout)
   214→	defer cancel()
   215→
   216→	// Find the merge base with the default branch for a holistic branch diff.
   217→	mergeBase := findMergeBase(ctx, repoRoot)
   218→	if mergeBase != "" {
   219→		wingmanLog("using merge-base %s for branch diff", mergeBase)
   220→		diff, err := gitDiff(ctx, repoRoot, mergeBase)
   221→		if err == nil && strings.TrimSpace(diff) != "" {
   222→			return diff, nil
   223→		}
   224→		// Fall through to HEAD diff if merge-base diff fails or is empty
   225→	}
   226→
   227→	// Fallback: diff uncommitted changes against HEAD
   228→	wingmanLog("falling back to HEAD diff")
   229→	diff, err := gitDiff(ctx, repoRoot, "HEAD")
   230→	if err != nil {
   231→		return "", fmt.Errorf("git diff failed: %w", err)
   232→	}
   233→
   234→	// If no uncommitted changes, try the latest commit itself
   235→	if strings.TrimSpace(diff) == "" {
   236→		diff, err = gitDiff(ctx, repoRoot, "HEAD~1", "HEAD")
   237→		if err != nil {
   238→			return "", nil
   239→		}
   240→	}
   241→
   242→	return diff, nil
   243→}
   244→
   245→// findMergeBase returns the merge-base commit between HEAD and the default
   246→// branch (tries main, then master). Returns empty string if not found.
   247→func findMergeBase(ctx context.Context, repoRoot string) string {
   248→	for _, branch := range []string{"main", "master"} {
   249→		cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "merge-base", branch, "HEAD") //nolint:gosec // branch is from hardcoded slice
   250→		cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   251→		out, err := cmd.Output()
   252→		if err == nil {
   253→			return strings.TrimSpace(string(out))
   254→		}
   255→	}
   256→	return ""
   257→}
   258→
   259→// gitDiff runs git diff with the given args and returns stdout.
   260→func gitDiff(ctx context.Context, repoRoot string, args ...string) (string, error) {
   261→	fullArgs := append([]string{"diff"}, args...)
   262→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", fullArgs...) //nolint:gosec // args are from internal logic
   263→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   264→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   265→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   266→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   267→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   268→		return "", fmt.Errorf("git diff %v: %w (stderr: %s)", args, err, stderr.String())
   269→	}
   270→	return stdout.String(), nil
   271→}
   272→
   273→// callClaudeForReview calls the claude CLI to perform the review.
   274→// repoRoot is the repository root so the reviewer can access the full codebase.
   275→func callClaudeForReview(repoRoot, prompt string) (string, error) {
   276→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), wingmanReviewTimeout)
   277→	defer cancel()
   278→
   279→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "claude",
   280→		"--print",
   281→		"--output-format", "json",
   282→		"--model", wingmanReviewModel,
   283→		"--setting-sources", "",
   284→		// Grant read-only tool access so the reviewer can inspect source files
   285→		// beyond just the diff. Permission bypass is safe here since tools are
   286→		// restricted to read-only operations.
   287→		"--allowedTools", "Read,Glob,Grep",
   288→		"--permission-mode", "bypassPermissions",
   289→	)
   290→
   291→	// Run from repo root so the reviewer can read source files for context.
   292→	// Loop-breaking is handled by --setting-sources "" (disables hooks) and
   293→	// wingmanStripGitEnv (prevents git index pollution).
   294→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   295→	cmd.Env = wingmanStripGitEnv(os.Environ())
   296→
   297→	cmd.Stdin = strings.NewReader(prompt)
   298→
   299→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   300→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   301→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   302→
   303→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   304→		var execErr *exec.Error
   305→		if errors.As(err, &execErr) {
   306→			return "", fmt.Errorf("claude CLI not found: %w", err)
   307→		}
   308→		var exitErr *exec.ExitError
   309→		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
   310→			return "", fmt.Errorf("claude CLI failed (exit %d): %s", exitErr.ExitCode(), stderr.String())
   311→		}
   312→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to run claude CLI: %w", err)
   313→	}
   314→
   315→	// Parse the CLI response
   316→	var cliResponse wingmanCLIResponse
   317→	if err := json.Unmarshal(stdout.Bytes(), &cliResponse); err != nil {
   318→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to parse claude CLI response: %w", err)
   319→	}
   320→
   321→	return cliResponse.Result, nil
   322→}
   323→
   324→// readSessionContext reads the context.md file from the session's checkpoint
   325→// metadata directory. Returns empty string if unavailable.
   326→func readSessionContext(repoRoot, sessionID string) string {
   327→	if sessionID == "" {
   328→		return ""
   329→	}
   330→	contextPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".entire", "metadata", sessionID, "context.md")
   331→	data, err := os.ReadFile(contextPath) //nolint:gosec // path constructed from repoRoot + session ID
   332→	if err != nil {
   333→		return ""
   334→	}
   335→	return string(data)
   336→}
   337→
   338→// isSessionIdle checks if any session is in the IDLE phase by reading
   339→// session state files directly from the repo. Uses repoRoot to locate
   340→// .git/entire-sessions/ without relying on the current working directory
   341→// (which is "/" in detached subprocesses).
   342→func isSessionIdle(repoRoot, sessionID string) bool {
   343→	sessDir := findSessionStateDir(repoRoot)
   344→	if sessDir == "" {
   345→		wingmanLog("cannot find session state dir for %s", repoRoot)
   346→		return false
   347→	}
   348→
   349→	// Fast path: check the specific session
   350→	if sessionID != "" {
   351→		if phase := readSessionPhase(sessDir, sessionID); phase == string(session.PhaseIdle) {
   352→			return true
   353→		}
   354→	}
   355→
   356→	// Slow path: check if ANY session is idle
   357→	// (covers session restart, ended sessions, etc.)
   358→	entries, err := os.ReadDir(sessDir)
   359→	if err != nil {
   360→		return false
   361→	}
   362→	const maxSessionCheck = 50
   363→	checked := 0
   364→	for _, entry := range entries {
   365→		if checked >= maxSessionCheck {
   366→			wingmanLog("hit session check limit (%d), assuming not idle", maxSessionCheck)
   367→			return false
   368→		}
   369→		name := entry.Name()
   370→		if !strings.HasSuffix(name, ".json") {
   371→			continue
   372→		}
   373→		sid := strings.TrimSuffix(name, ".json")
   374→		if sid == sessionID {
   375→			continue // Already checked
   376→		}
   377→		checked++
   378→		if phase := readSessionPhase(sessDir, sid); phase == string(session.PhaseIdle) {
   379→			wingmanLog("found idle session %s (original session %s was not idle)", sid, sessionID)
   380→			return true
   381→		}
   382→	}
   383→
   384→	return false
   385→}
   386→
   387→// findSessionStateDir locates the .git/entire-sessions/ directory by
   388→// reading .git to handle both normal repos and worktrees.
   389→func findSessionStateDir(repoRoot string) string {
   390→	gitPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".git")
   391→	info, err := os.Stat(gitPath)
   392→	if err != nil {
   393→		return ""
   394→	}
   395→
   396→	var gitDir string
   397→	if info.IsDir() {
   398→		// Normal repo: .git is a directory
   399→		gitDir = gitPath
   400→	} else {
   401→		// Worktree: .git is a file containing "gitdir: <path>"
   402→		data, readErr := os.ReadFile(gitPath) //nolint:gosec // path from repoRoot
   403→		if readErr != nil {
   404→			return ""
   405→		}
   406→		content := strings.TrimSpace(string(data))
   407→		if !strings.HasPrefix(content, "gitdir: ") {
   408→			return ""
   409→		}
   410→		gitDir = strings.TrimPrefix(content, "gitdir: ")
   411→		if !filepath.IsAbs(gitDir) {
   412→			gitDir = filepath.Join(repoRoot, gitDir)
   413→		}
   414→		// For worktrees, session state is in the common dir
   415→		// .git/worktrees/<name> → ../../ is the common .git dir
   416→		commonDir := filepath.Join(gitDir, "..", "..")
   417→		gitDir = filepath.Clean(commonDir)
   418→	}
   419→
   420→	sessDir := filepath.Join(gitDir, "entire-sessions")
   421→	if _, statErr := os.Stat(sessDir); statErr != nil {
   422→		return ""
   423→	}
   424→	return sessDir
   425→}
   426→
   427→// readSessionPhase reads just the phase field from a session state JSON file.
   428→func readSessionPhase(sessDir, sessionID string) string {
   429→	data, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(sessDir, sessionID+".json")) //nolint:gosec // sessDir is from git internals
   430→	if err != nil {
   431→		return ""
   432→	}
   433→	var partial struct {
   434→		Phase string `json:"phase"`
   435→	}
   436→	if json.Unmarshal(data, &partial) != nil {
   437→		return ""
   438→	}
   439→	return partial.Phase
   440→}
   441→
   442→// runWingmanApply is the entrypoint for the __apply subcommand, spawned by the
   443→// stop hook when a pending REVIEW.md is detected. It re-checks preconditions
   444→// and triggers claude --continue to apply the review.
   445→func runWingmanApply(repoRoot string) error {
   446→	wingmanLog("apply process started (pid=%d)", os.Getpid())
   447→
   448→	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
   449→	if !fileExists(reviewPath) {
   450→		wingmanLog("no REVIEW.md found, nothing to apply")
   451→		return nil
   452→	}
   453→
   454→	// Retry prevention: check if apply was already attempted for this review
   455→	state := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)
   456→	if state != nil && state.ApplyAttemptedAt != nil {
   457→		wingmanLog("apply already attempted at %s, skipping", state.ApplyAttemptedAt.Format(time.RFC3339))
   458→		return nil
   459→	}
   460→
   461→	// Re-check session is still idle (user may have typed during spawn delay)
   462→	if state != nil && state.SessionID != "" {
   463→		if !isSessionIdle(repoRoot, state.SessionID) {
   464→			wingmanLog("session became active during spawn, aborting (next stop hook will retry)")
   465→			return nil
   466→		}
   467→	}
   468→
   469→	// Mark apply as attempted BEFORE triggering
   470→	if state != nil {
   471→		now := time.Now()
   472→		state.ApplyAttemptedAt = &now
   473→		saveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot, state)
   474→	}
   475→
   476→	wingmanLog("triggering auto-apply via claude --continue")
   477→	applyStart := time.Now()
   478→	if err := triggerAutoApply(repoRoot); err != nil {
   479→		wingmanLog("ERROR auto-apply failed after %s: %v", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond), err)
   480→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to trigger auto-apply: %w", err)
   481→	}
   482→	wingmanLog("auto-apply completed in %s", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond))
   483→
   484→	return nil
   485→}
   486→
   487→// triggerAutoApply spawns claude --continue to apply the review suggestions.
   488→func triggerAutoApply(repoRoot string) error {
   489→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), wingmanApplyTimeout)
   490→	defer cancel()
   491→
   492→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "claude",
   493→		"--continue",
   494→		"--print",
   495→		"--setting-sources", "",
   496→		// Auto-accept edits so the agent can modify files and delete REVIEW.md
   497→		// without requiring user consent (this runs in a background process).
   498→		"--permission-mode", "acceptEdits",
   499→		wingmanApplyInstruction,
   500→	)
   501→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   502→	// Strip GIT_* env vars to prevent hook interference, and set
   503→	// ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY=1 so git hooks (post-commit) know not to
   504→	// trigger another wingman review (prevents infinite recursion).
   505→	env := wingmanStripGitEnv(os.Environ())
   506→	env = append(env, "ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY=1")
   507→	cmd.Env = env
   508→
   509→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   510→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   511→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   512→
   513→	return cmd.Run() //nolint:wrapcheck // caller wraps
   514→}
   515→
   516→// wingmanStripGitEnv returns a copy of env with all GIT_* variables removed.
   517→// This prevents a subprocess from discovering or modifying the parent's git repo
   518→// when we want isolation (e.g., running claude --print for review).
   519→func wingmanStripGitEnv(env []string) []string {
   520→	filtered := make([]string, 0, len(env))
   521→	for _, e := range env {
   522→		if !strings.HasPrefix(e, "GIT_") {
   523→			filtered = append(filtered, e)
   524→		}
   525→	}
   526→	return filtered
   527→}
   528→
   529→// saveWingmanStateDirect writes the wingman state file directly to a known path
   530→// under repoRoot, avoiding os.Chdir (which mutates process-wide state).
   531→func saveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot string, state *WingmanState) {
   532→	statePath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanStateFile)
   533→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(statePath), 0o750); err != nil {
   534→		return
   535→	}
   536→	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(state, "", "  ")
   537→	if err != nil {
   538→		return
   539→	}
   540→	//nolint:gosec,errcheck // G306: state file is config, not secrets; best-effort write
   541→	_ = os.WriteFile(statePath, data, 0o644)
   542→}
   543→
```

```
    24→// hookInputData contains parsed hook input and session identifiers.
    25→type hookInputData struct {
    26→	agent     agent.Agent
    27→	input     *agent.HookInput
    28→	sessionID string
    29→}
    30→
    31→// parseAndLogHookInput parses the hook input and sets up logging context.
    32→func parseAndLogHookInput() (*hookInputData, error) {
    33→	// Get the agent from the hook command context (e.g., "entire hooks claude-code ...")
    34→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
    35→	if err != nil {
    36→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
    37→	}
    38→
    39→	// Parse hook input using agent interface
    40→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookUserPromptSubmit, os.Stdin)
    41→	if err != nil {
    42→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
    43→	}
    44→
    45→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
    46→	logging.Info(logCtx, "user-prompt-submit",
    47→		slog.String("hook", "user-prompt-submit"),
    48→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
    49→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
    50→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
    51→	)
    52→
    53→	sessionID := input.SessionID
    54→	if sessionID == "" {
    55→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
    56→	}
    57→
    58→	// Get the Entire session ID, preferring the persisted value to handle midnight boundary
    59→	return &hookInputData{
    60→		agent:     ag,
    61→		input:     input,
    62→		sessionID: sessionID,
    63→	}, nil
    64→}
    65→
    66→// captureInitialState captures the initial state on user prompt submit.
    67→func captureInitialState() error {
    68→	// Parse hook input and setup logging
    69→	hookData, err := parseAndLogHookInput()
    70→	if err != nil {
    71→		return err
    72→	}
    73→
    74→	// CLI captures state directly (including transcript position)
    75→	if err := CapturePrePromptState(hookData.sessionID, hookData.input.SessionRef); err != nil {
    76→		return err
    77→	}
    78→
    79→	// If a wingman review is pending, inject it as additionalContext so the
    80→	// agent addresses it BEFORE the user's request. This is the primary
    81→	// delivery mechanism — the agent sees the instruction as mandatory context.
    82→	if settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {
    83→		repoRoot, rootErr := paths.RepoRoot()
    84→		if rootErr == nil {
    85→			wingmanLogCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "wingman")
    86→			if _, statErr := os.Stat(filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)); statErr == nil {
    87→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review available: .entire/REVIEW.md — injecting into context\n")
    88→				logging.Info(wingmanLogCtx, "wingman injecting review instruction on prompt-submit",
    89→					slog.String("session_id", hookData.sessionID),
    90→				)
    91→				if err := outputHookResponseWithContext(wingmanApplyInstruction); err != nil {
    92→					fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Warning: failed to inject review instruction: %v\n", err)
    93→				}
    94→			}
    95→
    96→			// Log if a review is currently in progress (lock file exists)
    97→			lockPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanLockFile)
    98→			if _, statErr := os.Stat(lockPath); statErr == nil {
    99→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review in progress...\n")
   100→				logging.Info(wingmanLogCtx, "wingman review in progress",
   101→					slog.String("session_id", hookData.sessionID),
   102→				)
   103→			}
   104→		}
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	// If strategy implements SessionInitializer, call it to initialize session state
   108→	strat := GetStrategy()
   109→	if initializer, ok := strat.(strategy.SessionInitializer); ok {
   110→		agentType := hookData.agent.Type()
   111→		if err := initializer.InitializeSession(hookData.sessionID, agentType, hookData.input.SessionRef, hookData.input.UserPrompt); err != nil {
   112→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to initialize session state: %v\n", err)
   113→		}
   114→	}
   115→
   116→	return nil
   117→}

   119→// commitWithMetadata commits the session changes with metadata.
   120→func commitWithMetadata() error { //nolint:maintidx // already present in codebase
   121→	// Get the agent for hook input parsing and session ID transformation
   122→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   123→	if err != nil {
   124→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   125→	}
   126→
   127→	// Parse hook input using agent interface
   128→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookStop, os.Stdin)
   129→	if err != nil {
   130→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   131→	}
   132→
   133→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   134→	logging.Info(logCtx, "stop",
   135→		slog.String("hook", "stop"),
   136→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   137→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   138→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
   139→	)
   140→
   141→	sessionID := input.SessionID
   142→	if sessionID == "" {
   143→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
   144→	}
   145→
   146→	transcriptPath := input.SessionRef
   147→	if transcriptPath == "" || !fileExists(transcriptPath) {
   148→		return fmt.Errorf("transcript file not found or empty: %s", transcriptPath)
   149→	}
   150→
   151→	// Early check: bail out quickly if the repo has no commits yet.
   152→	// Without this, the function does a lot of work (transcript copy, prompt extraction, etc.)
   153→	// before eventually failing deep in strategy.SaveChanges().
   154→	if repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository(); err == nil && strategy.IsEmptyRepository(repo) {
   155→		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping checkpoint. Will activate after first commit.")
   156→		return NewSilentError(strategy.ErrEmptyRepository)
   157→	}
   158→
   159→	// Create session metadata folder using the entire session ID (preserves original date on resume)
   160→	// Use AbsPath to ensure we create at repo root, not relative to cwd
   161→	sessionDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   162→	sessionDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(sessionDir)
   163→	if err != nil {
   164→		sessionDirAbs = sessionDir // Fallback to relative
   165→	}
   166→	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessionDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
   167→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session directory: %w", err)
   168→	}
   169→
   170→	// Wait for Claude Code to flush the transcript file.
   171→	// The stop hook fires before the transcript is fully written to disk.
   172→	// We poll for our own hook_progress sentinel entry in the file tail,
   173→	// which guarantees all prior entries have been flushed.
   174→	waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath, time.Now())
   175→
   176→	// Copy transcript
   177→	logFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   178→	if err := copyFile(transcriptPath, logFile); err != nil {
   179→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to copy transcript: %w", err)
   180→	}
   181→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Copied transcript to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.TranscriptFileName)
   182→
   183→	// Load pre-prompt state (captured on UserPromptSubmit)
   184→	// Needed for transcript offset and file change detection
   185→	preState, err := LoadPrePromptState(sessionID)
   186→	if err != nil {
   187→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   188→	}
   189→
   190→	// Determine transcript offset: prefer pre-prompt state, fall back to session state.
   191→	// Pre-prompt state has the offset when the transcript path was available at prompt time.
   192→	// Session state has the offset updated after each successful checkpoint save (auto-commit).
   193→	var transcriptOffset int
   194→	if preState != nil && preState.StepTranscriptStart > 0 {
   195→		transcriptOffset = preState.StepTranscriptStart
   196→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state found: parsing transcript from line %d\n", transcriptOffset)
   197→	} else {
   198→		// Fall back to session state (e.g., auto-commit strategy updates it after each save)
   199→		sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   200→		if loadErr != nil {
   201→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   202→		}
   203→		if sessionState != nil && sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart > 0 {
   204→			transcriptOffset = sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart
   205→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session state found: parsing transcript from line %d\n", transcriptOffset)
   206→		}
   207→	}
   208→
   209→	// Parse transcript (optionally from offset for strategies that track transcript position)
   210→	// When transcriptOffset > 0, only parse NEW lines since the last checkpoint
   211→	var transcript []transcriptLine
   212→	var totalLines int
   213→	if transcriptOffset > 0 {
   214→		// Parse only NEW lines since last checkpoint
   215→		transcript, totalLines, err = parseTranscriptFromLine(transcriptPath, transcriptOffset)
   216→		if err != nil {
   217→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript from line %d: %w", transcriptOffset, err)
   218→		}
   219→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Parsed %d new transcript lines (total: %d)\n", len(transcript), totalLines)
   220→	} else {
   221→		// First prompt or no session state - parse entire transcript
   222→		// Use parseTranscriptFromLine with offset 0 to also get totalLines
   223→		transcript, totalLines, err = parseTranscriptFromLine(transcriptPath, 0)
   224→		if err != nil {
   225→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   226→		}
   227→	}
   228→
   229→	// Extract all prompts since last checkpoint for prompt file
   230→	allPrompts := extractUserPrompts(transcript)
   231→	promptFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.PromptFileName)
   232→	promptContent := strings.Join(allPrompts, "\n\n---\n\n")
   233→	if err := os.WriteFile(promptFile, []byte(promptContent), 0o600); err != nil {
   234→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write prompt file: %w", err)
   235→	}
   236→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted %d prompt(s) to: %s\n", len(allPrompts), sessionDir+"/"+paths.PromptFileName)
   237→
   238→	// Extract summary
   239→	summaryFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.SummaryFileName)
   240→	summary := extractLastAssistantMessage(transcript)
   241→	if err := os.WriteFile(summaryFile, []byte(summary), 0o600); err != nil {
   242→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write summary file: %w", err)
   243→	}
   244→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted summary to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.SummaryFileName)
   245→
   246→	// Get modified files from transcript
   247→	modifiedFiles := extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   248→
   249→	// Generate commit message from last user prompt
   250→	lastPrompt := ""
   251→	if len(allPrompts) > 0 {
   252→		lastPrompt = allPrompts[len(allPrompts)-1]
   253→	}
   254→	commitMessage := generateCommitMessage(lastPrompt)
   255→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Using commit message: %s\n", commitMessage)
   256→
   257→	// Get repo root for path conversion (not cwd, since Claude may be in a subdirectory)
   258→	// Using cwd would filter out files in sibling directories (paths starting with ..)
   259→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   260→	if err != nil {
   261→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   262→	}
   263→
   264→	if preState != nil {
   265→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state: %d pre-existing untracked files\n", len(preState.UntrackedFiles))
   266→	}
   267→
   268→	// Compute new and deleted files (single git status call)
   269→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preState.PreUntrackedFiles())
   270→	if err != nil {
   271→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   272→	}
   273→
   274→	// Filter and normalize all paths (CLI responsibility)
   275→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   276→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   277→	if changes != nil {
   278→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   279→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   280→	}
   281→
   282→	// Check if there are any changes to commit
   283→	totalChanges := len(relModifiedFiles) + len(relNewFiles) + len(relDeletedFiles)
   284→	if totalChanges == 0 {
   285→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No files were modified during this session\n")
   286→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipping commit\n")
   287→		// Still transition phase even when skipping commit — the turn is ending.
   288→		transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   289→		// Auto-apply pending wingman review even when no file changes this turn
   290→		triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending(repoRoot)
   291→		// Clean up state even when skipping
   292→		if err := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); err != nil {
   293→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   294→		}
   295→		return nil
   296→	}
   297→
   298→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files modified during session (%d):\n", len(relModifiedFiles))
   299→	for _, file := range relModifiedFiles {
   300→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   301→	}
   302→	if len(relNewFiles) > 0 {
   303→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "New files created (%d):\n", len(relNewFiles))
   304→		for _, file := range relNewFiles {
   305→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  + %s\n", file)
   306→		}
   307→	}
   308→	if len(relDeletedFiles) > 0 {
   309→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files deleted (%d):\n", len(relDeletedFiles))
   310→		for _, file := range relDeletedFiles {
   311→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   312→		}
   313→	}
   314→
   315→	// Create context file before saving changes
   316→	contextFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.ContextFileName)
   317→	if err := createContextFileMinimal(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID, promptFile, summaryFile, transcript); err != nil {
   318→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create context file: %w", err)
   319→	}
   320→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created context file: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.ContextFileName)
   321→
   322→	// Get git author from local/global config
   323→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   324→	if err != nil {
   325→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   326→	}
   327→
   328→	// Get the configured strategy
   329→	strat := GetStrategy()
   330→
   331→	// Ensure strategy setup is in place (auto-installs git hook, gitignore, etc. if needed)
   332→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   333→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   334→	}
   335→
   336→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   337→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   338→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   339→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   340→	}
   341→
   342→	// Get transcript position from pre-prompt state (captured at step/turn start)
   343→	var transcriptIdentifierAtStart string
   344→	var transcriptLinesAtStart int
   345→	if preState != nil {
   346→		transcriptIdentifierAtStart = preState.LastTranscriptIdentifier
   347→		transcriptLinesAtStart = preState.StepTranscriptStart
   348→	}
   349→
   350→	// Calculate token usage for this checkpoint (Claude Code specific)
   351→	var tokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage
   352→	if transcriptPath != "" {
   353→		// Subagents are stored in a subagents/ directory next to the main transcript
   354→		subagentsDir := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(transcriptPath), sessionID, "subagents")
   355→		usage, err := claudecode.CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptPath, transcriptLinesAtStart, subagentsDir)
   356→		if err != nil {
   357→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage: %v\n", err)
   358→		} else {
   359→			tokenUsage = usage
   360→		}
   361→	}
   362→
   363→	// Build fully-populated save context and delegate to strategy
   364→	ctx := strategy.SaveContext{
   365→		SessionID:                sessionID,
   366→		ModifiedFiles:            relModifiedFiles,
   367→		NewFiles:                 relNewFiles,
   368→		DeletedFiles:             relDeletedFiles,
   369→		MetadataDir:              sessionDir,
   370→		MetadataDirAbs:           sessionDirAbs,
   371→		CommitMessage:            commitMessage,
   372→		TranscriptPath:           transcriptPath,
   373→		AuthorName:               author.Name,
   374→		AuthorEmail:              author.Email,
   375→		AgentType:                agentType,
   376→		StepTranscriptIdentifier: transcriptIdentifierAtStart,
   377→		StepTranscriptStart:      transcriptLinesAtStart,
   378→		TokenUsage:               tokenUsage,
   379→	}
   380→
   381→	if err := strat.SaveChanges(ctx); err != nil {
   382→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save changes: %w", err)
   383→	}
   384→
   385→	// Update session state with new transcript position for strategies that create
   386→	// commits on the active branch (auto-commit strategy). This prevents parsing old transcript
   387→	// lines on subsequent checkpoints.
   388→	// Note: Shadow strategy tracks transcript position per-step via StepTranscriptStart in
   389→	// pre-prompt state, but doesn't advance CheckpointTranscriptStart in session state because
   390→	// its checkpoints accumulate all files touched across the entire session.
   391→	if strat.Name() == strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit {
   392→		// Load session state for updating transcript position
   393→		sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   394→		if loadErr != nil {
   395→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   396→		}
   397→		// Create session state lazily if it doesn't exist (backward compat for resumed sessions
   398→		// or if InitializeSession was never called/failed)
   399→		if sessionState == nil {
   400→			sessionState = &strategy.SessionState{
   401→				SessionID: sessionID,
   402→			}
   403→		}
   404→		sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = totalLines
   405→		sessionState.StepCount++
   406→		if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); updateErr != nil {
   407→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", updateErr)
   408→		} else {
   409→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Updated session state: transcript position=%d, checkpoint=%d\n",
   410→				totalLines, sessionState.StepCount)
   411→		}
   412→	}
   413→
   414→	// Fire EventTurnEnd to transition session phase (all strategies).
   415→	// This moves ACTIVE → IDLE or ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE.
   416→	// For ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE, HandleTurnEnd dispatches ActionCondense.
   417→	transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   418→
   419→	// Trigger wingman review for auto-commit strategy (commit already happened
   420→	// in SaveChanges). Manual-commit triggers wingman from the git post-commit hook
   421→	// instead, since the user commits manually.
   422→	if totalChanges > 0 && strat.Name() == strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit && settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {
   423→		triggerWingmanReview(WingmanPayload{
   424→			SessionID:     sessionID,
   425→			RepoRoot:      repoRoot,
   426→			ModifiedFiles: relModifiedFiles,
   427→			NewFiles:      relNewFiles,
   428→			DeletedFiles:  relDeletedFiles,
   429→			Prompts:       allPrompts,
   430→			CommitMessage: commitMessage,
   431→		})
   432→	}
   433→
   434→	// Auto-apply pending wingman review on turn end
   435→	triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending(repoRoot)
   436→
   437→	// Clean up pre-prompt state (CLI responsibility)
   438→	if err := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); err != nil {
   439→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   440→	}
   441→
   442→	return nil
   443→}
   444→
   445→// handleClaudeCodePostTodo handles the PostToolUse[TodoWrite] hook for subagent checkpoints.
   446→// Creates a checkpoint if we're in a subagent context (active pre-task file exists).
   447→// Skips silently if not in subagent context (main agent).
   448→func handleClaudeCodePostTodo() error {
   449→	input, err := parseSubagentCheckpointHookInput(os.Stdin)
   450→	if err != nil {
   451→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PostToolUse[TodoWrite] input: %w", err)
   452→	}
   453→
   454→	// Get agent for logging context
   455→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   456→	if err != nil {
   457→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   458→	}
   459→
   460→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   461→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-todo",
   462→		slog.String("hook", "post-todo"),
   463→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   464→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   465→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.TranscriptPath),
   466→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   467→	)
   468→
   469→	// Check if we're in a subagent context by looking for an active pre-task file
   470→	taskToolUseID, found := FindActivePreTaskFile()
   471→	if !found {
   472→		// Not in subagent context - this is a main agent TodoWrite, skip
   473→		return nil
   474→	}
   475→
   476→	// Skip on default branch to avoid polluting main/master history
   477→	if skip, branchName := ShouldSkipOnDefaultBranch(); skip {
   478→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping incremental checkpoint on branch '%s'\n", branchName)
   479→		return nil
   480→	}
   481→
   482→	// Detect file changes since last checkpoint
   483→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(nil)
   484→	if err != nil {
   485→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to detect changed files: %v\n", err)
   486→		return nil
   487→	}
   488→
   489→	// If no file changes, skip creating a checkpoint
   490→	if len(changes.Modified) == 0 && len(changes.New) == 0 && len(changes.Deleted) == 0 {
   491→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping incremental checkpoint\n")
   492→		return nil
   493→	}
   494→
   495→	// Get git author
   496→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   497→	if err != nil {
   498→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to get git author: %v\n", err)
   499→		return nil
   500→	}
   501→
   502→	// Get the active strategy
   503→	strat := GetStrategy()
   504→
   505→	// Ensure strategy setup is complete
   506→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   507→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   508→		return nil
   509→	}
   510→
   511→	// Get the session ID from the transcript path or input, then transform to Entire session ID
   512→	sessionID := input.SessionID
   513→	if sessionID == "" {
   514→		sessionID = paths.ExtractSessionIDFromTranscriptPath(input.TranscriptPath)
   515→	}
   516→
   517→	// Get next checkpoint sequence
   518→	seq := GetNextCheckpointSequence(sessionID, taskToolUseID)
   519→
   520→	// Extract the todo content from the tool_input.
   521→	// PostToolUse receives the NEW todo list where the just-completed work is
   522→	// marked as "completed". The last completed item is the work that was just done.
   523→	todoContent := ExtractLastCompletedTodoFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
   524→	if todoContent == "" {
   525→		// No completed items - this is likely the first TodoWrite (planning phase).
   526→		// Check if there are any todos at all to avoid duplicate messages.
   527→		todoCount := CountTodosFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
   528→		if todoCount > 0 {
   529→			// Use "Planning: N todos" format for the first TodoWrite
   530→			todoContent = fmt.Sprintf("Planning: %d todos", todoCount)
   531→		}
   532→		// If todoCount == 0, todoContent remains empty and FormatIncrementalMessage
   533→		// will fall back to "Checkpoint #N" format
   534→	}
   535→
   536→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   537→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   538→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   539→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   540→	}
   541→
   542→	// Build incremental checkpoint context
   543→	ctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{
   544→		SessionID:           sessionID,
   545→		ToolUseID:           taskToolUseID,
   546→		ModifiedFiles:       changes.Modified,
   547→		NewFiles:            changes.New,
   548→		DeletedFiles:        changes.Deleted,
   549→		TranscriptPath:      input.TranscriptPath,
   550→		AuthorName:          author.Name,
   551→		AuthorEmail:         author.Email,
   552→		IsIncremental:       true,
   553→		IncrementalSequence: seq,
   554→		IncrementalType:     input.ToolName,
   555→		IncrementalData:     input.ToolInput,
   556→		TodoContent:         todoContent,
   557→		AgentType:           agentType,
   558→	}
   559→
   560→	// Save incremental checkpoint
   561→	if err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   562→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to save incremental checkpoint: %v\n", err)
   563→		return nil
   564→	}
   565→
   566→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Created incremental checkpoint #%d for %s (task: %s)\n",
   567→		seq, input.ToolName, taskToolUseID[:min(12, len(taskToolUseID))])
   568→	return nil
   569→}
   570→
   571→// handleClaudeCodePreTask handles the PreToolUse[Task] hook
   572→func handleClaudeCodePreTask() error {
   573→	input, err := parseTaskHookInput(os.Stdin)
   574→	if err != nil {
   575→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PreToolUse[Task] input: %w", err)
   576→	}
   577→
   578→	// Get agent for logging context
   579→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   580→	if err != nil {
   581→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   582→	}
   583→
   584→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   585→	logging.Info(logCtx, "pre-task",
   586→		slog.String("hook", "pre-task"),
   587→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   588→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   589→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.TranscriptPath),
   590→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   591→	)
   592→
   593→	// Log context to stdout
   594→	logPreTaskHookContext(os.Stdout, input)
   595→
   596→	// Capture pre-task state locally (for computing new files when task completes).
   597→	// We don't create a shadow branch commit here. Commits are created during
   598→	// task completion (handleClaudeCodePostTask/handleClaudeCodePostTodo) only if the task resulted
   599→	// in file changes.
   600→	if err := CapturePreTaskState(input.ToolUseID); err != nil {
   601→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to capture pre-task state: %w", err)
   602→	}
   603→
   604→	return nil
   605→}
   606→
   607→// handleClaudeCodePostTask handles the PostToolUse[Task] hook
   608→func handleClaudeCodePostTask() error {
   609→	input, err := parsePostTaskHookInput(os.Stdin)
   610→	if err != nil {
   611→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PostToolUse[Task] input: %w", err)
   612→	}
   613→
   614→	// Extract subagent type from tool_input for logging
   615→	subagentType, taskDescription := ParseSubagentTypeAndDescription(input.ToolInput)
   616→
   617→	// Get agent for logging context
   618→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   619→	if err != nil {
   620→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   621→	}
   622→
   623→	// Log parsed input context
   624→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   625→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-task",
   626→		slog.String("hook", "post-task"),
   627→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   628→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   629→		slog.String("agent_id", input.AgentID),
   630→		slog.String("subagent_type", subagentType),
   631→	)
   632→
   633→	// Determine subagent transcript path
   634→	transcriptDir := filepath.Dir(input.TranscriptPath)
   635→	var subagentTranscriptPath string
   636→	if input.AgentID != "" {
   637→		subagentTranscriptPath = AgentTranscriptPath(transcriptDir, input.AgentID)
   638→		if !fileExists(subagentTranscriptPath) {
   639→			subagentTranscriptPath = ""
   640→		}
   641→	}
   642→
   643→	// Log context to stdout
   644→	logPostTaskHookContext(os.Stdout, input, subagentTranscriptPath)
   645→
   646→	// Parse transcript to extract modified files
   647→	var modifiedFiles []string
   648→	if subagentTranscriptPath != "" {
   649→		// Use subagent transcript if available
   650→		transcript, err := parseTranscript(subagentTranscriptPath)
   651→		if err != nil {
   652→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to parse subagent transcript: %v\n", err)
   653→		} else {
   654→			modifiedFiles = extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   655→		}
   656→	} else {
   657→		// Fall back to main transcript
   658→		transcript, err := parseTranscript(input.TranscriptPath)
   659→		if err != nil {
   660→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to parse transcript: %v\n", err)
   661→		} else {
   662→			modifiedFiles = extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   663→		}
   664→	}
   665→
   666→	// Load pre-task state and compute new files
   667→	preState, err := LoadPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID)
   668→	if err != nil {
   669→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-task state: %v\n", err)
   670→	}
   671→
   672→	// Compute new and deleted files (single git status call)
   673→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preState.PreUntrackedFiles())
   674→	if err != nil {
   675→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   676→	}
   677→
   678→	// Get repo root for path conversion (not cwd, since Claude may be in a subdirectory)
   679→	// Using cwd would filter out files in sibling directories (paths starting with ..)
   680→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   681→	if err != nil {
   682→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   683→	}
   684→
   685→	// Filter and normalize paths
   686→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   687→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   688→	if changes != nil {
   689→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   690→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   691→	}
   692→
   693→	// If no file changes, skip creating a checkpoint
   694→	if len(relModifiedFiles) == 0 && len(relNewFiles) == 0 && len(relDeletedFiles) == 0 {
   695→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping task checkpoint\n")
   696→		// Cleanup pre-task state (ignore error - cleanup is best-effort)
   697→		_ = CleanupPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   698→		return nil
   699→	}
   700→
   701→	// Find checkpoint UUID from main transcript (best-effort, ignore errors)
   702→	transcript, _ := parseTranscript(input.TranscriptPath) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort extraction
   703→	checkpointUUID, _ := FindCheckpointUUID(transcript, input.ToolUseID)
   704→
   705→	// Get git author
   706→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   707→	if err != nil {
   708→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   709→	}
   710→
   711→	// Get the configured strategy
   712→	strat := GetStrategy()
   713→
   714→	// Ensure strategy setup is in place
   715→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   716→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   717→	}
   718→
   719→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   720→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   721→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   722→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   723→	}
   724→
   725→	// Build task checkpoint context - strategy handles metadata creation
   726→	// Note: Incremental checkpoints are now created during task execution via handleClaudeCodePostTodo,
   727→	// so we don't need to collect/cleanup staging area here.
   728→	ctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{
   729→		SessionID:              input.SessionID,
   730→		ToolUseID:              input.ToolUseID,
   731→		AgentID:                input.AgentID,
   732→		ModifiedFiles:          relModifiedFiles,
   733→		NewFiles:               relNewFiles,
   734→		DeletedFiles:           relDeletedFiles,
   735→		TranscriptPath:         input.TranscriptPath,
   736→		SubagentTranscriptPath: subagentTranscriptPath,
   737→		CheckpointUUID:         checkpointUUID,
   738→		AuthorName:             author.Name,
   739→		AuthorEmail:            author.Email,
   740→		SubagentType:           subagentType,
   741→		TaskDescription:        taskDescription,
   742→		AgentType:              agentType,
   743→	}
   744→
   745→	// Call strategy to save task checkpoint - strategy handles all metadata creation
   746→	if err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   747→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save task checkpoint: %w", err)
   748→	}
   749→
   750→	// Cleanup pre-task state (ignore error - cleanup is best-effort)
   751→	_ = CleanupPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   752→
   753→	return nil
   754→}
   755→
   756→// handleClaudeCodeSessionStart handles the SessionStart hook for Claude Code.
   757→func handleClaudeCodeSessionStart() error {
   758→	return handleSessionStartCommon()
   759→}
   760→
   761→// handleClaudeCodeSessionEnd handles the SessionEnd hook for Claude Code.
   762→// This fires when the user explicitly closes the session.
   763→// Updates the session state with EndedAt timestamp.
   764→func handleClaudeCodeSessionEnd() error {
   765→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   766→	if err != nil {
   767→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   768→	}
   769→
   770→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookSessionEnd, os.Stdin)
   771→	if err != nil {
   772→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   773→	}
   774→
   775→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   776→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-end",
   777→		slog.String("hook", "session-end"),
   778→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   779→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   780→	)
   781→
   782→	if input.SessionID == "" {
   783→		return nil // No session to update
   784→	}
   785→
   786→	// Best-effort cleanup - don't block session closure on failure
   787→	if err := markSessionEnded(input.SessionID); err != nil {
   788→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to mark session ended: %v\n", err)
   789→	}
   790→	return nil
   791→}
   792→
   793→// transitionSessionTurnEnd fires EventTurnEnd to move the session from
   794→// ACTIVE → IDLE (or ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE). Best-effort: logs warnings
   795→// on failure rather than returning errors.
   796→func transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID string) {
   797→	turnState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   798→	if loadErr != nil {
   799→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for turn end: %v\n", loadErr)
   800→		return
   801→	}
   802→	if turnState == nil {
   803→		return
   804→	}
   805→	remaining := strategy.TransitionAndLog(turnState, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})
   806→
   807→	// Dispatch strategy-specific actions (e.g., ActionCondense for ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE)
   808→	if len(remaining) > 0 {
   809→		strat := GetStrategy()
   810→		if handler, ok := strat.(strategy.TurnEndHandler); ok {
   811→			if err := handler.HandleTurnEnd(turnState, remaining); err != nil {
   812→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end action dispatch failed: %v\n", err)
   813→			}
   814→		}
   815→	}
   816→
   817→	if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(turnState); updateErr != nil {
   818→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session phase on turn end: %v\n", updateErr)
   819→	}
   820→}
   821→
   822→// triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending checks for a pending REVIEW.md and spawns
   823→// the auto-apply subprocess if conditions are met. Called from the stop hook
   824→// on every turn end (both with-changes and no-changes paths).
   825→func triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending(repoRoot string) {
   826→	if !settings.IsWingmanEnabled() || os.Getenv("ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY") != "" {
   827→		return
   828→	}
   829→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "wingman")
   830→	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
   831→	if _, statErr := os.Stat(reviewPath); statErr != nil {
   832→		return
   833→	}
   834→	wingmanState := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)
   835→	if wingmanState != nil && wingmanState.ApplyAttemptedAt != nil {
   836→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply already attempted, skipping")
   837→		return
   838→	}
   839→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Pending review found, spawning auto-apply\n")
   840→	logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply spawning",
   841→		slog.String("review_path", reviewPath),
   842→	)
   843→	spawnDetachedWingmanApply(repoRoot)
   844→}
   845→
   846→// markSessionEnded transitions the session to ENDED phase via the state machine.
   847→func markSessionEnded(sessionID string) error {
   848→	state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   849→	if err != nil {
   850→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   851→	}
   852→	if state == nil {
   853→		return nil // No state file, nothing to update
   854→	}
   855→
   856→	strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStop, session.TransitionContext{})
   857→
   858→	now := time.Now()
   859→	state.EndedAt = &now
   860→
   861→	if err := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   862→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   863→	}
   864→	return nil
   865→}
   866→
   867→// stopHookSentinel is the string that appears in Claude Code's hook_progress
   868→// transcript entry when it launches our stop hook. Used to detect that the
   869→// transcript file has been fully flushed before we copy it.
   870→const stopHookSentinel = "hooks claude-code stop"
   871→
   872→// waitForTranscriptFlush polls the transcript file tail for the hook_progress
   873→// sentinel entry that Claude Code writes when launching the stop hook.
   874→// Once this entry appears in the file, all prior entries (assistant replies,
   875→// tool results) are guaranteed to have been flushed.
   876→//
   877→// hookStartTime is the approximate time our process started, used to avoid
   878→// matching stale sentinel entries from previous stop hook invocations.
   879→//
   880→// Falls back silently after a timeout — the transcript copy will proceed
   881→// with whatever data is available.
   882→func waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath string, hookStartTime time.Time) {
   883→	const (
   884→		maxWait      = 3 * time.Second
   885→		pollInterval = 50 * time.Millisecond
   886→		tailBytes    = 4096 // Read last 4KB — sentinel is near the end
   887→		maxSkew      = 2 * time.Second
   888→	)
   889→
   890→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks")
   891→	deadline := time.Now().Add(maxWait)
   892→	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
   893→		if checkStopSentinel(transcriptPath, tailBytes, hookStartTime, maxSkew) {
   894→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel found",
   895→				slog.Duration("wait", time.Since(hookStartTime)),
   896→			)
   897→			return
   898→		}
   899→		time.Sleep(pollInterval)
   900→	}
   901→	// Timeout — proceed with whatever is on disk.
   902→	logging.Warn(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel not found within timeout, proceeding",
   903→		slog.Duration("timeout", maxWait),
   904→	)
   905→}
   906→
   907→// checkStopSentinel reads the tail of the transcript file and looks for a
   908→// hook_progress entry containing the stop hook sentinel, with a timestamp
   909→// close to hookStartTime.
   910→func checkStopSentinel(path string, tailBytes int64, hookStartTime time.Time, maxSkew time.Duration) bool {
   911→	f, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // path comes from agent hook input
   912→	if err != nil {
   913→		return false
   914→	}
   915→	defer f.Close()
   916→
   917→	// Seek to tail
   918→	info, err := f.Stat()
   919→	if err != nil {
   920→		return false
   921→	}
   922→	offset := info.Size() - tailBytes
   923→	if offset < 0 {
   924→		offset = 0
   925→	}
   926→	buf := make([]byte, info.Size()-offset)
   927→	if _, err := f.ReadAt(buf, offset); err != nil {
   928→		return false
   929→	}
   930→
   931→	// Scan lines from the tail for the sentinel
   932→	lines := strings.Split(string(buf), "\n")
   933→	for _, line := range lines {
   934→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   935→		if line == "" || !strings.Contains(line, stopHookSentinel) {
   936→			continue
   937→		}
   938→
   939→		// Parse timestamp to check recency
   940→		var entry struct {
   941→			Timestamp string `json:"timestamp"`
   942→		}
   943→		if json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry) != nil || entry.Timestamp == "" {
   944→			continue
   945→		}
   946→		ts, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, entry.Timestamp)
   947→		if err != nil {
   948→			ts, err = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, entry.Timestamp)
   949→			if err != nil {
   950→				continue
   951→			}
   952→		}
   953→
   954→		// Check timestamp is within skew window of our start time
   955→		if ts.After(hookStartTime.Add(-maxSkew)) && ts.Before(hookStartTime.Add(maxSkew)) {
   956→			return true
   957→		}
   958→	}
   959→	return false
   960→}
   961→
```

```
    26→// WingmanPayload is the data passed to the detached review subprocess.

    42→	ReviewedAt       time.Time  `json:"reviewed_at"`

   189→// triggerWingmanReview checks preconditions and spawns the detached review process.
   190→func triggerWingmanReview(payload WingmanPayload) {
   191→	// Prevent infinite recursion: if we're inside a wingman auto-apply,
   192→	// don't trigger another review. The env var is set by triggerAutoApply.
   193→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY") != "" {
   194→		return
   195→	}
   196→
   197→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "wingman")
   198→	repoRoot := payload.RepoRoot
   199→
   200→	totalFiles := len(payload.ModifiedFiles) + len(payload.NewFiles) + len(payload.DeletedFiles)
   201→	logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman trigger evaluating",
   202→		slog.String("session_id", payload.SessionID),
   203→		slog.Int("file_count", totalFiles),
   204→	)
   205→
   206→	// Check if a pending REVIEW.md already exists for the current session
   207→	if shouldSkipPendingReview(repoRoot, payload.SessionID) {
   208→		logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman skipped: pending review exists for current session")
   209→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Pending review exists, skipping\n")
   210→		return
   211→	}
   212→
   213→	// Atomic lock file prevents concurrent review spawns. O_CREATE|O_EXCL
   214→	// is atomic on all platforms, avoiding the TOCTOU race of Stat+WriteFile.
   215→	lockPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanLockFile)
   216→	if !acquireWingmanLock(lockPath, payload.SessionID) {
   217→		logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman skipped: review already in progress")
   218→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review in progress, skipping\n")
   219→		return
   220→	}
   221→
   222→	// Dedup check: compute hash of sorted file paths
   223→	allFiles := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))
   224→	allFiles = append(allFiles, payload.ModifiedFiles...)
   225→	allFiles = append(allFiles, payload.NewFiles...)
   226→	allFiles = append(allFiles, payload.DeletedFiles...)
   227→	filesHash := computeFilesHash(allFiles)
   228→
   229→	state, _ := loadWingmanState() //nolint:errcheck // best-effort dedup
   230→	if state != nil && state.FilesHash == filesHash && state.SessionID == payload.SessionID {
   231→		logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman skipped: dedup hash match",
   232→			slog.String("files_hash", filesHash[:12]),
   233→		)
   234→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Already reviewed these changes, skipping\n")
   235→		return
   236→	}
   237→
   238→	// Capture HEAD at trigger time so the detached review diffs against
   239→	// the correct commit even if HEAD moves during the initial delay.
   240→	payload.BaseCommit = resolveHEAD(repoRoot)
   241→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman captured base commit",
   242→		slog.String("base_commit", payload.BaseCommit),
   243→	)
   244→
   245→	// Write payload to a temp file instead of passing as a CLI argument,
   246→	// which can exceed OS argv limits (~128KB Linux, ~256KB macOS) with
   247→	// many files or long prompts.
   248→	payloadJSON, err := json.Marshal(payload)
   249→	if err != nil {
   250→		logging.Error(logCtx, "wingman failed to marshal payload", slog.Any("error", err))
   251→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to marshal payload: %v\n", err)
   252→		_ = os.Remove(lockPath)
   253→		return
   254→	}
   255→	payloadPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".entire", "wingman-payload.json")
   256→	//nolint:gosec // G306: payload file is not secrets
   257→	if err := os.WriteFile(payloadPath, payloadJSON, 0o644); err != nil {
   258→		logging.Error(logCtx, "wingman failed to write payload file", slog.Any("error", err))
   259→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to write payload file: %v\n", err)
   260→		_ = os.Remove(lockPath)
   261→		return
   262→	}
   263→
   264→	// Spawn detached review process with path to payload file
   265→	spawnDetachedWingmanReview(repoRoot, payloadPath)
   266→	logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman review spawned",
   267→		slog.String("session_id", payload.SessionID),
   268→		slog.String("base_commit", payload.BaseCommit),
   269→		slog.Int("file_count", totalFiles),
   270→	)
   271→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review starting in background...\n")
   272→}
   273→
   274→// triggerWingmanFromCommit builds a wingman payload from the HEAD commit and
   275→// triggers a review. Used by the git post-commit hook for manual-commit strategy
   276→// where files are committed by the user (not by SaveChanges).
   277→func triggerWingmanFromCommit() {
   278→	// Prevent infinite recursion: skip if inside wingman auto-apply
   279→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY") != "" {
   280→		return
   281→	}
   282→	if !settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {
   283→		return
   284→	}
   285→
   286→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   287→	if err != nil {
   288→		return
   289→	}
   290→
   291→	head := resolveHEAD(repoRoot)
   292→	if head == "" {
   293→		return
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	// Get changed files from the commit
   297→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 30*time.Second)
   298→	defer cancel()
   299→	//nolint:gosec // G204: head is from git rev-parse, not user input
   300→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "diff-tree", "--no-commit-id", "--name-status", "-r", head)
   301→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   302→	out, err := cmd.Output()
   303→	if err != nil || len(out) == 0 {
   304→		return
   305→	}
   306→
   307→	var modified, newFiles, deleted []string
   308→	for _, line := range strings.Split(strings.TrimSpace(string(out)), "\n") {
   309→		if len(line) < 3 {
   310→			continue
   311→		}
   312→		status := line[0]
   313→		file := strings.TrimSpace(line[1:])
   314→		switch status {
   315→		case 'M':
   316→			modified = append(modified, file)
   317→		case 'A':
   318→			newFiles = append(newFiles, file)
   319→		case 'D':
   320→			deleted = append(deleted, file)
   321→		}
   322→	}
   323→
   324→	if len(modified)+len(newFiles)+len(deleted) == 0 {
   325→		return
   326→	}
   327→
   328→	// Get commit message
   329→	//nolint:gosec // G204: head is from git rev-parse, not user input
   330→	msgCmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "log", "-1", "--format=%B", head)
   331→	msgCmd.Dir = repoRoot
   332→	msgOut, _ := msgCmd.Output() //nolint:errcheck // best-effort commit message
   333→	commitMessage := strings.TrimSpace(string(msgOut))
   334→
   335→	sessionID := strategy.FindMostRecentSession()
   336→
   337→	triggerWingmanReview(WingmanPayload{
   338→		SessionID:     sessionID,
   339→		RepoRoot:      repoRoot,
   340→		ModifiedFiles: modified,
   341→		NewFiles:      newFiles,
   342→		DeletedFiles:  deleted,
   343→		CommitMessage: commitMessage,
   344→	})
   345→}
   346→
   347→// staleLockThreshold is how old a lock file can be before we consider it stale
   348→// (e.g., the detached process was SIGKILLed and the defer never ran).
   349→const staleLockThreshold = 30 * time.Minute
   350→
   351→// acquireWingmanLock atomically creates the lock file. Returns true if acquired.
   352→// If the lock already exists but is older than staleLockThreshold, it is removed
   353→// and re-acquired (handles crashed detached processes).
   354→func acquireWingmanLock(lockPath, sessionID string) bool {
   355→	//nolint:gosec // G304: lockPath is constructed from repoRoot + constant
   356→	f, err := os.OpenFile(lockPath, os.O_WRONLY|os.O_CREATE|os.O_EXCL, 0o644)
   357→	if err != nil {
   358→		if !errors.Is(err, os.ErrExist) {
   359→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to create lock: %v\n", err)
   360→			return false
   361→		}
   362→		// Lock exists — check if it's stale
   363→		info, statErr := os.Stat(lockPath)
   364→		if statErr != nil || time.Since(info.ModTime()) <= staleLockThreshold {
   365→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review already in progress, skipping\n")
   366→			return false
   367→		}
   368→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Removing stale lock (age: %s)\n",
   369→			time.Since(info.ModTime()).Round(time.Second))
   370→		_ = os.Remove(lockPath)
   371→		// Retry the atomic create
   372→		//nolint:gosec // G304: lockPath is constructed from repoRoot + constant
   373→		f, err = os.OpenFile(lockPath, os.O_WRONLY|os.O_CREATE|os.O_EXCL, 0o644)
   374→		if err != nil {
   375→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to create lock after stale removal: %v\n", err)
   376→			return false
   377→		}
   378→	}
   379→	_, _ = f.WriteString(sessionID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort session ID write
   380→	_ = f.Close()
   381→	return true
   382→}
   383→
   384→// resolveHEAD returns the current HEAD commit hash, or empty string on error.
   385→func resolveHEAD(repoRoot string) string {
   386→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 5*time.Second)
   387→	defer cancel()
   388→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "HEAD")
   389→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   390→	out, err := cmd.Output()
   391→	if err != nil {
   392→		return ""
   393→	}
   394→	return strings.TrimSpace(string(out))
   395→}
   396→
   397→// computeFilesHash returns a SHA256 hex digest of the sorted file paths.
   398→// Uses null byte separator (impossible in filenames) to avoid ambiguity.
   399→func computeFilesHash(files []string) string {
   400→	sorted := make([]string, len(files))
   401→	copy(sorted, files)
   402→	sort.Strings(sorted)
   403→	h := sha256.Sum256([]byte(strings.Join(sorted, "\x00")))
   404→	return hex.EncodeToString(h[:])
   405→}
   406→
   407→// loadWingmanState loads the wingman state from .entire/wingman-state.json.
   408→func loadWingmanState() (*WingmanState, error) {
   409→	statePath, err := paths.AbsPath(wingmanStateFile)
   410→	if err != nil {
   411→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("resolving wingman state path: %w", err)
   412→	}
   413→
   414→	data, err := os.ReadFile(statePath) //nolint:gosec // path is repo-relative
   415→	if err != nil {
   416→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("reading wingman state: %w", err)
   417→	}
   418→
   419→	var state WingmanState
   420→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &state); err != nil {
   421→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("parsing wingman state: %w", err)
   422→	}
   423→	return &state, nil
   424→}
   425→
   426→// saveWingmanState saves the wingman state to .entire/wingman-state.json.
   427→func saveWingmanState(state *WingmanState) error {
   428→	statePath, err := paths.AbsPath(wingmanStateFile)
   429→	if err != nil {
   430→		return fmt.Errorf("resolving wingman state path: %w", err)
   431→	}
   432→
   433→	dir := filepath.Dir(statePath)
   434→	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o750); err != nil {
   435→		return fmt.Errorf("creating wingman state directory: %w", err)
   436→	}
   437→
   438→	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(state, "", "  ")
   439→	if err != nil {
   440→		return fmt.Errorf("marshaling wingman state: %w", err)
   441→	}
   442→
   443→	//nolint:gosec // G306: state file is config, not secrets
   444→	if err := os.WriteFile(statePath, data, 0o644); err != nil {
   445→		return fmt.Errorf("writing wingman state: %w", err)
   446→	}
   447→	return nil
   448→}
   449→
   450→// loadWingmanStateDirect reads the wingman state file directly from a known
   451→// path under repoRoot. Returns nil if the file doesn't exist or can't be parsed.
   452→func loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot string) *WingmanState {
   453→	statePath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanStateFile)
   454→	data, err := os.ReadFile(statePath) //nolint:gosec // path is repo-relative constant
   455→	if err != nil {
   456→		return nil
   457→	}
   458→	var state WingmanState
   459→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &state); err != nil {
   460→		return nil
   461→	}
   462→	return &state
   463→}
   464→
   465→// newWingmanApplyCmd returns a hidden subcommand used by the stop hook to
   466→// apply a pending REVIEW.md via claude --continue in a detached subprocess.
   467→func newWingmanApplyCmd() *cobra.Command {
   468→	return &cobra.Command{
   469→		Use:    "__apply",
   470→		Hidden: true,
   471→		Args:   cobra.ExactArgs(1),
   472→		RunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
   473→			return runWingmanApply(args[0])
   474→		},
   475→	}
   476→}
   477→
   478→// shouldSkipPendingReview checks whether a pending REVIEW.md should prevent
   479→// a new review from being triggered. It cleans up stale/orphaned reviews.
   480→//
   481→// Returns true only when REVIEW.md exists AND belongs to the current session
   482→// AND is younger than wingmanStaleReviewTTL.
   483→func shouldSkipPendingReview(repoRoot, currentSessionID string) bool {
   484→	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
   485→	if _, err := os.Stat(reviewPath); err != nil {
   486→		return false // No REVIEW.md, don't skip
   487→	}
   488→
   489→	// REVIEW.md exists — check state to determine if it's current or stale
   490→	state := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)
   491→	if state == nil {
   492→		// Orphan: REVIEW.md without state file — clean up
   493→		_ = os.Remove(reviewPath)
   494→		return false
   495→	}
   496→
   497→	if state.SessionID != currentSessionID {
   498→		// Different session — stale review, clean up
   499→		_ = os.Remove(reviewPath)
   500→		return false
   501→	}
   502→
   503→	if time.Since(state.ReviewedAt) > wingmanStaleReviewTTL {
   504→		// Same session but too old — clean up
   505→		_ = os.Remove(reviewPath)
   506→		return false
   507→	}
   508→
   509→	return true // Current session, fresh review — skip
   510→}
   511→
```

> AGENT

Now I see the issue clearly. The background `claude --continue --print` auto-apply IS working (REVIEW.md gets created, applied, then deleted in 13-22s) — but it's **invisible** to the user. And since it deletes REVIEW.md before the user sends their next prompt, the `additionalContext` injection never fires (nothing to inject).

The fix: when any session is live (IDLE/ACTIVE), **don't** run background auto-apply. Leave REVIEW.md in place so the prompt-submit injection delivers it visibly. Only use background auto-apply when no sessions are alive.

Let me implement this.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01T5GyfhBVFEZcDbUeNR26DA
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go",
  "old_string": "\t// Check if session is idle right now (rare — user usually starts typing\n\t// during the 10s settle + review time). If idle, apply immediately.\n\t// Otherwise, the stop hook will handle auto-apply when the current turn ends.\n\tidle := isSessionIdle(repoRoot, payload.SessionID)\n\twingmanLog(\"session idle check: idle=%v\", idle)\n\n\tif !idle {\n\t\twingmanLog(\"session is active, stop hook will handle auto-apply when turn ends\")\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Mark apply as attempted before triggering (retry prevention)\n\tnow := time.Now()\n\tstate := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)\n\tif state != nil {\n\t\tstate.ApplyAttemptedAt = &now\n\t\tsaveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot, state)\n\t}\n\n\twingmanLog(\"triggering auto-apply via claude --continue (session idle)\")\n\tapplyStart := time.Now()\n\tif err := triggerAutoApply(repoRoot); err != nil {\n\t\twingmanLog(\"ERROR auto-apply failed after %s: %v\", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond), err)\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to trigger auto-apply: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\twingmanLog(\"auto-apply completed in %s\", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond))\n\n\treturn nil",
  "new_string": "\t// If any session is live (IDLE/ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED), don't auto-apply\n\t// in the background. The prompt-submit hook will inject REVIEW.md as\n\t// additionalContext when the user sends their next prompt — this is VISIBLE\n\t// in their terminal. Only use background auto-apply when no sessions are\n\t// alive (e.g., user closed all sessions).\n\tif hasAnyLiveSession(repoRoot) {\n\t\twingmanLog(\"live session detected, deferring to prompt-submit injection (visible)\")\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// No live sessions — apply in background as fallback\n\twingmanLog(\"no live sessions, triggering background auto-apply\")\n\tnow := time.Now()\n\tstate := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)\n\tif state != nil {\n\t\tstate.ApplyAttemptedAt = &now\n\t\tsaveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot, state)\n\t}\n\n\tapplyStart := time.Now()\n\tif err := triggerAutoApply(repoRoot); err != nil {\n\t\twingmanLog(\"ERROR auto-apply failed after %s: %v\", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond), err)\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to trigger auto-apply: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\twingmanLog(\"auto-apply completed in %s\", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond))\n\n\treturn nil"
}
```
