```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go",
  "old_string": "// findSessionStateDir locates the .git/entire-sessions/ directory by\n// reading .git to handle both normal repos and worktrees.",
  "new_string": "// hasAnyLiveSession checks if any session is in a non-ENDED phase (IDLE,\n// ACTIVE, or ACTIVE_COMMITTED). Used to decide whether to defer review\n// application to the prompt-submit injection (visible to user) vs background\n// auto-apply (invisible).\nfunc hasAnyLiveSession(repoRoot string) bool {\n\tsessDir := findSessionStateDir(repoRoot)\n\tif sessDir == \"\" {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\n\tentries, err := os.ReadDir(sessDir)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\n\tconst maxCheck = 50\n\tchecked := 0\n\tfor _, entry := range entries {\n\t\tif checked >= maxCheck {\n\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t}\n\t\tname := entry.Name()\n\t\tif !strings.HasSuffix(name, \".json\") {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tchecked++\n\t\tsid := strings.TrimSuffix(name, \".json\")\n\t\tphase := readSessionPhase(sessDir, sid)\n\t\tif phase != \"\" && phase != string(session.PhaseEnded) {\n\t\t\twingmanLog(\"found live session %s (phase=%s)\", sid, phase)\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn false\n}\n\n// findSessionStateDir locates the .git/entire-sessions/ directory by\n// reading .git to handle both normal repos and worktrees."
}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,

  "old_string": "// triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending checks for a pending REVIEW.md and spawns\n// the auto-apply subprocess if conditions are met. Called from the stop hook\n// on every turn end (both with-changes and no-changes paths).\nfunc triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending(repoRoot string) {\n\tif !settings.IsWingmanEnabled() || os.Getenv(\"ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY\") != \"\" {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tlogCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), \"wingman\")\n\treviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)\n\tif _, statErr := os.Stat(reviewPath); statErr != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\twingmanState := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)\n\tif wingmanState != nil && wingmanState.ApplyAttemptedAt != nil {\n\t\tlogging.Debug(logCtx, \"wingman auto-apply already attempted, skipping\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Pending review found, spawning auto-apply\\n\")\n\tlogging.Info(logCtx, \"wingman auto-apply spawning\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"review_path\", reviewPath),\n\t)\n\tspawnDetachedWingmanApply(repoRoot)\n}",
  "new_string": "// triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending checks for a pending REVIEW.md and spawns\n// the auto-apply subprocess if conditions are met. Called from the stop hook\n// on every turn end (both with-changes and no-changes paths).\n//\n// When a live session exists, this is a no-op: the prompt-submit injection\n// will deliver the review visibly in the user's terminal instead. Background\n// auto-apply is only used when no sessions are alive (all ended).\nfunc triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending(repoRoot string) {\n\tif !settings.IsWingmanEnabled() || os.Getenv(\"ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY\") != \"\" {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tlogCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), \"wingman\")\n\treviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)\n\tif _, statErr := os.Stat(reviewPath); statErr != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\twingmanState := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)\n\tif wingmanState != nil && wingmanState.ApplyAttemptedAt != nil {\n\t\tlogging.Debug(logCtx, \"wingman auto-apply already attempted, skipping\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\t// Don't spawn background auto-apply if a live session exists.\n\t// The prompt-submit hook will inject REVIEW.md as additionalContext,\n\t// which is visible to the user in their terminal.\n\tif hasAnyLiveSession(repoRoot) {\n\t\tlogging.Debug(logCtx, \"wingman auto-apply deferred: live session will handle via injection\")\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Review pending — will be injected on next prompt\\n\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Pending review found, spawning auto-apply (no live sessions)\\n\")\n\tlogging.Info(logCtx, \"wingman auto-apply spawning (no live sessions)\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"review_path\", reviewPath),\n\t)\n\tspawnDetachedWingmanApply(repoRoot)\n}"
}
```

```
    86→	}
    87→	if len(decoded.ModifiedFiles) != len(payload.ModifiedFiles) {
    88→		t.Errorf("modified_files: got %d, want %d", len(decoded.ModifiedFiles), len(payload.ModifiedFiles))
    89→	}
    90→	if len(decoded.NewFiles) != len(payload.NewFiles) {
    91→		t.Errorf("new_files: got %d, want %d", len(decoded.NewFiles), len(payload.NewFiles))
    92→	}
    93→	if len(decoded.DeletedFiles) != len(payload.DeletedFiles) {
    94→		t.Errorf("deleted_files: got %d, want %d", len(decoded.DeletedFiles), len(payload.DeletedFiles))
    95→	}
    96→	if len(decoded.Prompts) != len(payload.Prompts) {
    97→		t.Errorf("prompts: got %d, want %d", len(decoded.Prompts), len(payload.Prompts))
    98→	}
    99→	if decoded.CommitMessage != payload.CommitMessage {
   100→		t.Errorf("commit_message: got %q, want %q", decoded.CommitMessage, payload.CommitMessage)
   101→	}
   102→}

   104→func TestBuildReviewPrompt_IncludesAllSections(t *testing.T) {
   105→	t.Parallel()
   106→
   107→	prompts := []string{"Fix the authentication bug"}
   108→	fileList := "auth.go (modified), auth_test.go (new)"
   109→	diff := `diff --git a/auth.go b/auth.go
   110→--- a/auth.go
   111→+++ b/auth.go
   112→@@ -10,6 +10,8 @@ func Login(user, pass string) error {
   113→+    if user == "" {
   114→+        return errors.New("empty user")
   115→+    }
   116→`
   117→
   118→	commitMsg := "Fix empty user login crash"
   119→	sessionCtx := "## Summary\nFixed authentication bug for empty usernames"
   120→
   121→	result := buildReviewPrompt(prompts, commitMsg, sessionCtx, "test-session-456", fileList, diff)
   122→
   123→	if !strings.Contains(result, "Fix the authentication bug") {
   124→		t.Error("prompt should contain user prompt")
   125→	}
   126→	if !strings.Contains(result, "auth.go (modified)") {
   127→		t.Error("prompt should contain file list")
   128→	}
   129→	if !strings.Contains(result, "diff --git") {
   130→		t.Error("prompt should contain diff")
   131→	}
   132→	if !strings.Contains(result, "intent-aware review") {
   133→		t.Error("prompt should contain reviewer instruction")
   134→	}
   135→	if !strings.Contains(result, "Fix empty user login crash") {
   136→		t.Error("prompt should contain commit message")
   137→	}
   138→	if !strings.Contains(result, "Fixed authentication bug") {
   139→		t.Error("prompt should contain session context")
   140→	}
   141→	if !strings.Contains(result, ".entire/metadata/test-session-456/full.jsonl") {
   142→		t.Error("prompt should contain checkpoint transcript path")
   143→	}
   144→	if !strings.Contains(result, ".entire/metadata/test-session-456/prompt.txt") {
   145→		t.Error("prompt should contain checkpoint prompt path")
   146→	}
   147→	if !strings.Contains(result, ".entire/metadata/test-session-456/context.md") {
   148→		t.Error("prompt should contain checkpoint context path")
   149→	}
   150→}

   185→func TestWingmanState_SaveLoad(t *testing.T) {
   186→	// Uses t.Chdir so cannot be parallel
   187→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   188→
   189→	// Initialize a real git repo (paths.AbsPath needs git rev-parse)
   190→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), "git", "init", tmpDir)
   191→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   192→		t.Fatalf("failed to git init: %v", err)
   193→	}
   194→
   195→	// Create .entire directory
   196→	entireDir := filepath.Join(tmpDir, ".entire")
   197→	if err := os.MkdirAll(entireDir, 0o755); err != nil {
   198→		t.Fatalf("failed to create .entire directory: %v", err)
   199→	}
   200→
   201→	t.Chdir(tmpDir)
   202→
   203→	state := &WingmanState{
   204→		SessionID:     "test-session",
   205→		FilesHash:     "abc123",
   206→		ReviewApplied: false,
   207→	}
   208→
   209→	if err := saveWingmanState(state); err != nil {
   210→		t.Fatalf("failed to save state: %v", err)
   211→	}
   212→
   213→	loaded, err := loadWingmanState()
   214→	if err != nil {
   215→		t.Fatalf("failed to load state: %v", err)
   216→	}
   217→
   218→	if loaded.SessionID != state.SessionID {
   219→		t.Errorf("session_id: got %q, want %q", loaded.SessionID, state.SessionID)
   220→	}
   221→	if loaded.FilesHash != state.FilesHash {
   222→		t.Errorf("files_hash: got %q, want %q", loaded.FilesHash, state.FilesHash)
   223→	}
   224→	if loaded.ReviewApplied != state.ReviewApplied {
   225→		t.Errorf("review_applied: got %v, want %v", loaded.ReviewApplied, state.ReviewApplied)
   226→	}
   227→}

   253→func TestIsWingmanEnabled_Settings(t *testing.T) {
   254→	t.Parallel()
   255→
   256→	tests := []struct {
   257→		name     string
   258→		options  map[string]any
   259→		expected bool
   260→	}{
   261→		{
   262→			name:     "nil options",
   263→			options:  nil,
   264→			expected: false,
   265→		},
   266→		{
   267→			name:     "empty options",
   268→			options:  map[string]any{},
   269→			expected: false,
   270→		},
   271→		{
   272→			name:     "wingman not present",
   273→			options:  map[string]any{"summarize": map[string]any{"enabled": true}},
   274→			expected: false,
   275→		},
   276→		{
   277→			name:     "wingman enabled",
   278→			options:  map[string]any{"wingman": map[string]any{"enabled": true}},
   279→			expected: true,
   280→		},
   281→		{
   282→			name:     "wingman disabled",
   283→			options:  map[string]any{"wingman": map[string]any{"enabled": false}},
   284→			expected: false,
   285→		},
   286→		{
   287→			name:     "wingman wrong type",
   288→			options:  map[string]any{"wingman": "invalid"},
   289→			expected: false,
   290→		},
   291→		{
   292→			name:     "wingman enabled wrong type",
   293→			options:  map[string]any{"wingman": map[string]any{"enabled": "yes"}},
   294→			expected: false,
   295→		},
   296→	}
   297→
   298→	for _, tt := range tests {
   299→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
   300→			t.Parallel()
   301→			s := &EntireSettings{
   302→				StrategyOptions: tt.options,
   303→			}
   304→			if got := s.IsWingmanEnabled(); got != tt.expected {
   305→				t.Errorf("IsWingmanEnabled() = %v, want %v", got, tt.expected)
   306→			}
   307→		})
   308→	}
   309→}

   311→func TestWingmanState_ApplyAttemptedAt_RoundTrip(t *testing.T) {
   312→	t.Parallel()
   313→
   314→	now := time.Now().Truncate(time.Second)
   315→	state := &WingmanState{
   316→		SessionID:        "sess-1",
   317→		FilesHash:        "hash1",
   318→		ReviewedAt:       now,
   319→		ReviewApplied:    false,
   320→		ApplyAttemptedAt: &now,
   321→	}
   322→
   323→	data, err := json.Marshal(state)
   324→	if err != nil {
   325→		t.Fatalf("failed to marshal: %v", err)
   326→	}
   327→
   328→	var decoded WingmanState
   329→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &decoded); err != nil {
   330→		t.Fatalf("failed to unmarshal: %v", err)
   331→	}
   332→
   333→	if decoded.ApplyAttemptedAt == nil {
   334→		t.Fatal("ApplyAttemptedAt should not be nil after round-trip")
   335→	}
   336→	if !decoded.ApplyAttemptedAt.Truncate(time.Second).Equal(now) {
   337→		t.Errorf("ApplyAttemptedAt: got %v, want %v", decoded.ApplyAttemptedAt, now)
   338→	}
   339→}

   359→func TestLoadWingmanStateDirect(t *testing.T) {
   360→	t.Parallel()
   361→
   362→	t.Run("missing file returns nil", func(t *testing.T) {
   363→		t.Parallel()
   364→		tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   365→		got := loadWingmanStateDirect(tmpDir)
   366→		if got != nil {
   367→			t.Errorf("expected nil for missing file, got %+v", got)
   368→		}
   369→	})
   370→
   371→	t.Run("valid file returns state", func(t *testing.T) {
   372→		t.Parallel()
   373→		tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   374→		entireDir := filepath.Join(tmpDir, ".entire")
   375→		if err := os.MkdirAll(entireDir, 0o755); err != nil {
   376→			t.Fatal(err)
   377→		}
   378→
   379→		now := time.Now()
   380→		stateJSON := `{"session_id":"sess-1","files_hash":"hash1","reviewed_at":"` + now.Format(time.RFC3339Nano) + `","review_applied":false}`
   381→		if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(entireDir, "wingman-state.json"), []byte(stateJSON), 0o644); err != nil {
   382→			t.Fatal(err)
   383→		}
   384→
   385→		got := loadWingmanStateDirect(tmpDir)
   386→		if got == nil {
   387→			t.Fatal("expected non-nil state")
   388→		}
   389→		if got.SessionID != "sess-1" {
   390→			t.Errorf("SessionID: got %q, want %q", got.SessionID, "sess-1")
   391→		}
   392→	})
   393→}
```

> AGENT

All tests pass. Here's a summary of what was wrong and what I changed:

**Root cause:** The background `claude --continue --print` auto-apply was racing with the `additionalContext` prompt-submit injection. The background apply was winning — it ran silently (invisible to the user), applied the review, and deleted REVIEW.md. By the time the user sent their next prompt, there was no REVIEW.md left to inject.

**Fix:** Added `hasAnyLiveSession(repoRoot)` that checks if ANY session is in a non-ENDED phase (IDLE, ACTIVE, or ACTIVE_COMMITTED). When a live session exists:

1. **In `runWingmanReview`** — don't trigger background auto-apply. Leave REVIEW.md in place for the prompt-submit injection to pick up.
2. **In `triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending`** (stop hook) — same, don't spawn background auto-apply.

Background auto-apply is only used as a fallback when no sessions are alive (all ended or none exist).

The flow now works as:
```
Commit → wingman review → REVIEW.md written → live session exists → defer to injection
User sends next prompt → prompt-submit hook → REVIEW.md exists → inject additionalContext
Agent reads REVIEW.md → applies suggestions → deletes REVIEW.md → user sees it all
```

**Files changed:**
- `wingman_review.go` — replaced idle-check auto-apply with `hasAnyLiveSession` guard, added `hasAnyLiveSession()` function
- `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` — added `hasAnyLiveSession` guard to `triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending()`
- `wingman_test.go` — 6 new tests for `hasAnyLiveSession`

> DEVELOPER

After what time period or determination do we do the "auto-apply" then?

> AGENT

Good question. Let me trace through the current logic:

**Primary path (live session exists):** The review is applied when the user sends their next prompt. The prompt-submit hook sees REVIEW.md → injects `additionalContext` → agent addresses it visibly.

**Fallback path (no live sessions):** Background `claude --continue --print` fires immediately when the review finishes AND `hasAnyLiveSession()` returns false. This is rare — it requires the user to have closed all sessions during the ~30-60s review window.

**Gap:** If the user closes their session *after* the review finishes, nothing triggers auto-apply. REVIEW.md sits there until:
- The stale TTL (1 hour) cleans it up
- A new session starts and the user sends a prompt

So the stop hook path (`triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending`) is effectively dead now — the session just transitioned to IDLE, so `hasAnyLiveSession` always returns true there.

The realistic trigger points are:

| Scenario | What happens | Visible? |
|----------|-------------|----------|
| User sends next prompt | `additionalContext` injection | Yes |
| User closed session before review finished | Background auto-apply | No |
| User closes session after review, opens new one | Injection on first prompt | Yes |
| User closes session, never returns | TTL cleanup after 1 hour | N/A |

Do you want me to also trigger background auto-apply from the `SessionEnd` hook to cover the "user closes after review" gap? That would make it: if session ends and REVIEW.md exists → spawn background auto-apply (since there may be no more prompts coming).

> DEVELOPER

Yes. Then document the entire scenario in /docs.

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go",
  "old_string": "// markSessionEnded transitions the session to ENDED phase via the state machine.",
  "new_string": "// triggerWingmanAutoApplyOnSessionEnd checks for a pending REVIEW.md after a\n// session ends. If no live sessions remain, spawns background auto-apply.\n// Called from both Claude Code and Gemini CLI SessionEnd hooks.\nfunc triggerWingmanAutoApplyOnSessionEnd() {\n\tif !settings.IsWingmanEnabled() || os.Getenv(\"ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY\") != \"\" {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\trepoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tlogCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), \"wingman\")\n\treviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)\n\tif _, statErr := os.Stat(reviewPath); statErr != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\twingmanState := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)\n\tif wingmanState != nil && wingmanState.ApplyAttemptedAt != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif hasAnyLiveSession(repoRoot) {\n\t\tlogging.Debug(logCtx, \"wingman auto-apply deferred on session-end: other live sessions remain\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Last session ended with pending review, spawning auto-apply\\n\")\n\tlogging.Info(logCtx, \"wingman auto-apply on session-end (no live sessions remain)\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"review_path\", reviewPath),\n\t)\n\tspawnDetachedWingmanApply(repoRoot)\n}\n\n// markSessionEnded transitions the session to ENDED phase via the state machine."
}
```

```
     1→# Entire CLI Hooks for Claude Code
     2→
     3→This document describes the hooks that Entire installs in Claude Code's `.claude/settings.json` to track AI-assisted development sessions.
     4→
     5→## Overview
     6→
     7→Entire integrates with Claude Code through six hooks that fire at different points during a session:
     8→
     9→| Hook                     | Trigger                        | Purpose                                        |
    10→| ------------------------ | ------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------- |
    11→| `SessionStart`           | New chat session begins        | Generate and persist Entire session ID         |
    12→| `UserPromptSubmit`       | User submits a prompt          | Capture pre-prompt state, check for conflicts  |
    13→| `Stop`                   | Claude finishes responding     | Create checkpoint with code + metadata         |
    14→| `PreToolUse[Task]`       | Subagent is about to start     | Capture pre-task state for diff computation    |
    15→| `PostToolUse[Task]`      | Subagent finishes              | Create final checkpoint for subagent work      |
    16→| `PostToolUse[TodoWrite]` | Subagent updates its todo list | Create incremental checkpoint if files changed |
    17→
    18→### Critical Capabilities
    19→
    20→  1. Prompt blocking - UserPromptSubmit hook needs to support returning a response to be shown in the cli session.
    21→    - Claude Code allows us to return a JSON response to stdout that can:
    22→      - Allow continuation: {"continue": true}
    23→      - Block with message: {"continue": false, "stopReason": "Your message here"}
    24→  2. Transcript access - Hooks receive transcript path; system needs to read it for:
    25→    - Extracting user prompts
    26→    - Extracting modified files
    27→    - Generating summaries
    28→  3. Tool use ID tracking - For subagent checkpoints, need unique tool_use_id to correlate PreToolUse and PostToolUse events
    29→  4. Stdin parsing - Hook input comes as JSON on stdin; agent must define its input schema
    30→
    31→## Detailed Hook Info
    32→
    33→### `SessionStart`
    34→
    35→- **Command**: `entire hooks claude-code session-start`
    36→- **Handler**: `handleSessionStart()` in `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go:907`
    37→
    38→Fires when a new chat session begins in Claude Code.
    39→
    40→**What it does:**
    41→
    42→1.  **Parse Input**: Reads session details from Claude Code's hook payload (`session_id`, `session_ref`).
    43→2.  **Generate Entire Session ID**: Creates a date-prefixed identifier by combining today's date with the model's session ID (e.g., `2026-01-15-ab310c99-f579-4a12-8b3c-1234567890ab`).
    44→3.  **Persist Session ID**: Writes the Entire session ID to `.entire/current_session`. This file is read by subsequent hooks to maintain session context across hook invocations, even if the session spans midnight (date boundary).
    45→
    46→### `UserPromptSubmit`
    47→
    48→- **Command**: `entire hooks claude-code user-prompt-submit`
    49→- **Handler**: `captureInitialState()` in `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go:248`
    50→
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/docs/architecture/wingman.md",
  "content": "# Wingman: Automated Code Review\n\nWingman is an automated code review system that reviews agent-produced code changes after each commit and delivers actionable suggestions back to the agent.\n\n## Overview\n\nWhen enabled, wingman runs a background review after each commit (or checkpoint), writes suggestions to `.entire/REVIEW.md`, and ensures the agent addresses them. The system prioritizes **visible delivery** — the user should see the agent reading and applying the review in their terminal.\n\n## Architecture\n\n### Components\n\n| Component | File | Purpose |\n|-----------|------|---------|\n| Trigger & state | `wingman.go` | Payload, state management, dedup, lock files, stale review cleanup |\n| Review process | `wingman_review.go` | Detached subprocess: diff, Claude review call, REVIEW.md creation, session detection |\n| Review prompt | `wingman_prompt.go` | Builds the review prompt from diff, prompts, context |\n| Instruction | `wingman_instruction.md` | Embedded instruction injected into agent context |\n| Process spawning | `wingman_spawn_unix.go` | Detached subprocess spawning (Unix) |\n| Process spawning | `wingman_spawn_other.go` | No-op stubs (non-Unix) |\n| Hook integration | `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` | Prompt-submit injection, stop hook trigger, session-end trigger |\n\n### State Files\n\n| File | Purpose |\n|------|---------|\n| `.entire/REVIEW.md` | The review itself, read by the agent |\n| `.entire/wingman-state.json` | Dedup state, session ID, apply tracking |\n| `.entire/wingman.lock` | Prevents concurrent review spawns |\n| `.entire/wingman-payload.json` | Payload passed to detached review process |\n| `.entire/logs/wingman.log` | Review process logs (stderr redirect) |\n\n## Lifecycle\n\n### Phase 1: Trigger\n\nA wingman review is triggered after code changes are committed:\n\n- **Manual-commit strategy**: Git `post-commit` hook calls `triggerWingmanFromCommit()`\n- **Auto-commit strategy**: Stop hook calls `triggerWingmanReview()` after `SaveChanges()`\n\nBefore spawning, preconditions are checked:\n1. `ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY` env var not set (prevents recursion from auto-apply)\n2. No pending REVIEW.md for the current session (`shouldSkipPendingReview()`)\n3. Lock file acquired atomically (`acquireWingmanLock()`)\n4. File hash dedup — skip if same files were already reviewed this session\n\n### Phase 2: Review (Detached Process)\n\nThe review runs in a fully detached subprocess (`entire wingman __review <payload-path>`):\n\n```\n┌─ Detached Process ─────────────────────────────────────┐\n│ 1. Read payload from file                              │\n│ 2. Wait 10s for agent turn to settle                   │\n│ 3. Compute diff (merge-base with main/master)          │\n│ 4. Load session context from checkpoint metadata       │\n│ 5. Build review prompt (diff + prompts + context)      │\n│ 6. Call claude --print (sonnet model, read-only tools)  │\n│ 7. Write REVIEW.md                                     │\n│ 8. Save dedup state                                    │\n│ 9. Determine delivery path (see Phase 3)               │\n│ 10. Remove lock file                                   │\n└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\nThe review process uses `--setting-sources \"\"` to disable hooks (prevents recursion) and strips `GIT_*` environment variables for isolation.\n\n### Phase 3: Delivery\n\nThere are two delivery mechanisms. The system chooses based on whether any session is still alive.\n\n#### Primary: Prompt-Submit Injection (Visible)\n\nWhen a live session exists (IDLE, ACTIVE, or ACTIVE_COMMITTED phase), the review is delivered through the agent's next prompt:\n\n```\nReview finishes → REVIEW.md written → live session detected → defer to injection\n\nUser sends prompt → UserPromptSubmit hook fires\n                  → REVIEW.md exists on disk\n                  → Inject as additionalContext (mandatory agent instruction)\n                  → Agent reads REVIEW.md, applies suggestions, deletes file\n                  → Agent then proceeds with user's actual request\n```\n\nThe `additionalContext` hook response field adds the instruction directly to Claude's context, making it a mandatory pre-step rather than an ignorable warning. The embedded instruction (`wingman_instruction.md`) tells the agent to:\n\n1. Read `.entire/REVIEW.md`\n2. Address each suggestion (skip any it disagrees with)\n3. Delete `.entire/REVIEW.md` when done\n4. Briefly tell the user what changed\n\nThis path is **visible** — the user sees the agent working through the review in their terminal.\n\n#### Fallback: Background Auto-Apply (Invisible)\n\nWhen no live sessions exist (all ENDED or none), REVIEW.md is applied via a background process:\n\n```\nReview finishes → REVIEW.md written → no live sessions → background auto-apply\n\nentire wingman __apply <repoRoot>\n  → Verify REVIEW.md exists\n  → Check ApplyAttemptedAt not set (retry prevention)\n  → Re-check session idle (guard against race)\n  → claude --continue --print --permission-mode acceptEdits\n```\n\nThis path is **invisible** — it runs silently. It exists as a fallback for when no session will receive the injection (e.g., user closed all sessions during the review window).\n\n### Decision Flow\n\n```\n                    REVIEW.md written\n                          │\n                          ▼\n                ┌─────────────────┐\n                │ Any live session │\n                │   exists?        │\n                └────────┬────────┘\n                    │          │\n                   Yes         No\n                    │          │\n                    ▼          ▼\n            ┌──────────┐ ┌──────────────┐\n            │  Defer   │ │  Background  │\n            │  to next │ │  auto-apply  │\n            │  prompt  │ │  immediately │\n            └──────────┘ └──────────────┘\n                    │\n                    ▼\n            User sends prompt\n                    │\n                    ▼\n            additionalContext\n            injection fires\n                    │\n                    ▼\n            Agent applies review\n            (visible in terminal)\n```\n\n### Trigger Points Summary\n\n| Trigger | When | What Happens |\n|---------|------|-------------|\n| **Review process** (`runWingmanReview`) | Review finishes | If no live sessions → background auto-apply. Otherwise defer. |\n| **Prompt-submit hook** (`captureInitialState`) | User sends prompt | If REVIEW.md exists → inject as `additionalContext`. |\n| **Stop hook** (`triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending`) | Agent turn ends | If REVIEW.md exists + no live sessions → spawn `__apply`. |\n| **Session-end hook** (`triggerWingmanAutoApplyOnSessionEnd`) | User closes session | If REVIEW.md exists + no remaining live sessions → spawn `__apply`. |\n\n## Timing\n\nTypical timeline for a review cycle:\n\n```\nT+0s    Commit happens → wingman review triggered\nT+0s    Lock file created, payload written\nT+10s   Initial settle delay completes\nT+10s   Diff computed (~30-50ms)\nT+11s   Claude review API call starts\nT+30-50s Review received, REVIEW.md written\nT+30-50s Delivery path determined\n```\n\nThe 10-second initial delay lets the agent turn fully settle before computing the diff, ensuring all file writes are flushed.\n\n## Stale Review Cleanup\n\nReviews can become stale in several scenarios. The `shouldSkipPendingReview()` function handles cleanup:\n\n| Scenario | Detection | Action |\n|----------|-----------|--------|\n| REVIEW.md without state file | `state == nil` | Delete REVIEW.md (orphan) |\n| REVIEW.md from different session | `state.SessionID != currentSessionID` | Delete REVIEW.md (stale) |\n| REVIEW.md older than 1 hour | `time.Since(state.ReviewedAt) > 1h` | Delete REVIEW.md (TTL expired) |\n| REVIEW.md from current session | Session matches + fresh | Keep (skip new review) |\n\n## Retry Prevention\n\nThe `ApplyAttemptedAt` field in `WingmanState` prevents infinite auto-apply attempts:\n\n- Set to current time before triggering auto-apply\n- Reset to `nil` when a new review is written\n- Checked before every auto-apply attempt — if set, skip\n\n## Concurrency Safety\n\n- **Lock file**: Atomic `O_CREATE|O_EXCL` prevents concurrent review spawns. Stale locks (>30 min) are auto-cleaned.\n- **Dedup hash**: SHA256 of sorted file paths prevents re-reviewing identical change sets.\n- **Detached processes**: Review and apply run in their own process groups (`Setpgid: true`), surviving parent exit.\n- **GIT_* stripping**: Subprocesses strip all `GIT_*` env vars to prevent index corruption.\n- **ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY=1**: Set during auto-apply to prevent the post-commit hook from triggering another review (recursion prevention).\n\n## Configuration\n\n```bash\nentire wingman enable   # Enable wingman auto-review\nentire wingman disable  # Disable and clean up pending reviews\nentire wingman status   # Show current status\n```\n\nWingman state is stored in `.entire/settings.json`:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"strategy_options\": {\n    \"wingman\": {\n      \"enabled\": true\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n## Key Constants\n\n| Constant | Value | Purpose |\n|----------|-------|---------|\n| `wingmanInitialDelay` | 10s | Settle time before computing diff |\n| `wingmanReviewModel` | `sonnet` | Model used for reviews |\n| `wingmanGitTimeout` | 60s | Timeout for git diff operations |\n| `wingmanReviewTimeout` | 5m | Timeout for Claude review API call |\n| `wingmanApplyTimeout` | 15m | Timeout for auto-apply process |\n| `wingmanStaleReviewTTL` | 1h | Max age before review is cleaned up |\n| `staleLockThreshold` | 30m | Max age before lock is considered stale |\n"
}
```

Done. Here's a summary of all changes:

**1. SessionEnd auto-apply trigger** (`hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`, `hooks_geminicli_handlers.go`):
- Added `triggerWingmanAutoApplyOnSessionEnd()` — when a session ends and REVIEW.md exists, checks if any live sessions remain. If none, spawns background auto-apply.
- Called from both Claude Code and Gemini CLI SessionEnd handlers.

**2. Documentation** (`docs/architecture/wingman.md`):
- Full lifecycle documentation covering trigger, review, and delivery phases
- Decision flow diagram showing visible vs invisible delivery paths
- All four trigger points documented in a summary table
- Stale review cleanup, retry prevention, concurrency safety
- Timing, configuration, and key constants

The complete trigger point coverage is now:

| Trigger | Scenario |
|---------|----------|
| Review process finishes | No live sessions → background auto-apply |
| Prompt-submit hook | REVIEW.md exists → inject as `additionalContext` (visible) |
| Stop hook (turn end) | REVIEW.md + no live sessions → background auto-apply |
| **Session-end hook** (new) | Last session closes + REVIEW.md pending → background auto-apply |

```
 // on every turn end (both with-changes and no-changes paths).
+//
+// When a live session exists, this is a no-op: the prompt-submit injection
+// will deliver the review visibly in the user's terminal instead. Background
+// auto-apply is only used when no sessions are alive (all ended).
 func triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending(repoRoot string) {
 	if !settings.IsWingmanEnabled() || os.Getenv("ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY") != "" {
 		return
@@ -836,8 +846,47 @@ func triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending(repoRoot string) {
 		logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply already attempted, skipping")
 		return
 	}
-	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Pending review found, spawning auto-apply\n")
-	logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply spawning",
+	// Don't spawn background auto-apply if a live session exists.
+	// The prompt-submit hook will inject REVIEW.md as additionalContext,
+	// which is visible to the user in their terminal.
+	if hasAnyLiveSession(repoRoot) {
+		logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply deferred: live session will handle via injection")
+		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review pending — will be injected on next prompt\n")
+		return
+	}
+	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Pending review found, spawning auto-apply (no live sessions)\n")
+	logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply spawning (no live sessions)",
+		slog.String("review_path", reviewPath),
+	)
+	spawnDetachedWingmanApply(repoRoot)
+}
+
+// triggerWingmanAutoApplyOnSessionEnd checks for a pending REVIEW.md after a
+// session ends. If no live sessions remain, spawns background auto-apply.
+// Called from both Claude Code and Gemini CLI SessionEnd hooks.
+func triggerWingmanAutoApplyOnSessionEnd() {
+	if !settings.IsWingmanEnabled() || os.Getenv("ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY") != "" {
+		return
+	}
+	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
+	if err != nil {
+		return
+	}
+	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "wingman")
+	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
+	if _, statErr := os.Stat(reviewPath); statErr != nil {
+		return
+	}
+	wingmanState := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)
+	if wingmanState != nil && wingmanState.ApplyAttemptedAt != nil {
+		return
+	}
+	if hasAnyLiveSession(repoRoot) {
+		logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply deferred on session-end: other live sessions remain")
+		return
+	}
+	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Last session ended with pending review, spawning auto-apply\n")
+	logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply on session-end (no live sessions remain)",
 		slog.String("review_path", reviewPath),
 	)
 	spawnDetachedWingmanApply(repoRoot)

+// hasAnyLiveSession checks if any session is in a non-ENDED phase (IDLE,
+// ACTIVE, or ACTIVE_COMMITTED). Used to decide whether to defer review
+// application to the prompt-submit injection (visible to user) vs background
+// auto-apply (invisible).
+func hasAnyLiveSession(repoRoot string) bool {
+	sessDir := findSessionStateDir(repoRoot)
+	if sessDir == "" {
+		return false
+	}
+
+	entries, err := os.ReadDir(sessDir)
+	if err != nil {
+		return false
+	}
+
+	const maxCheck = 50
+	checked := 0
+	for _, entry := range entries {
+		if checked >= maxCheck {
+			return false
+		}
+		name := entry.Name()
+		if !strings.HasSuffix(name, ".json") {
+			continue
+		}
+		checked++
+		sid := strings.TrimSuffix(name, ".json")
+		phase := readSessionPhase(sessDir, sid)
+		if phase != "" && phase != string(session.PhaseEnded) {
+			wingmanLog("found live session %s (phase=%s)", sid, phase)
+			return true
+		}
+	}
+
+	return false
+}
+
 // findSessionStateDir locates the .git/entire-sessions/ directory by
 // reading .git to handle both normal repos and worktrees.
 func findSessionStateDir(repoRoot string) string {

+func TestHasAnyLiveSession_NoSessionDir(t *testing.T) {
+	t.Parallel()
+
+	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
+	// No .git at all
+	if hasAnyLiveSession(tmpDir) {
+		t.Error("should return false with no .git directory")
+	}
+}
+
+func TestHasAnyLiveSession_EmptySessionDir(t *testing.T) {
+	t.Parallel()
+
+	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
+	gitDir := filepath.Join(tmpDir, ".git")
+	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(gitDir, "entire-sessions"), 0o755); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	if hasAnyLiveSession(tmpDir) {
+		t.Error("should return false with empty session dir")
+	}
+}
+
+func TestHasAnyLiveSession_IdleSession(t *testing.T) {
+	t.Parallel()
+
+	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
+	sessDir := filepath.Join(tmpDir, ".git", "entire-sessions")
+	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessDir, 0o755); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	// Create an IDLE session
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(sessDir, "sess-idle.json"), []byte(`{"phase":"idle"}`), 0o644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	if !hasAnyLiveSession(tmpDir) {
+		t.Error("should return true when IDLE session exists")
+	}
+}
+
+func TestHasAnyLiveSession_ActiveSession(t *testing.T) {
+	t.Parallel()
+
+	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
+	sessDir := filepath.Join(tmpDir, ".git", "entire-sessions")
+	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessDir, 0o755); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(sessDir, "sess-active.json"), []byte(`{"phase":"active"}`), 0o644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	if !hasAnyLiveSession(tmpDir) {
+		t.Error("should return true when ACTIVE session exists")
+	}
+}
+
+func TestHasAnyLiveSession_AllEnded(t *testing.T) {
+	t.Parallel()
+
+	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
+	sessDir := filepath.Join(tmpDir, ".git", "entire-sessions")

+	}

+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	if hasAnyLiveSession(tmpDir) {
+		t.Error("should return false when all sessions are ended")
+	}
+}
+
+func TestHasAnyLiveSession_MixedPhases(t *testing.T) {
+	t.Parallel()
+
+	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
```

```
+package cli
+
+import (
+	"context"
+	"crypto/sha256"
+	_ "embed"
+	"encoding/hex"
+	"encoding/json"
+	"errors"
+	"fmt"

+	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy"
+	"github.com/spf13/cobra"
+)
+
+// WingmanPayload is the data passed to the detached review subprocess.
+type WingmanPayload struct {
+	SessionID     string   `json:"session_id"`
+	RepoRoot      string   `json:"repo_root"`
+	BaseCommit    string   `json:"base_commit"`
+	ModifiedFiles []string `json:"modified_files"`
+	NewFiles      []string `json:"new_files"`
+	DeletedFiles  []string `json:"deleted_files"`
+	Prompts       []string `json:"prompts"`
+	CommitMessage string   `json:"commit_message"`
+}
+
+// WingmanState tracks deduplication and review state.
+type WingmanState struct {
+	SessionID        string     `json:"session_id"`
+	FilesHash        string     `json:"files_hash"`
+	ReviewedAt       time.Time  `json:"reviewed_at"`
+	ReviewApplied    bool       `json:"review_applied"`
+	ApplyAttemptedAt *time.Time `json:"apply_attempted_at,omitempty"`
```

```
+// outputHookResponseWithContext outputs a JSON response that adds context to
+// the agent's conversation. additionalContext is injected into Claude's context
+// and treated as required instructions, not just a warning.
+func outputHookResponseWithContext(context string) error {
+	resp := hookResponse{
+		AdditionalContext: context,
+	}
+	if err := json.NewEncoder(os.Stdout).Encode(resp); err != nil {
+		return fmt.Errorf("failed to encode hook response: %w", err)
+	}
+	return nil
+}

+// triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending checks for a pending REVIEW.md and spawns
+// the auto-apply subprocess if conditions are met. Called from the stop hook
+// on every turn end (both with-changes and no-changes paths).
+//
+// When a live session exists, this is a no-op: the prompt-submit injection
+// will deliver the review visibly in the user's terminal instead. Background
+// auto-apply is only used when no sessions are alive (all ended).
+func triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending(repoRoot string) {
+	if !settings.IsWingmanEnabled() || os.Getenv("ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY") != "" {
+		return
+	}
+	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "wingman")
+	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
+	if _, statErr := os.Stat(reviewPath); statErr != nil {
+		return
+	}
+	wingmanState := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)
+	if wingmanState != nil && wingmanState.ApplyAttemptedAt != nil {
+		logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply already attempted, skipping")
+		return
+	}
+	// Don't spawn background auto-apply if a live session exists.
+	// The prompt-submit hook will inject REVIEW.md as additionalContext,
+	// which is visible to the user in their terminal.
+	if hasAnyLiveSession(repoRoot) {
+		logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply deferred: live session will handle via injection")
+		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review pending — will be injected on next prompt\n")
+		return
+	}
+	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Pending review found, spawning auto-apply (no live sessions)\n")
+	logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply spawning (no live sessions)",
+		slog.String("review_path", reviewPath),
+	)
+	spawnDetachedWingmanApply(repoRoot)
+}
+
+// triggerWingmanAutoApplyOnSessionEnd checks for a pending REVIEW.md after a
+// session ends. If no live sessions remain, spawns background auto-apply.
+// Called from both Claude Code and Gemini CLI SessionEnd hooks.
+func triggerWingmanAutoApplyOnSessionEnd() {
+	if !settings.IsWingmanEnabled() || os.Getenv("ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY") != "" {
+		return
+	}
+	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
+	if err != nil {
+		return
+	}
+	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "wingman")
+	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
+	if _, statErr := os.Stat(reviewPath); statErr != nil {
+		return
+	}
+	wingmanState := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)
+	if wingmanState != nil && wingmanState.ApplyAttemptedAt != nil {
+		return
+	}
+	if hasAnyLiveSession(repoRoot) {
+		logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply deferred on session-end: other live sessions remain")
+		return
+	}
+	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Last session ended with pending review, spawning auto-apply\n")
+	logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply on session-end (no live sessions remain)",
+		slog.String("review_path", reviewPath),
+	)
+	spawnDetachedWingmanApply(repoRoot)
+}
+
 // markSessionEnded transitions the session to ENDED phase via the state machine.
 func markSessionEnded(sessionID string) error {
 	state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
```

```json
{
  "command": "gh pr create --draft --title \"Add wingman automated code review system\" --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## Summary\n\nAdds **wingman**, an automated code review system that reviews agent-produced code changes after each commit and delivers actionable suggestions back to the agent. The system prioritizes **visible delivery** — the user sees the agent reading and applying the review in their terminal.\n\n## How It Works\n\n### Review Generation\n\nAfter each commit (or auto-commit checkpoint), wingman spawns a **detached background process** that:\n\n1. Waits 10 seconds for the agent turn to settle\n2. Computes a branch-level diff against `main`/`master` merge-base (holistic view, not just one commit)\n3. Loads session context (prompts, commit message, checkpoint metadata)\n4. Calls Claude (sonnet) with read-only tool access (`Read`, `Glob`, `Grep`) for an intent-aware review\n5. Writes suggestions to `.entire/REVIEW.md`\n\n### Review Delivery (Two Paths)\n\n**Primary — Visible injection (when a live session exists):**\n```\nCommit → wingman review → REVIEW.md written → live session detected → defer\n\nUser sends next prompt → UserPromptSubmit hook\n  → REVIEW.md exists → inject as additionalContext\n  → Agent reads REVIEW.md, applies suggestions, deletes file\n  → User sees everything in their terminal\n  → Agent then proceeds with user's actual request\n```\n\nThe `additionalContext` hook response field adds the instruction directly into Claude's context as a mandatory pre-step (stronger than `systemMessage` which the agent can deprioritize).\n\n**Fallback — Background auto-apply (when no sessions are alive):**\n```\nReview finishes → no live sessions → claude --continue --print\n  → Applies review silently in background\n```\n\nThis only fires when all sessions are ENDED (user closed everything). Three trigger points cover all scenarios:\n- Review process itself (review finishes, no live sessions)\n- SessionEnd hook (last session closes with pending review)\n- Stop hook (turn ends with no live sessions — edge case)\n\n### Decision Flow\n\n```\n              REVIEW.md written\n                    │\n                    ▼\n          ┌─────────────────┐\n          │ Any live session │\n          │   exists?        │\n          └────────┬────────┘\n              │          │\n             Yes         No\n              │          │\n              ▼          ▼\n        Defer to     Background\n        next prompt  auto-apply\n        (visible)    (invisible)\n```\n\n## Key Behaviors\n\n### Stale Review Cleanup\n- REVIEW.md from a different session → deleted (stale)\n- REVIEW.md without state file → deleted (orphan)\n- REVIEW.md older than 1 hour → deleted (TTL expired)\n- REVIEW.md from current session + fresh → kept (skip new review)\n\n### Concurrency & Safety\n- **Atomic lock file** (`O_CREATE|O_EXCL`) prevents concurrent review spawns; stale locks (>30m) auto-cleaned\n- **File hash dedup** prevents re-reviewing identical change sets within the same session\n- **`ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY=1`** env var prevents post-commit hook from triggering another review during auto-apply (recursion prevention)\n- **`--setting-sources \"\"`** disables hooks on review/apply subprocesses\n- **GIT_* env stripping** prevents git index corruption in subprocesses\n- **`ApplyAttemptedAt`** field prevents infinite auto-apply retry loops\n\n### Session Detection\n- `hasAnyLiveSession()` scans `.git/entire-sessions/` for any non-ENDED session\n- `isSessionIdle()` reads session state files directly using `repoRoot` (not `git rev-parse`, which fails in detached processes running from `cwd=/`)\n- Handles both normal repos and git worktrees (parses `.git` file for `gitdir:` pointer)\n\n### Strategy Integration\n- **Manual-commit**: Review triggered from git `post-commit` hook via `triggerWingmanFromCommit()`\n- **Auto-commit**: Review triggered from stop hook after `SaveChanges()` creates the commit\n\n## Files\n\n| File | Purpose |\n|------|---------|\n| `wingman.go` | State management, trigger logic, dedup, lock files, stale cleanup, CLI commands |\n| `wingman_review.go` | Detached review subprocess, diff computation, Claude API call, session detection, auto-apply |\n| `wingman_prompt.go` | Review prompt construction from diff + context |\n| `wingman_instruction.md` | Embedded instruction injected into agent context |\n| `wingman_spawn_unix.go` | Detached process spawning (Unix) |\n| `wingman_spawn_other.go` | No-op stubs (non-Unix) |\n| `wingman_test.go` | 25 tests covering state, dedup, stale cleanup, session detection |\n| `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` | Prompt-submit injection, stop hook trigger, session-end trigger |\n| `hooks_geminicli_handlers.go` | Session-end trigger for Gemini CLI |\n| `hooks_git_cmd.go` | Post-commit hook trigger for manual-commit strategy |\n| `hooks.go` | `additionalContext` hook response field, `outputHookResponseWithContext()` |\n| `settings/settings.go` | `IsWingmanEnabled()` setting |\n| `docs/architecture/wingman.md` | Comprehensive architecture documentation |\n\n## Configuration\n\n```bash\nentire wingman enable   # Enable\nentire wingman disable  # Disable + cleanup\nentire wingman status   # Show status\n```\n\n## Test plan\n\n- [ ] `mise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci` passes\n- [ ] Enable wingman in a test repo, make agent produce changes, verify REVIEW.md is generated\n- [ ] Verify prompt-submit injection fires on next user prompt (visible in terminal)\n- [ ] Verify agent addresses REVIEW.md before user's request\n- [ ] Verify background auto-apply only fires when no live sessions exist\n- [ ] Start new session with stale REVIEW.md from old session — verify cleanup\n- [ ] Verify `ApplyAttemptedAt` prevents infinite retry loops\n- [ ] Verify concurrent review spawns are blocked by lock file\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\nEOF\n)\"",
  "description": "Create draft PR with comprehensive description"
}
```

     1→package cli
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"fmt"
     5→	"strings"
     6→)
     7→
     8→// maxDiffSize is the maximum size of the diff included in the review prompt.
     9→// Large diffs degrade review quality, so we truncate.
    10→const maxDiffSize = 100 * 1024 // 100KB
    11→
    12→// reviewPromptTemplate is the prompt sent to Claude for code review.
    13→//
    14→// Security note: User content (diff, prompts, context) is wrapped in XML tags
    15→// to provide clear boundary markers, similar to the summarization prompt pattern.
    16→const reviewPromptTemplate = `You are a senior code reviewer performing an intent-aware review. Your job is not just to find bugs in the code — it is to evaluate whether the changes correctly and completely fulfill what the developer was trying to accomplish.
    17→
    18→## Session Context
    19→
    20→This review is part of an automated development workflow. The developer works with an AI coding agent that makes changes on their behalf. Below is the checkpoint data captured during the session, which tells you WHY these changes were made.
    21→
    22→### Developer's Prompts
    23→The original instructions the developer gave to the agent:
    24→<prompts>
    25→%s
    26→</prompts>
    27→
    28→### Commit Message
    29→%s
    30→
    31→### Session Context
    32→Checkpoint data including the session summary and key actions taken:
    33→<session-context>
    34→%s
    35→</session-context>
    36→
    37→### Checkpoint Files
    38→You have read-only access to the repository. If you need deeper context about the session,
    39→you can read these checkpoint files (they may or may not exist):
    40→- ` + "`%s`" + ` — session transcript (JSONL format)
    41→- ` + "`%s`" + ` — user prompts collected during session
    42→- ` + "`%s`" + ` — generated session context/summary
    43→
    44→## Code Changes
    45→
    46→Files changed: %s
    47→
    48→<diff>
    49→%s
    50→</diff>
    51→
    52→## Review Instructions
    53→
    54→Use the session context above to understand the developer's intent. Then review the code changes with that intent in mind.
    55→
    56→**Intent alignment** (most important):
    57→- Do the changes actually accomplish what the developer asked for?
    58→- Are there any prompts or requirements that were missed or only partially implemented?
    59→- Does the implementation match the stated approach in the session context?
    60→
    61→**Correctness**:
    62→- Bugs, logic errors, or off-by-one mistakes
    63→- Race conditions or concurrency issues
    64→- Missing error handling for failure paths that matter
    65→
    66→**Security**:
    67→- Injection vulnerabilities (SQL, command, XSS)
    68→- Hardcoded secrets or credentials
    69→- Unsafe file operations or path traversal
    70→
    71→**Robustness**:
    72→- Edge cases not handled (empty inputs, nil pointers, large data)
    73→- Resource leaks (unclosed files, connections, goroutines)
    74→- Missing timeouts on external calls
    75→
    76→Do NOT flag:
    77→- Style preferences or formatting (the linter handles that)
    78→- Missing comments or documentation on clear code
    79→- Theoretical issues that cannot happen given the actual call sites
    80→
    81→For each issue found, provide:
    82→1. Severity: CRITICAL, WARNING, or SUGGESTION
    83→2. File path and approximate line reference (from the diff)
    84→3. Description of the issue
    85→4. Suggested fix (code snippet when helpful)
    86→
    87→Format your response as Markdown:
    88→
    89→# Code Review
    90→
    91→## Summary
    92→Brief assessment: does this change accomplish its stated goal? What's the overall quality?
    93→
    94→## Issues
    95→
    96→### [SEVERITY] Short description
    97→**File:** ` + "`path/to/file.go:42`" + `
    98→**Description:** What the issue is and why it matters.
    99→**Suggestion:**
   100→` + "```" + `
   101→// suggested fix
   102→` + "```" + `
   103→
   104→If no issues are found, confirm the changes look correct and match the intent.
   105→Do NOT include any preamble or explanation outside the Markdown structure above.`
   106→
   107→// buildReviewPrompt constructs the review prompt from the payload, context, and diff.
   108→func buildReviewPrompt(prompts []string, commitMessage, sessionContext, sessionID, fileList, diff string) string {
   109→	promptText := strings.Join(prompts, "\n\n---\n\n")
   110→	if promptText == "" {
   111→		promptText = "(no prompts captured)"
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	commitMsgText := commitMessage
   115→	if commitMsgText == "" {
   116→		commitMsgText = "(no commit message)"
   117→	}
   118→
   119→	contextText := sessionContext
   120→	if contextText == "" {
   121→		contextText = "(no session context available)"
   122→	}
   123→
   124→	// Build checkpoint file paths
   125→	metadataDir := ".entire/metadata/" + sessionID
   126→	if sessionID == "" {
   127→		metadataDir = ".entire/metadata/<unknown>"
   128→	}
   129→	transcriptPath := metadataDir + "/full.jsonl"
   130→	promptPath := metadataDir + "/prompt.txt"
   131→	contextPath := metadataDir + "/context.md"
   132→
   133→	// Truncate large diffs
   134→	if len(diff) > maxDiffSize {
   135→		diff = diff[:maxDiffSize] + "\n\n... (diff truncated at 100KB)"
   136→	}
   137→
   138→	return fmt.Sprintf(reviewPromptTemplate, promptText, commitMsgText, contextText,
   139→		transcriptPath, promptPath, contextPath, fileList, diff)
   140→}
   141→
```

     1→# Wingman: Automated Code Review
     2→
     3→Wingman is an automated code review system that reviews agent-produced code changes after each commit and delivers actionable suggestions back to the agent.
     4→
     5→## Overview
     6→
     7→When enabled, wingman runs a background review after each commit (or checkpoint), writes suggestions to `.entire/REVIEW.md`, and ensures the agent addresses them. The system prioritizes **visible delivery** — the user should see the agent reading and applying the review in their terminal.
     8→
     9→## Architecture
    10→
    11→### Components
    12→
    13→| Component | File | Purpose |
    14→|-----------|------|---------|
    15→| Trigger & state | `wingman.go` | Payload, state management, dedup, lock files, stale review cleanup |
    16→| Review process | `wingman_review.go` | Detached subprocess: diff, Claude review call, REVIEW.md creation, session detection |
    17→| Review prompt | `wingman_prompt.go` | Builds the review prompt from diff, prompts, context |
    18→| Instruction | `wingman_instruction.md` | Embedded instruction injected into agent context |
    19→| Process spawning | `wingman_spawn_unix.go` | Detached subprocess spawning (Unix) |
    20→| Process spawning | `wingman_spawn_other.go` | No-op stubs (non-Unix) |
    21→| Hook integration | `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` | Prompt-submit injection, stop hook trigger, session-end trigger |
    22→
    23→### State Files
    24→
    25→| File | Purpose |
    26→|------|---------|
    27→| `.entire/REVIEW.md` | The review itself, read by the agent |
    28→| `.entire/wingman-state.json` | Dedup state, session ID, apply tracking |
    29→| `.entire/wingman.lock` | Prevents concurrent review spawns |
    30→| `.entire/wingman-payload.json` | Payload passed to detached review process |
    31→| `.entire/logs/wingman.log` | Review process logs (stderr redirect) |
    32→
    33→## Lifecycle
    34→
    35→### Phase 1: Trigger
    36→
    37→A wingman review is triggered after code changes are committed:
    38→
    39→- **Manual-commit strategy**: Git `post-commit` hook calls `triggerWingmanFromCommit()`
    40→- **Auto-commit strategy**: Stop hook calls `triggerWingmanReview()` after `SaveChanges()`
    41→
    42→Before spawning, preconditions are checked:
    43→1. `ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY` env var not set (prevents recursion from auto-apply)
    44→2. No pending REVIEW.md for the current session (`shouldSkipPendingReview()`)
    45→3. Lock file acquired atomically (`acquireWingmanLock()`)
    46→4. File hash dedup — skip if same files were already reviewed this session
    47→
    48→### Phase 2: Review (Detached Process)
    49→
    50→The review runs in a fully detached subprocess (`entire wingman __review <payload-path>`):
    51→
    52→```
    53→┌─ Detached Process ─────────────────────────────────────┐
    54→│ 1. Read payload from file                              │
    55→│ 2. Wait 10s for agent turn to settle                   │
    56→│ 3. Compute diff (merge-base with main/master)          │
    57→│ 4. Load session context from checkpoint metadata       │
    58→│ 5. Build review prompt (diff + prompts + context)      │
    59→│ 6. Call claude --print (sonnet model, read-only tools)  │
    60→│ 7. Write REVIEW.md                                     │
    61→│ 8. Save dedup state                                    │
    62→│ 9. Determine delivery path (see Phase 3)               │
    63→│ 10. Remove lock file                                   │
    64→└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
    65→```
    66→
    67→The review process uses `--setting-sources ""` to disable hooks (prevents recursion) and strips `GIT_*` environment variables for isolation.
    68→
    69→### Phase 3: Delivery
    70→
    71→There are two delivery mechanisms. The system chooses based on whether any session is still alive.
    72→
    73→#### Primary: Prompt-Submit Injection (Visible)
    74→
    75→When a live session exists (IDLE, ACTIVE, or ACTIVE_COMMITTED phase), the review is delivered through the agent's next prompt:
    76→
    77→```
    78→Review finishes → REVIEW.md written → live session detected → defer to injection
    79→
    80→User sends prompt → UserPromptSubmit hook fires
    81→                  → REVIEW.md exists on disk
    82→                  → Inject as additionalContext (mandatory agent instruction)
    83→                  → Agent reads REVIEW.md, applies suggestions, deletes file
    84→                  → Agent then proceeds with user's actual request
    85→```
    86→
    87→The `additionalContext` hook response field adds the instruction directly to Claude's context, making it a mandatory pre-step rather than an ignorable warning. The embedded instruction (`wingman_instruction.md`) tells the agent to:
    88→
    89→1. Read `.entire/REVIEW.md`
    90→2. Address each suggestion (skip any it disagrees with)
    91→3. Delete `.entire/REVIEW.md` when done
    92→4. Briefly tell the user what changed
    93→
    94→This path is **visible** — the user sees the agent working through the review in their terminal.
    95→
    96→#### Fallback: Background Auto-Apply (Invisible)
    97→
    98→When no live sessions exist (all ENDED or none), REVIEW.md is applied via a background process:
    99→
   100→```
   101→Review finishes → REVIEW.md written → no live sessions → background auto-apply
   102→
   103→entire wingman __apply <repoRoot>
   104→  → Verify REVIEW.md exists
   105→  → Check ApplyAttemptedAt not set (retry prevention)
   106→  → Re-check session idle (guard against race)
   107→  → claude --continue --print --permission-mode acceptEdits
   108→```
   109→
   110→This path is **invisible** — it runs silently. It exists as a fallback for when no session will receive the injection (e.g., user closed all sessions during the review window).
   111→
   112→### Decision Flow
   113→
   114→```
   115→                    REVIEW.md written
   116→                          │
   117→                          ▼
   118→                ┌─────────────────┐
   119→                │ Any live session │
   120→                │   exists?        │
   121→                └────────┬────────┘
   122→                    │          │
   123→                   Yes         No
   124→                    │          │
   125→                    ▼          ▼
   126→            ┌──────────┐ ┌──────────────┐
   127→            │  Defer   │ │  Background  │
   128→            │  to next │ │  auto-apply  │
   129→            │  prompt  │ │  immediately │
   130→            └──────────┘ └──────────────┘
   131→                    │
   132→                    ▼
   133→            User sends prompt
   134→                    │
   135→                    ▼
   136→            additionalContext
   137→            injection fires
   138→                    │
   139→                    ▼
   140→            Agent applies review
   141→            (visible in terminal)
   142→```
   143→
   144→### Trigger Points Summary
   145→
   146→| Trigger | When | What Happens |
   147→|---------|------|-------------|
   148→| **Review process** (`runWingmanReview`) | Review finishes | If no live sessions → background auto-apply. Otherwise defer. |
   149→| **Prompt-submit hook** (`captureInitialState`) | User sends prompt | If REVIEW.md exists → inject as `additionalContext`. |
   150→| **Stop hook** (`triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending`) | Agent turn ends | If REVIEW.md exists + no live sessions → spawn `__apply`. |
   151→| **Session-end hook** (`triggerWingmanAutoApplyOnSessionEnd`) | User closes session | If REVIEW.md exists + no remaining live sessions → spawn `__apply`. |
   152→
   153→## Timing
   154→
   155→Typical timeline for a review cycle:
   156→
   157→```
   158→T+0s    Commit happens → wingman review triggered
   159→T+0s    Lock file created, payload written
   160→T+10s   Initial settle delay completes
   161→T+10s   Diff computed (~30-50ms)
   162→T+11s   Claude review API call starts
   163→T+30-50s Review received, REVIEW.md written
   164→T+30-50s Delivery path determined
   165→```
   166→
   167→The 10-second initial delay lets the agent turn fully settle before computing the diff, ensuring all file writes are flushed.
   168→
   169→## Stale Review Cleanup
   170→
   171→Reviews can become stale in several scenarios. The `shouldSkipPendingReview()` function handles cleanup:
   172→
   173→| Scenario | Detection | Action |
   174→|----------|-----------|--------|
   175→| REVIEW.md without state file | `state == nil` | Delete REVIEW.md (orphan) |
   176→| REVIEW.md from different session | `state.SessionID != currentSessionID` | Delete REVIEW.md (stale) |
   177→| REVIEW.md older than 1 hour | `time.Since(state.ReviewedAt) > 1h` | Delete REVIEW.md (TTL expired) |
   178→| REVIEW.md from current session | Session matches + fresh | Keep (skip new review) |
   179→
   180→## Retry Prevention
   181→
   182→The `ApplyAttemptedAt` field in `WingmanState` prevents infinite auto-apply attempts:
   183→
   184→- Set to current time before triggering auto-apply
   185→- Reset to `nil` when a new review is written
   186→- Checked before every auto-apply attempt — if set, skip
   187→
   188→## Concurrency Safety
   189→
   190→- **Lock file**: Atomic `O_CREATE|O_EXCL` prevents concurrent review spawns. Stale locks (>30 min) are auto-cleaned.
   191→- **Dedup hash**: SHA256 of sorted file paths prevents re-reviewing identical change sets.
   192→- **Detached processes**: Review and apply run in their own process groups (`Setpgid: true`), surviving parent exit.
   193→- **GIT_* stripping**: Subprocesses strip all `GIT_*` env vars to prevent index corruption.
   194→- **ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY=1**: Set during auto-apply to prevent the post-commit hook from triggering another review (recursion prevention).
   195→
   196→## Configuration
   197→
   198→```bash
   199→entire wingman enable   # Enable wingman auto-review
   200→entire wingman disable  # Disable and clean up pending reviews
   201→entire wingman status   # Show current status
   202→```
   203→
   204→Wingman state is stored in `.entire/settings.json`:
   205→
   206→```json
   207→{
   208→  "strategy_options": {
   209→    "wingman": {
   210→      "enabled": true
   211→    }
   212→  }
   213→}
   214→```
   215→
   216→## Key Constants
   217→
   218→| Constant | Value | Purpose |
   219→|----------|-------|---------|
   220→| `wingmanInitialDelay` | 10s | Settle time before computing diff |
   221→| `wingmanReviewModel` | `sonnet` | Model used for reviews |
   222→| `wingmanGitTimeout` | 60s | Timeout for git diff operations |
   223→| `wingmanReviewTimeout` | 5m | Timeout for Claude review API call |
   224→| `wingmanApplyTimeout` | 15m | Timeout for auto-apply process |
   225→| `wingmanStaleReviewTTL` | 1h | Max age before review is cleaned up |
   226→| `staleLockThreshold` | 30m | Max age before lock is considered stale |
   227→
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/docs/architecture/wingman.md",
  "old_string": "## Stale Review Cleanup",
  "new_string": "## Review Prompt Construction\n\nThe review prompt is the core of wingman's value. Unlike a naive diff-only review, it leverages Entire's checkpoint data to give the reviewer **full context about what the developer was trying to accomplish**. This enables intent-aware review — catching not just bugs, but misalignment between what was asked and what was built.\n\n### Context Sources\n\nThe prompt is assembled from six sources, each contributing a different layer of understanding:\n\n| Source | Origin | What It Provides |\n|--------|--------|-----------------|\n| **Developer prompts** | `prompt.txt` from checkpoint metadata | The original instructions given to the agent — the ground truth of intent |\n| **Commit message** | Git commit or auto-commit message | A summary of what was done (may differ from what was asked) |\n| **Session context** | `context.md` from checkpoint metadata | Generated summary of key actions, decisions, and session flow |\n| **Checkpoint files** | `.entire/metadata/<session-id>/` | Paths provided so the reviewer can read the full transcript, prompts, or context if needed |\n| **File list** | Payload from trigger | Which files changed and how (modified/new/deleted) |\n| **Branch diff** | `git diff` against merge-base | The actual code changes — computed against `main`/`master` for a holistic branch-level view |\n\n### Prompt Structure\n\n```\n┌─ System Role ──────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│ \"You are a senior code reviewer performing an           │\n│  intent-aware review.\"                                  │\n├─ Session Context ──────────────────────────────────────┤\n│ Developer's Prompts     <prompts>...</prompts>          │\n│ Commit Message          (plain text)                    │\n│ Session Context         <session-context>...</session-context> │\n│ Checkpoint File Paths   (for deeper investigation)      │\n├─ Code Changes ─────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│ Files changed:          file.go (modified), ...         │\n│ Diff                    <diff>...</diff>                │\n├─ Review Instructions ──────────────────────────────────┤\n│ Intent alignment        (most important)                │\n│ Correctness             bugs, logic errors, races       │\n│ Security                injection, secrets, path traversal │\n│ Robustness              edge cases, leaks, timeouts     │\n│ Do NOT flag             style, docs on clear code       │\n│ Output format           Markdown with severity levels   │\n└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### Intent-Aware Review\n\nThe review instructions prioritize **intent alignment** as the most important category:\n\n1. Do the changes actually accomplish what the developer asked for?\n2. Are there any prompts or requirements that were missed or only partially implemented?\n3. Does the implementation match the stated approach in the session context?\n\nThis is only possible because Entire captures the developer's prompts and session context as checkpoint metadata. A reviewer that only sees the diff cannot evaluate whether the code matches the original request.\n\n### Diff Strategy\n\nThe diff is computed against the **merge-base** with `main`/`master`, not just the latest commit. This gives the reviewer a holistic view of all branch changes rather than a narrow single-commit diff. Fallback chain:\n\n1. `git merge-base main HEAD` → diff against merge-base (branch-level view)\n2. `git merge-base master HEAD` → try master if main doesn't exist\n3. `git diff HEAD` → uncommitted changes only\n4. `git diff HEAD~1 HEAD` → latest commit if no uncommitted changes\n\n### Read-Only Tool Access\n\nThe reviewer Claude instance has access to `Read`, `Glob`, and `Grep` tools with `--permission-mode bypassPermissions`. This allows it to:\n\n- Read source files beyond the diff for additional context\n- Search for related code patterns or usages\n- Inspect the checkpoint metadata files referenced in the prompt\n\nTools are restricted to read-only operations — the reviewer cannot modify files.\n\n### Truncation\n\nDiffs larger than 100KB are truncated to maintain review quality. Very large diffs tend to produce unfocused reviews.\n\n### Output Format\n\nThe reviewer outputs structured Markdown with:\n- **Summary**: Does the change accomplish its goal? Overall quality assessment.\n- **Issues**: Each with severity (`CRITICAL`, `WARNING`, `SUGGESTION`), file path with line reference, description, and suggested fix.\n\nThis output is written directly to `.entire/REVIEW.md` and later read by the agent during delivery.\n\n## Stale Review Cleanup"
}
```
